     2→Baseline Test Suite for LoRA Format Resolution
     3→
     4→Tests all 8 LoRA input formats and their code paths through the current system.
     5→This establishes a baseline before refactoring, so we can validate that the
     6→new unified resolver produces identical results.
     7→
     8→Run with: python tests/test_lora_formats_baseline.py
     9→"""

    26→class TestResult:
    27→    """Track test results for reporting."""
    28→    def __init__(self):
    29→        self.passed = 0
    30→        self.failed = 0
    31→        self.errors = []
    32→    
    33→    def record_pass(self, test_name: str):
    34→        self.passed += 1
    35→        print(f"  ✅ {test_name}")
    36→    
    37→    def record_fail(self, test_name: str, reason: str):
    38→        self.failed += 1
    39→        self.errors.append((test_name, reason))
    40→        print(f"  ❌ {test_name}: {reason}")
    41→    
    42→    def summary(self):
    43→        total = self.passed + self.failed
    44→        print(f"\n{'='*70}")
    45→        print(f"Test Results: {self.passed}/{total} passed")
    46→        if self.errors:
    47→            print(f"\nFailures:")
    48→            for test_name, reason in self.errors:
    49→                print(f"  - {test_name}: {reason}")
    50→        print(f"{'='*70}\n")
    51→        return self.failed == 0
    52→
    53→
    54→def dprint(msg: str):
    55→    """Debug print with prefix."""
    56→    print(f"    [DEBUG] {msg}")
    57→
    58→

    60→# Format Processing Functions (Current System)

    63→def process_format_1_wgp_legacy(params: dict) -> dict:
    64→    """
    65→    Test Format 1: WGP Legacy (activated_loras list + loras_multipliers string)
    66→    
    67→    Current code path: worker.py lines 1022-1042
    68→    """
    69→    print("  📋 Processing Format 1: WGP Legacy")
    70→    result = params.copy()
    71→    
    72→    # Simulate worker.py conversion
    73→    if "activated_loras" in result:
    74→        loras = result["activated_loras"]
    75→        if isinstance(loras, str):
    76→            result["lora_names"] = [lora.strip() for lora in loras.split(",") if lora.strip()]
    77→        elif isinstance(loras, list):
    78→            result["lora_names"] = loras
    79→        del result["activated_loras"]
    80→    
    81→    if "loras_multipliers" in result:
    82→        multipliers = result["loras_multipliers"]
    83→        if isinstance(multipliers, str):
    84→            if ";" in multipliers:
    85→                # Phase-config format (space or comma separated)
    86→                # Check which separator is primary (whichever appears without the other)
    87→                if "," in multipliers and " " not in multipliers.replace(",", ""):
    88→                    sep = ","
    89→                else:
    90→                    sep = " "
    91→                result["lora_multipliers"] = [x.strip() for x in multipliers.split(sep) if x.strip()]
    92→            else:
    93→                # Regular format (space or comma separated)
    94→                # Try comma first, fall back to space
    95→                if "," in multipliers:
    96→                    result["lora_multipliers"] = [float(x.strip()) for x in multipliers.split(",") if x.strip()]
    97→                else:
    98→                    result["lora_multipliers"] = [float(x.strip()) for x in multipliers.split() if x.strip()]
    99→    
   100→    dprint(f"Result: lora_names={result.get('lora_names', [])}, lora_multipliers={result.get('lora_multipliers', [])}")
   101→    return result

   209→        for lora_dict in qwen_loras:

   211→                lora_path = lora_dict.get("path") or lora_dict.get("url")
   212→                lora_scale = lora_dict.get("scale") or lora_dict.get("strength", 1.0)
   213→                
   214→                if lora_path:
   215→                    lora_filename = Path(lora_path).name
   216→                    if lora_filename not in lora_names:
   217→                        lora_names.append(lora_filename)
   218→                        lora_mults.append(float(lora_scale))
   219→        
   220→        result["lora_names"] = lora_names
   221→        result["lora_multipliers"] = lora_mults
   222→    
   223→    dprint(f"Result: lora_names={result.get('lora_names', [])}, lora_multipliers={result.get('lora_multipliers', [])}")
   224→    return result
   225→
   226→
   227→def process_format_7_model_preset(params: dict, model_type: str) -> dict:
   228→    """
   229→    Test Format 7: Model Preset (from JSON model definition)
   230→    
   231→    Current code path: headless_wgp.py _resolve_parameters() lines 744-792
   232→    Note: This requires loading model JSON, so we'll simulate with fake data
   233→    """
   234→    print("  📋 Processing Format 7: Model Preset (simulated)")
   235→    result = params.copy()
   236→    
   237→    # Simulate model preset having default LoRAs
   238→    if "2_2" in model_type or "cocktail" in model_type:
   239→        # Wan 2.2 models have built-in acceleration LoRAs
   240→        if "lora_names" not in result:
   241→            result["lora_names"] = ["CausVid", "DetailEnhancerV1"]
   242→            result["lora_multipliers"] = [1.0, 0.2]
   243→            dprint("Applied default Wan 2.2 acceleration LoRAs")
   244→    
   245→    dprint(f"Result: lora_names={result.get('lora_names', [])}, lora_multipliers={result.get('lora_multipliers', [])}")
   246→    return result

   249→def process_format_8_feature_flags(params: dict, model_type: str) -> dict:
   250→    """
   251→    Test Format 8: Boolean Feature Flags
   252→    
   253→    Current code path: lora_utils.py detect_lora_optimization_flags()
   254→    """
   255→    print("  📋 Processing Format 8: Feature Flags")
   256→    result = params.copy()
   257→    
   258→    lora_names = result.get("lora_names", [])
   259→    lora_mults = result.get("lora_multipliers", [])
   260→    
   261→    # CausVid flag
   262→    if result.get("use_causvid_lora"):
   263→        causvid_lora = "CausVid" if "2_2" in model_type else [REDACTED]
   264→        if causvid_lora not in lora_names:
   265→            lora_names.append(causvid_lora)
   266→            lora_mults.append(1.0)
   267→            dprint(f"Added CausVid LoRA: {causvid_lora}")
   268→    
   269→    # LightI2X flag
   270→    if result.get("use_lighti2x_lora"):
   271→        lighti2x_lora = "high_noise_model.safetensors"
   272→        if lighti2x_lora not in lora_names:
   273→            lora_names.append(lighti2x_lora)
   274→            lora_mults.append(1.0)
   275→            dprint(f"Added LightI2X LoRA: {lighti2x_lora}")
   276→    
   277→    # Reward LoRA flag
   278→    if result.get("apply_reward_lora"):
   279→        reward_lora = "reward_model.safetensors"
   280→        if reward_lora not in lora_names:
   281→            lora_names.append(reward_lora)
   282→            lora_mults.append(0.5)
   283→            dprint(f"Added reward LoRA: {reward_lora}")
   284→    
   285→    result["lora_names"] = lora_names
   286→    result["lora_multipliers"] = lora_mults
   287→    
   288→    dprint(f"Result: lora_names={result.get('lora_names', [])}, lora_multipliers={result.get('lora_multipliers', [])}")
   289→    return result
   290→

   296→def test_format_1_wgp_legacy():

   298→    print("\n" + "="*70)

   570→def test_combined_formats():
   571→    """Test combinations of multiple formats"""
   572→    print("\n" + "="*70)
   573→    print("TEST: Combined Formats")
   574→    print("="*70)
   575→    
   576→    results = TestResult()
   577→    
   578→    # Test: activated_loras + additional_loras + feature flags
   579→    print("\nTest: Multiple format sources combined")
   580→    params = {
   581→        "activated_loras": ["explicit.safetensors"],
   582→        "loras_multipliers": "1.2",
   583→        "additional_loras": {
   584→            "https://example.com/downloaded.safetensors": 0.9
   585→        },
   586→        "use_causvid_lora": True
   587→    }
   588→    
   589→    # Process through pipeline
   590→    result = process_format_1_wgp_legacy(params)
   591→    result = process_format_5_additional_dict(result)
   592→    result = process_format_8_feature_flags(result, TEST_MODEL_TYPE)
   593→    
   594→    expected_loras = ["explicit.safetensors", "downloaded.safetensors", "CausVid"]
   595→    if set(result.get("lora_names", [])) == set(expected_loras):
   596→        results.record_pass("Combined formats - all LoRAs present")
   597→    else:
   598→        results.record_fail("Combined formats", f"Expected {expected_loras}, got {result.get('lora_names')}")
   599→    
   600→    # Check multipliers match
   601→    if len(result.get("lora_multipliers", [])) == len(result.get("lora_names", [])):
   602→        results.record_pass("Combined formats - multipliers match names")
   603→    else:
   604→        results.record_fail("Multipliers length", f"Names: {len(result.get('lora_names', []))}, Mults: {len(result.get('lora_multipliers', []))}")
   605→    
   606→    results.summary()
   607→    assert results.failed == 0, f"{results.failed} sub-test(s) failed"

   610→def test_edge_cases():
   611→    """Test edge cases and error conditions"""
   612→    print("\n" + "="*70)
   613→    print("TEST: Edge Cases")
   614→    print("="*70)
   615→    
   616→    results = TestResult()
   617→    
   618→    # Test: Empty lists
   619→    print("\nTest: Empty lists")
   620→    params = {
   621→        "activated_loras": [],
   622→        "loras_multipliers": ""
   623→    }
   624→    result = process_format_1_wgp_legacy(params)
   625→    
   626→    if result.get("lora_names", []) == []:
   627→        results.record_pass("Empty lists handled")
   628→    else:
   629→        results.record_fail("Empty lists", f"Got {result.get('lora_names')}")
   630→    
   631→    # Test: Missing multipliers
   632→    print("\nTest: Missing multipliers")
   633→    params = {
   634→        "lora_names": ["lora1.safetensors", "lora2.safetensors"]
   635→        # No multipliers provided
   636→    }
   637→    result = process_format_3_internal_list(params)
   638→    
   639→    if "lora_names" in result:
   640→        results.record_pass("Missing multipliers doesn't crash")
   641→    else:
   642→        results.record_fail("Missing multipliers", "Unexpected error")
   643→    
   644→    # Test: Whitespace handling
   645→    print("\nTest: Whitespace handling")
   646→    params = {
   647→        "activated_loras": " lora1.safetensors , lora2.safetensors ",
   648→        "loras_multipliers": "1.0, 0.8"  # Comma-separated (matching the loras format)
   649→    }
   650→    result = process_format_1_wgp_legacy(params)
   651→    
   652→    if result.get("lora_names") == ["lora1.safetensors", "lora2.safetensors"]:
   653→        results.record_pass("Whitespace trimmed")
   654→    else:
   655→        results.record_fail("Whitespace", f"Got {result.get('lora_names')}")
   656→    
   657→    # Test: Deduplication
   658→    print("\nTest: Deduplication")
   659→    params = {
   660→        "lora_names": ["lora1.safetensors"],
   661→        "lora_multipliers": [1.0],
   662→        "additional_loras": {
   663→            "lora1.safetensors": 0.8  # Duplicate
   664→        }
   665→    }
   666→    result = process_format_5_additional_dict(params)
   667→    
   668→    # Current system doesn't deduplicate - just checking it doesn't crash
   669→    if "lora_names" in result:
   670→        results.record_pass("Duplicate handling doesn't crash")
   671→    else:
   672→        results.record_fail("Deduplication", "Unexpected error")
   673→    
   674→    results.summary()
   675→    assert results.failed == 0, f"{results.failed} sub-test(s) failed"
   676→

   678→# =============================================================================
   679→# Main Test Runner
   680→# =============================================================================
   681→
   682→def main():
   683→    """Run all baseline tests."""
   684→    print("\n" + "="*70)
   685→    print("LORA FORMAT BASELINE TEST SUITE")
   686→    print("Testing current system behavior before refactoring")
   687→    print("="*70)
   688→    
   689→    all_passed = True
   690→    
   691→    # Run all test suites
   692→    all_passed &= test_format_1_wgp_legacy()
   693→    all_passed &= test_format_3_internal_list()
   694→    all_passed &= test_format_5_additional_dict()
   695→    all_passed &= test_format_6_qwen_task()
   696→    all_passed &= test_format_8_feature_flags()
   697→    all_passed &= test_combined_formats()
   698→    all_passed &= test_edge_cases()
   699→    
   700→    # Final summary
   701→    print("\n" + "="*70)
   702→    print("FINAL RESULTS")
   703→    print("="*70)
   704→    if all_passed:
   705→        print("✅ ALL TESTS PASSED - Baseline established")
   706→        print("\nYou can now refactor with confidence!")
   707→        print("Run this test again after refactoring to validate behavior is preserved.")
   708→    else:
   709→        print("❌ SOME TESTS FAILED - Review failures above")
   710→        print("\nNote: Some failures may be expected (e.g., deduplication not implemented)")
   711→    print("="*70 + "\n")
   712→    
   713→    return 0 if all_passed else 1

    68→
    69→def create_test_video(output_path: Path, num_frames: int = 30, resolution: tuple = (256, 256), fps: int = 16) -> Path:
    70→    """Create a simple test video with colored frames."""
    71→    import cv2
    72→    
    73→    fourcc = cv2.VideoWriter_fourcc(*'mp4v')
    74→    writer = cv2.VideoWriter(str(output_path), fourcc, fps, resolution)
    75→    
    76→    for i in range(num_frames):
    77→        # Create a frame with color gradient based on frame number
    78→        # This makes it easy to visually verify frame extraction
    79→        hue = int(180 * i / num_frames)  # Cycle through hues
    80→        hsv = np.full((resolution[1], resolution[0], 3), [hue, 255, 200], dtype=np.uint8)
    81→        bgr = cv2.cvtColor(hsv, cv2.COLOR_HSV2BGR)
    82→        
    83→        # Add frame number text
    84→        cv2.putText(bgr, f"Frame {i}", (10, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (255, 255, 255), 2)
    85→        
    86→        writer.write(bgr)
    87→    
    88→    writer.release()
    89→    return output_path
    90→

   328→    def test_first_segment(self):

   344→        
   345→        assert start == 71  # 81 - 10
   346→        assert count == 81

   370→        seg_frames = [81, 65, 49, 81]

   372→

   375→        assert start0 == 0

   381→        assert count1 == 65

    78→            self.SAMPLE_PHASE_CONFIG,
    79→            num_inference_steps=6,
    80→            task_id="test_task"
    81→        )
    82→        
    83→        # Simulate what task_registry does
    84→        params = {
    85→            "activated_loras": parsed["lora_names"],
    86→            "loras_multipliers": " ".join(parsed["lora_multipliers"]),
    87→            "additional_loras": parsed["additional_loras"],
    88→        }
    89→        
    90→        config = LoRAConfig.from_params(params, task_id="test_task")
    91→        
    92→        # Should have 2 entries (deduplicated)
    93→        assert len(config.entries) == 2
    94→        
    95→        # All should be PENDING (URLs need download)
    96→        assert all(e.status == LoRAStatus.PENDING for e in config.entries)
    97→        
    98→        # All should have URLs
    99→        assert all(e.url is not None for e in config.entries)
   100→        
   101→        # Filenames should be extracted
   102→        filenames = [e.filename for e in config.entries]
   103→        assert "high_noise.safetensors" in filenames
   104→        assert "style.safetensors" in filenames
   105→
   106→    def test_to_wgp_format_excludes_pending(self):
   107→        """to_wgp_format should exclude PENDING entries."""

   116→            "loras_multipliers": " ".join(parsed["lora_multipliers"]),
   117→        }
   118→        
   119→        config = LoRAConfig.from_params(params, task_id="test_task")

   126→    def test_to_wgp_format_after_download(self):
   127→        """to_wgp_format should include entries after mark_downloaded."""
   128→        parsed = parse_phase_config(

   131→            task_id="test_task"

   134→        params = {
   135→            "activated_loras": parsed["lora_names"],
   136→            "loras_multipliers": " ".join(parsed["lora_multipliers"]),
   137→        }
   138→        
   139→        config = LoRAConfig.from_params(params, task_id="test_task")
   140→        
   141→        # Simulate downloads
   142→        for entry in config.entries:

   144→                config.mark_downloaded(entry.url, entry.filename)
   145→        
   146→        # Now to_wgp_format should return the entries

   153→        assert " " in wgp["loras_multipliers"]
   154→        assert ";" in wgp["loras_multipliers"]

   156→    def test_full_flow_with_mock_download(self):
   157→        """Test the complete flow with mocked download function."""
   158→        
   159→        # 1. Parse phase_config (like orchestrator does)
   160→        parsed = parse_phase_config(
   161→            self.SAMPLE_PHASE_CONFIG,
   162→            num_inference_steps=6,
   163→            task_id="test_task",
   164→            model_name="wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2"
   165→        )
   166→        
   167→        # 2. Build generation_params (like task_registry does)
   168→        generation_params = {
   169→            "prompt": "test prompt",
   170→            "model_name": "wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2",
   171→            "resolution": "896x496",
   172→            "video_length": 61,
   173→            "activated_loras": parsed["lora_names"],
   174→            "loras_multipliers": " ".join(parsed["lora_multipliers"]),
   175→            "additional_loras": parsed["additional_loras"],
   176→            "guidance_phases": parsed["guidance_phases"],
   177→            "switch_threshold": parsed["switch_threshold"],
   178→            "flow_shift": parsed["flow_shift"],
   179→        }
   180→        
   181→        # 3. Parse via TaskConfig (like _convert_to_wgp_task does)
   182→        task_config = TaskConfig.from_params(
   183→            generation_params,
   184→            task_id="test_task",
   185→            task_type="travel_segment",
   186→            model="wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2"
   187→        )
   188→        
   189→        # 4. Verify pending downloads detected
   190→        assert task_config.lora.has_pending_downloads()
   191→        pending = task_config.lora.get_pending_downloads()
   192→        assert len(pending) == 2
   193→        
   194→        # 5. Mock the download step
   195→        with patch('source.models.lora.lora_utils._download_lora_from_url') as mock_download:
   196→            # Mock returns the filename
   197→            mock_download.side_effect = lambda url, task_id: os.path.basename(url)
   198→            
   199→            # Simulate what _convert_to_wgp_task does
   200→            for url, mult in list(task_config.lora.get_pending_downloads().items()):
   201→                local_path = mock_download(url, "test_task")
   202→                task_config.lora.mark_downloaded(url, local_path)
   203→        
   204→        # 6. Convert to WGP format
   205→        wgp_params = task_config.to_wgp_format()
   206→        
   207→        # 7. Verify final output
   208→        assert "activated_loras" in wgp_params
   209→        assert len(wgp_params["activated_loras"]) == 2
   210→        
   211→        # Should be filenames, not URLs
   212→        for lora in wgp_params["activated_loras"]:
   213→            assert not lora.startswith("http")
   214→            assert lora.endswith(".safetensors")
   215→        
   216→        # Multipliers should be phase-config format
   217→        assert "loras_multipliers" in wgp_params
   218→        assert ";" in wgp_params["loras_multipliers"]
   219→        
   220→        print("\n✅ Full flow test passed!")
   221→        print(f"   activated_loras: {wgp_params['activated_loras']}")
   222→        print(f"   loras_multipliers: {wgp_params['loras_multipliers']}")
   223→
   224→    def test_mixed_local_and_url_loras(self):
   225→        """Test mix of local filenames and URLs."""
   226→        params = {

   236→        # Should have 2 entries
   237→        assert len(config.entries) == 2
   238→        
   239→        # One LOCAL, one PENDING
   240→        statuses = [e.status for e in config.entries]
   241→        assert LoRAStatus.LOCAL in statuses
   242→        assert LoRAStatus.PENDING in statuses

   249→    def test_deduplication(self):
   250→        """Test that same LoRA in activated_loras and additional_loras is deduplicated."""
   251→        url = "https://huggingface.co/test/lora.safetensors"
   252→        
   253→        params = {
   254→            "activated_loras": [url],
   255→            "loras_multipliers": "0.5;0.8",
   256→            "additional_loras": {url: 1.0},  # Same URL, different multiplier
   257→        }
   258→        
   259→        config = LoRAConfig.from_params(params, task_id="test_task")
   260→        
   261→        # Should only have 1 entry (deduplicated by filename)
   262→        assert len(config.entries) == 1
   263→        
   264→        # Phase-config multiplier from activated_loras should be preserved
   265→        # (additional_loras doesn't have semicolon, so it doesn't override)
   266→        assert config.entries[0].multiplier == "0.5;0.8"

   313→class TestFromDbTask:

   400→class TestThreePhaseConfig:
   401→    """Test 3-phase configurations (common for Lightning models)."""
   402→    
   403→    THREE_PHASE_CONFIG = {
   404→        "num_phases": 3,
   405→        "steps_per_phase": [2, 2, 2],
   406→        "phases": [
   407→            {
   408→                "guidance_scale": 1.0,
   409→                "loras": [
   410→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/high_noise.safetensors", "multiplier": 1.2},
   411→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/style.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.5},
   412→                ]
   413→            },
   414→            {
   415→                "guidance_scale": 1.0,
   416→                "loras": [
   417→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/high_noise.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.6},
   418→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/style.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.5},
   419→                ]
   420→            },
   421→            {
   422→                "guidance_scale": 1.0,
   423→                "loras": [
   424→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/high_noise.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.0},
   425→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/style.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.8},
   426→                ]
   427→            }
   428→        ]
   429→    }

   431→    def test_three_phase_multipliers(self):
   432→        """3-phase config should produce multipliers with 3 values."""
   433→        result = parse_phase_config(
   434→            self.THREE_PHASE_CONFIG,
   435→            num_inference_steps=6,
   436→            task_id="test"
   437→        )
   438→        
   439→        # Should have 2 LoRAs
   440→        assert len(result["lora_names"]) == 2
   441→        assert len(result["lora_multipliers"]) == 2
   442→        
   443→        # Each multiplier should have 3 semicolon-separated values
   444→        for mult in result["lora_multipliers"]:
   445→            values = mult.split(";")
   446→            assert len(values) == 3, f"Expected 3 phases, got {len(values)}: {mult}"
   447→        
   448→        # Verify specific values
   449→        assert result["lora_multipliers"][0] == "1.2;0.6;0.0"  # high_noise
   450→        assert result["lora_multipliers"][1] == "0.5;0.5;0.8"  # style
   451→    
   452→    def test_three_phase_through_taskconfig(self):
   453→        """3-phase config should work through full TaskConfig flow."""
   454→        parsed = parse_phase_config(
   455→            self.THREE_PHASE_CONFIG,
   456→            num_inference_steps=6,
   457→            task_id="test"
   458→        )

   460→        params = {
   461→            "activated_loras": parsed["lora_names"],
   462→            "loras_multipliers": " ".join(parsed["lora_multipliers"]),
   463→        }
   464→        
   465→        config = LoRAConfig.from_params(params, task_id="test")
   466→        
   467→        # Should preserve 3-phase multipliers
   468→        for entry in config.entries:
   469→            if ";" in str(entry.multiplier):
   470→                values = str(entry.multiplier).split(";")
   471→                assert len(values) == 3

   474→class TestQueueIntegration:
   475→    """Test that simulates what _convert_to_wgp_task does."""
   476→    
   477→    def test_queue_conversion_flow(self):
   478→        """
   479→        Simulate the exact flow in HeadlessTaskQueue._convert_to_wgp_task.
   480→        This is the critical integration point.
   481→        """
   482→        # Params as they would come from a GenerationTask
   483→        task_params = {
   484→            "prompt": "test prompt",
   485→            "model_name": "wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2",
   486→            "resolution": "896x496",
   487→            "video_length": 61,
   488→            "activated_loras": [
   489→                "https://huggingface.co/test/lora1.safetensors",
   490→                "https://huggingface.co/test/lora2.safetensors",
   491→            ],
   492→            "loras_multipliers": "1.0;0.0 0.5;0.8",
   493→            "_source_task_type": "travel_segment",
   494→        }
   495→        
   496→        # Step 1: Parse into TaskConfig (like _convert_to_wgp_task does)
   497→        config = TaskConfig.from_db_task(
   498→            task_params,
   499→            task_id="test_queue",
   500→            task_type=task_params.get('_source_task_type', ''),
   501→            model="wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2",
   502→            debug_mode=True
   503→        )
   504→        
   505→        # Step 2: Check for pending downloads
   506→        assert config.lora.has_pending_downloads()
   507→        pending = config.lora.get_pending_downloads()
   508→        assert len(pending) == 2
   509→        
   510→        # Step 3: Simulate downloads
   511→        for url, mult in list(pending.items()):
   512→            local_filename = os.path.basename(url)
   513→            config.lora.mark_downloaded(url, local_filename)
   514→        
   515→        # Step 4: Validate
   516→        config.validate()
   517→        # Should have no critical errors after download
   518→        
   519→        # Step 5: Convert to WGP format
   520→        wgp_params = config.to_wgp_format()
   521→        
   522→        # Step 6: Verify output
   523→        assert "activated_loras" in wgp_params
   524→        loras = wgp_params["activated_loras"]
   525→        
   526→        # Should have 2 LoRAs
   527→        assert len(loras) == 2
   528→        
   529→        # Should be filenames, NOT URLs
   530→        for lora in loras:
   531→            assert not lora.startswith("http"), f"URL leaked to WGP: {lora}"
   532→            assert lora.endswith(".safetensors")
   533→        
   534→        # Multipliers should be preserved
   535→        assert "loras_multipliers" in wgp_params
   536→        assert ";" in wgp_params["loras_multipliers"]  # Phase config format
   537→        
   538→        print("\n✅ Queue integration test passed!")
   539→        print(f"   Input URLs: {task_params['activated_loras']}")
   540→        print(f"   Output filenames: {wgp_params['activated_loras']}")
   541→        print(f"   Output multipliers: {wgp_params['loras_multipliers']}")
   542→

   544→class TestDownloaderUtils:
   545→    """Test downloader helper behaviors used by the queue on feat/typed-params."""
   546→
   547→    def test_download_normalizes_hf_blob_url(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
   548→        """
   549→        HF URLs sometimes come in as /blob/ links; we should normalize to /resolve/
   550→        and pass the correct repo_id + filename to hf_hub_download.
   551→        """
   552→        loras_dir = tmp_path / "loras"
   553→        loras_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   554→
   555→        called = {}
   556→
   557→        def fake_hf_hub_download(repo_id, filename, local_dir, local_dir_use_symlinks=False, subfolder=None, **kwargs):
   558→            called["repo_id"] = repo_id
   559→            called["filename"] = filename
   560→            called["local_dir"] = local_dir
   561→            called["subfolder"] = subfolder
   562→            # create the file the function expects to exist afterwards
   563→            Path(local_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   564→            out = Path(local_dir) / filename
   565→            out.write_bytes(b"stub")
   566→            return str(out)
   567→
   568→        # Patch the import used inside _download_lora_from_url
   569→        import huggingface_hub
   570→        monkeypatch.setattr(huggingface_hub, "hf_hub_download", fake_hf_hub_download)
   571→
   572→        # Mock path resolution so downloads go to tmp_path instead of real Wan2GP/
   573→        import source.models.lora.lora_paths as lora_paths
   574→        monkeypatch.setattr(lora_paths, "get_lora_dir_for_model", lambda model_type, wan_dir: loras_dir)
   575→
   576→        url = "https://huggingface.co/org/repo/blob/main/loras/my_lora.safetensors"
   577→        out = _download_lora_from_url(url, task_id="test")
   578→
   579→        assert out == "my_lora.safetensors"
   580→        assert called["repo_id"] == "org/repo"
   581→        # lora_utils passes filename as basename and subfolder for resolve/main/<subfolder>/<filename>
   582→        assert called["filename"] == "my_lora.safetensors"
   583→        assert called["subfolder"] == "loras"
   584→        assert Path(called["local_dir"]).name == "loras"
   585→
   586→    def test_download_collision_prefixes_parent_and_removes_legacy_generic(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
   587→        """
   588→        When a collision-prone filename like high_noise_model.safetensors is used, we prefix the
   589→        parent folder and remove any legacy generic file in standard lora dirs.
   590→        """
   591→        loras_dir = tmp_path / "loras"
   592→        loras_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   593→
   594→        # Create a legacy collision-prone file that should be cleaned up
   595→        legacy = loras_dir / "high_noise_model.safetensors"
   596→        legacy.write_bytes(b"old")
   597→
   598→        # Patch urlretrieve to avoid network and write the "downloaded" file
   599→        def fake_urlretrieve(url, filename):
   600→            Path(filename).write_bytes(b"new")

way too small\n        ok, msg = validate_lora_file(p, \"lora.safetensors\")\n        assert ok is False\n        assert \"too small\" in msg.lower()\n\n\nclass TestCheckLorasInDirectory:\n    def test_nonexistent_dir(self, tmp_path):\n        result = check_loras_in_directory(tmp_path / \"nonexistent\")\n        assert \"error\" in result\n\n    def test_empty_dir(self, tmp_path):\n        result = check_loras_in_directory(tmp_path)\n        assert result[\"total_files\"] == 0\n\n    def test_dir_with_valid_lora(self, tmp_path):\n        # File must have 'lora' in name or .safetensors extension to be found\n        p = tmp_path / \"my_lora.safetensors\"\n        p.write_bytes(b\"\\x00\" * 2_000_000)\n        result = check_loras_in_directory(tmp_path)\n        assert result[\"total_files\"] >= 1\n\n\nclass TestNormalizeActivatedLorasList:\n    def test_list_passthrough(self):\n        assert _normalize_activated_loras_list([\"a\", \"b\"]) == [\"a\", \"b\"]\n\n    def test_string_splitting(self):\n        result = _normalize_activated_loras_list(\"a,b,c\")\n        assert result == [\"a\", \"b\", \"c\"]\n\n    def test_empty_list(self):\n        assert _normalize_activated_loras_list([]) == []\n"

   205→class TestGetUserIdFromJwt:
   206→    def _make_jwt(self, payload: dict) -> str:
   207→        header = base64.b64encode(b'{"alg":"HS256"}').decode().rstrip("=")
   208→        body = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(payload).encode()).decode().rstrip("=")
   209→        return f"{header}.{body}.fakesig"
   210→
   211→    def test_valid_jwt(self):
   212→        jwt = self._make_jwt({"sub": "user-abc-123"})
   213→        assert _get_user_id_from_jwt(jwt) == "user-abc-123"

   216→        assert _get_user_id_from_jwt("") is None
   217→

   219→        assert _get_user_id_from_jwt("not.a.valid-base64!") is None
   220→

   223→        assert _get_user_id_from_jwt(jwt) is None

1→"""
     2→Edge function call helpers: retry wrapper and JWT utilities.
     3→"""
     4→import json
     5→import time
     6→import base64
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→import httpx
    10→
    11→from source.core.log import headless_logger
    12→
    13→__all__: list[str] = []
    14→
    15→from .config import (
    16→    EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX,
    17→    RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES)
    18→
    19→def _call_edge_function_with_retry(
    20→    edge_url: str,
    21→    payload,
    22→    headers: dict,
    23→    function_name: str,
    24→    *,
    25→    context_id: str = "",  # task_id or worker_id for logging
    26→    timeout: int = 30,
    27→    max_retries: int = 3,
    28→    method: str = "POST",
    29→    fallback_url: str | None = None,  # Optional 404 fallback URL
    30→    retry_on_404_patterns: list[str] | None = None,  # Patterns in 404 response body to retry
    31→) -> tuple[httpx.Response | None, str | None]:
    32→    """
    33→    Call a Supabase edge function with retry logic for transient errors.
    34→
    35→    Retries on:
    36→    - 502 Bad Gateway, 503 Service Unavailable, 504 Gateway Timeout
    37→    - Network errors (TimeoutException, RequestError)
    38→    - 404 errors if response body matches retry_on_404_patterns (e.g. "Task not found")
    39→
    40→    Args:
    41→        edge_url: The edge function URL
    42→        payload: JSON payload (for POST) or None
    43→        headers: HTTP headers
    44→        function_name: Name of function for logging (e.g. "complete_task", "update-task-status")
    45→        context_id: Task/worker ID for logging context
    46→        timeout: Base timeout in seconds (increases on retries)
    47→        max_retries: Maximum retry attempts
    48→        method: HTTP method ("POST" or "PUT")
    49→        fallback_url: Optional fallback URL to try on 404
    50→        retry_on_404_patterns: List of substrings - if 404 response contains any, retry
    51→
    52→    Returns:
    53→        Tuple of (response, error_message)
    54→        - On success: (response, None)
    55→        - On failure: (response_or_None, standardized_error_message)
    56→
    57→        Error message format for detection:
    58→        "[EDGE_FAIL:{function_name}:{error_type}] {details}"
    59→    """
    60→    ctx = f" for {context_id}" if context_id else ""
    61→
    62→    for attempt in range(max_retries):
    63→        try:
    64→            current_timeout = timeout + (attempt * 15)  # Increase timeout on retries
    65→
    66→            if method == "PUT":
    67→                # For large uploads, avoid buffering entire files in memory:
    68→                # - If payload is a Path/str to an existing file, open+stream per attempt.
    69→                # - If payload is bytes, send directly.
    70→                # - Otherwise, pass through to httpx (caller responsibility).
    71→                if isinstance(payload, (str, Path)) and Path(payload).exists():
    72→                    with open(Path(payload), "rb") as f:
    73→                        resp = httpx.put(edge_url, content=f, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    74→                else:
    75→                    resp = httpx.put(edge_url, content=payload, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    76→            else:
    77→                resp = httpx.post(edge_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    78→
    79→            # Handle 404 fallback (e.g. hyphen vs underscore naming)
    80→            if resp.status_code == 404 and fallback_url:
    81→                headless_logger.debug(f"[RETRY] {function_name} returned 404; trying fallback URL: {fallback_url}")
    82→                if method == "PUT":
    83→                    if isinstance(payload, (str, Path)) and Path(payload).exists():
    84→                        with open(Path(payload), "rb") as f:
    85→                            resp = httpx.put(fallback_url, content=f, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    86→                    else:
    87→                        resp = httpx.put(fallback_url, content=payload, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    88→                else:
    89→                    resp = httpx.post(fallback_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    90→
    91→            # Success
    92→            if resp.status_code in [200, 201]:
    93→                return resp, None
    94→
    95→            # Retry on specific 404 patterns (e.g. "Task not found" due to replication lag)
    96→            if resp.status_code == 404 and retry_on_404_patterns and attempt < max_retries - 1:
    97→                resp_text = resp.text
    98→                should_retry = any(pattern.lower() in resp_text.lower() for pattern in retry_on_404_patterns)
    99→                if should_retry:
   100→                    wait_time = 2 ** attempt  # Exponential backoff: 1s, 2s, 4s
   101→                    headless_logger.essential(f"[RETRY] {function_name} got 404 with retryable pattern{ctx} (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {wait_time}s...")
   102→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[RETRY] 404 response: {resp_text[:200]}")
   103→                    time.sleep(wait_time)
   104→                    continue
   105→
   106→            # Retryable error (5xx)
   107→            if resp.status_code in RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES and attempt < max_retries - 1:
   108→                wait_time = 2 ** attempt  # Exponential backoff: 1s, 2s, 4s
   109→                headless_logger.essential(f"[RETRY] {function_name} got {resp.status_code}{ctx} (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {wait_time}s...")
   110→                time.sleep(wait_time)
   111→                continue
   112→
   113→            # Non-retryable error or final attempt
   114→            error_type = "5XX_TRANSIENT" if resp.status_code in RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES else f"HTTP_{resp.status_code}"
   115→            error_msg = f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:{function_name}:{error_type}] {resp.status_code} after {attempt + 1} attempts{ctx}: {resp.text[:200]}"
   116→
   117→            if resp.status_code in RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES:
   118→                headless_logger.error(f"{function_name} failed with {resp.status_code} after {max_retries} attempts{ctx}")
   119→
   120→            return resp, error_msg
   121→
   122→        except httpx.TimeoutException as e:
   123→            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
   124→                wait_time = 2 ** attempt
   125→                headless_logger.essential(f"[RETRY] {function_name} timeout{ctx} (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {wait_time}s...")
   126→                time.sleep(wait_time)
   127→                continue
   128→            else:
   129→                error_msg = f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:{function_name}:TIMEOUT] Timed out after {max_retries} attempts{ctx}: {e}"
   130→                headless_logger.error(error_msg)
   131→                return None, error_msg
   132→
   133→        except httpx.RequestError as e:
   134→            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
   135→                wait_time = 2 ** attempt
   136→                headless_logger.essential(f"[RETRY] {function_name} network error{ctx} (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {wait_time}s: {e}")
   137→                time.sleep(wait_time)
   138→                continue
   139→            else:
   140→                error_msg = f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:{function_name}:NETWORK] Request failed after {max_retries} attempts{ctx}: {e}"
   141→                headless_logger.error(error_msg)
   142→                return None, error_msg
   143→
   144→    # Should not reach here, but safety fallback
   145→    error_msg = f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:{function_name}:UNKNOWN] All retries exhausted{ctx}"
   146→    return None, error_msg
   147→
   148→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Internal Helpers for Supabase
   150→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→def _get_user_id_from_jwt(jwt_str: str) -> str | None:
   153→    """Decodes a JWT and extracts the 'sub' (user ID) claim without validation."""
   154→    if not jwt_str:
   155→        return None
   156→    try:
   157→        # JWT is composed of header.payload.signature
   158→        _, payload_b64, _ = jwt_str.split('.')
   159→        # The payload is base64 encoded. It needs to be padded to be decoded correctly.
   160→        payload_b64 += '=' * (-len(payload_b64) % 4)
   161→        payload_json = base64.b64decode(payload_b64).decode('utf-8')
   162→        payload = json.loads(payload_json)
   163→        user_id = payload.get('sub')
   164→        headless_logger.debug(f"JWT Decode: Extracted user ID (sub): {user_id}")
   165→        return user_id
   166→    except (ValueError, KeyError, json.JSONDecodeError) as e:
   167→        headless_logger.debug(f"[ERROR] Could not decode JWT to get user ID: {e}")
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→def _is_jwt_token(token_str: str) -> bool:
   171→    """
   172→    Checks if a token string looks like a JWT (has 3 parts separated by dots).
   173→    """
   174→    if not token_str:
   175→        return False
   176→    parts = token_str.split('.')
   177→    return len(parts) == 3
   178→
```

1→"""
     2→Shared pytest fixtures for all test modules.
     3→
     4→Provides:
     5→- mock_db_config: patches source.core.db.config module globals
     6→- mock_httpx: patches httpx.post/put at module level
     7→- mock_sleep: patches time.sleep to no-op
     8→- sample_task_params: minimal valid task parameter dict
     9→- sample_phase_config: 2-phase config with 2 LoRAs
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
    13→import pytest
    14→
    15→
    16→@pytest.fixture
    17→def mock_db_config():
    18→    """Patch source.core.db.config module globals with MagicMock values.
    19→
    20→    All DB submodules import ``from . import config as _cfg`` and access
    21→    ``_cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT``, so patching the module-level attributes is
    22→    the correct target.

    25→
    26→    originals = {
    27→        "SUPABASE_CLIENT": _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT,
    28→        "SUPABASE_URL": _cfg.SUPABASE_URL,
    29→        "[REDACTED],

    31→        "SUPABASE_EDGE_CREATE_TASK_URL": _cfg.SUPABASE_EDGE_CREATE_TASK_URL,
    32→        "SUPABASE_EDGE_CLAIM_TASK_URL": _cfg.SUPABASE_EDGE_CLAIM_TASK_URL,
    33→    }
    34→
    35→    mock_client = MagicMock()
    36→    _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT = mock_client
    37→    _cfg.SUPABASE_URL = "https://test.supabase.co"
    38→    _cfg.[REDACTED]
    39→    _cfg.SUPABASE_EDGE_COMPLETE_TASK_URL = "https://test.supabase.co/functions/v1/complete_task"
    40→    _cfg.SUPABASE_EDGE_CREATE_TASK_URL = "https://test.supabase.co/functions/v1/create-task"
    41→    _cfg.SUPABASE_EDGE_CLAIM_TASK_URL = "https://test.supabase.co/functions/v1/claim-next-task"
    42→
    43→    yield {
    44→        "client": mock_client,
    45→        "url": _cfg.SUPABASE_URL,
    46→        "[REDACTED],
    47→    }
    48→
    49→    # Restore originals
    50→    for attr, val in originals.items():
    51→        setattr(_cfg, attr, val)

    54→@pytest.fixture
    55→def mock_httpx():

    58→    mock_put = MagicMock()

    63→            status_code=200,
    64→            text="{}",
    65→            json=MagicMock(return_value={}),
    66→        )
    67→
    68→    with patch("httpx.post", mock_post), patch("httpx.put", mock_put):
    69→        yield {"post": mock_post, "put": mock_put}
    70→
    71→
    72→@pytest.fixture

1→"""
     2→Task polling, output queries, and parameter retrieval.
     3→"""
     4→import os
     5→import time
     6→import traceback
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→import httpx
    10→from postgrest.exceptions import APIError
    11→
    12→from source.core.log import headless_logger
    13→
    14→__all__ = [
    15→    "poll_task_status",
    16→    "get_task_output_location_from_db",
    17→    "get_task_params",
    18→    "get_abs_path_from_db_path",
    19→]
    20→
    21→from . import config as _cfg
    22→from .config import (
    23→    STATUS_QUEUED,
    24→    STATUS_IN_PROGRESS,
    25→    STATUS_COMPLETE,
    26→    STATUS_FAILED)
    27→from .edge_helpers import _call_edge_function_with_retry
    28→
    29→def poll_task_status(task_id: str, poll_interval_seconds: int = 10, timeout_seconds: int = 1800, db_path: str | None = None) -> str | None:
    30→    """
    31→    Polls Supabase for task completion and returns the output_location.
    32→
    33→    Args:
    34→        task_id: Task ID to poll
    35→        poll_interval_seconds: Seconds between polls
    36→        timeout_seconds: Maximum time to wait
    37→        db_path: Ignored (kept for API compatibility)
    38→
    39→    Returns:
    40→        Output location string if successful, None otherwise
    41→    """
    42→    headless_logger.essential(f"Polling for completion of task {task_id} (timeout: {timeout_seconds}s)...", task_id=task_id)
    43→    start_time = time.time()
    44→    last_status_print_time = 0
    45→
    46→    while True:
    47→        current_time = time.time()
    48→        if current_time - start_time > timeout_seconds:
    49→            headless_logger.error(f"Timeout polling for task {task_id} after {timeout_seconds} seconds.", task_id=task_id)
    50→            return None
    51→
    52→        status = None
    53→        output_location = None
    54→
    55→        if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
    56→            headless_logger.error("Supabase client not initialized. Cannot poll status.", task_id=task_id)
    57→            time.sleep(poll_interval_seconds)
    58→            continue
    59→        try:
    60→            # Direct table query for polling status
    61→            resp = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).select("status, output_location").eq("id", task_id).single().execute()
    62→            if resp.data:
    63→                status = resp.data.get("status")
    64→                output_location = resp.data.get("output_location")
    65→        except (APIError, httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError, KeyError) as e:
    66→            headless_logger.error(f"Supabase error while polling task {task_id}: {e}. Retrying...", task_id=task_id)
    67→
    68→        if status:
    69→            if current_time - last_status_print_time > poll_interval_seconds * 2:
    70→                headless_logger.essential(f"Task {task_id}: Status = {status} (Output: {output_location if output_location else 'N/A'})", task_id=task_id)
    71→                last_status_print_time = current_time
    72→
    73→            if status == STATUS_COMPLETE:
    74→                if output_location:
    75→                    headless_logger.essential(f"Task {task_id} completed successfully. Output: {output_location}", task_id=task_id)
    76→                    return output_location
    77→                else:
    78→                    headless_logger.error(f"Task {task_id} is COMPLETE but output_location is missing. Assuming failure.", task_id=task_id)
    79→                    return None
    80→            elif status == STATUS_FAILED:
    81→                headless_logger.error(f"Task {task_id} failed. Error details: {output_location}", task_id=task_id)
    82→                return None
    83→            elif status not in [STATUS_QUEUED, STATUS_IN_PROGRESS]:
    84→                headless_logger.warning(f"Task {task_id} has unknown status '{status}'. Treating as error.", task_id=task_id)
    85→                return None
    86→        else:
    87→            if current_time - last_status_print_time > poll_interval_seconds * 2:
    88→                headless_logger.essential(f"Task {task_id}: Not found in DB yet or status pending...", task_id=task_id)
    89→                last_status_print_time = current_time
    90→
    91→        time.sleep(poll_interval_seconds)
    92→
    93→# Helper to query DB for a specific task's output (needed by segment handler)
    94→def get_task_output_location_from_db(task_id_to_find: str) -> str | None:
    95→    """
    96→    Fetches a task's output location via the get-task-output Edge Function.
    97→
    98→    This uses an edge function instead of direct DB query to work with
    99→    workers that only have anon key access (RLS would block direct queries).
   100→
   101→    Args:
   102→        task_id_to_find: Task ID to look up
   103→
   104→    Returns:
   105→        Output location string if task is complete, None otherwise
   106→    """
   107→    headless_logger.debug(f"Fetching output location for task: {task_id_to_find}")
   108→
   109→    # Build edge function URL
   110→    edge_url = (
   111→        os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_GET_TASK_OUTPUT_URL")
   112→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/get-task-output" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    if not edge_url:
   116→        headless_logger.error(f"No edge function URL available for get-task-output", task_id=task_id_to_find)
   117→        return None
   118→
   119→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   120→        headless_logger.error(f"No access token available for get-task-output", task_id=task_id_to_find)
   121→        return None
   122→
   123→    headers = {
   124→        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   125→        "Authorization": f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    payload = {"task_id": task_id_to_find}
   129→
   130→    try:
   131→        resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   132→            edge_url=edge_url,
   133→            payload=payload,
   134→            headers=headers,
   135→            function_name="get-task-output",
   136→            context_id=task_id_to_find,
   137→            timeout=30,
   138→            max_retries=3,
   139→            method="POST",
   140→            retry_on_404_patterns=["not found", "Task not found"],  # Handle race conditions
   141→        )
   142→
   143→        if edge_error:
   144→            headless_logger.error(f"get-task-output failed for {task_id_to_find}: {edge_error}", task_id=task_id_to_find)
   145→            return None
   146→
   147→        if resp and resp.status_code == 200:
   148→            data = resp.json()
   149→            status = data.get("status")
   150→            output_location = data.get("output_location")
   151→
   152→            if status == STATUS_COMPLETE and output_location:
   153→                headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id_to_find} output fetched successfully")
   154→                return output_location
   155→            else:
   156→                headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id_to_find} not complete or no output. Status: {status}")
   157→                return None
   158→        elif resp and resp.status_code == 404:
   159→            headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id_to_find} not found")
   160→            return None
   161→        else:
   162→            status_code = resp.status_code if resp else "no response"
   163→            headless_logger.error(f"get-task-output unexpected response for {task_id_to_find}: {status_code}", task_id=task_id_to_find)
   164→            return None
   165→
   166→    except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   167→        headless_logger.error(f"get-task-output exception for {task_id_to_find}: {e}", task_id=task_id_to_find)
   168→        traceback.print_exc()
   169→        return None
   170→

   186→                if resp.data:

   188→            except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   189→                headless_logger.debug(f"Error getting task params for {task_id}: {e}")
   190→        return None
   191→
   192→    headers = {
   193→        "Content-Type": "application/json",

   195→    }
   196→
   197→    payload = {"task_id": task_id}
   198→
   199→    try:
   200→        resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   201→            edge_url=edge_url,
   202→            payload=payload,
   203→            headers=headers,
   204→            function_name="get-task-output",
   205→            context_id=task_id,
   206→            timeout=30,
   207→            max_retries=3,
   208→            method="POST",
   209→            retry_on_404_patterns=["not found", "Task not found"],  # Handle race conditions
   210→        )
   211→
   212→        if edge_error:
   213→            headless_logger.debug(f"get-task-output (params) failed for {task_id}: {edge_error}")
   214→            return None
   215→
   216→        if resp and resp.status_code == 200:
   217→            data = resp.json()
   218→            return data.get("params")
   219→        return None
   220→
   221→    except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   222→        headless_logger.debug(f"Error getting task params for {task_id}: {e}")
   223→        return None
   224→
   225→def get_abs_path_from_db_path(db_path: str) -> Path | None:
   226→    """
   227→    Helper to resolve a path from the DB to a usable absolute path.
   228→    Assumes paths from Supabase are already absolute or valid URLs.
   229→    """
   230→    if not db_path:
   231→        return None
   232→
   233→    # Path from DB is assumed to be absolute (Supabase) or a URL
   234→    resolved_path = Path(db_path).resolve()
   235→
   236→    if resolved_path and resolved_path.exists():
   237→        return resolved_path
   238→    else:
   239→        headless_logger.debug(f"Warning: Resolved path '{resolved_path}' from DB path '{db_path}' does not exist.")
   240→        return None
   241→

    31→def get_task_dependency(task_id: str, max_retries: int = 3, retry_delay: float = 0.5) -> str | None:
    32→    """
    33→    Gets the dependency task ID for a given task ID via edge function.
    34→
    35→    Includes retry logic to handle race conditions where a newly created task
    36→    may not be immediately visible in the database.
    37→
    38→    Args:
    39→        task_id: Task ID to get dependency for
    40→        max_retries: Maximum number of retry attempts (default: 3)
    41→        retry_delay: Seconds to wait between retries (default: 0.5)
    42→
    43→    Returns:
    44→        Dependency task ID or None if no dependency
    45→    """
    46→    headless_logger.debug(f"Fetching dependency for task: {task_id}")
    47→
    48→    # Build edge function URL
    49→    edge_url = (
    50→        os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_GET_TASK_OUTPUT_URL")
    51→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/get-task-output" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
    52→    )
    53→
    54→    if not edge_url or not _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
    55→        # Fallback to direct query if edge function not available
    56→        if _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
    57→            for attempt in range(max_retries):
    58→                try:
    59→                    response = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).select("dependant_on").eq("id", task_id).single().execute()
    60→                    if response.data:
    61→                        return response.data.get("dependant_on")
    62→                    return None
    63→                except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
    64→                    if "0 rows" in str(e) and attempt < max_retries - 1:
    65→                        time.sleep(retry_delay)
    66→                        continue
    67→                    headless_logger.debug(f"Error fetching dependant_on for task {task_id}: {e}")
    68→                    return None
    69→        return None
    70→
    71→    headers = {
    72→        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    73→        "Authorization": f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    payload = {"task_id": task_id}
    77→
    78→    # Use consistent retry pattern with retry_on_404_patterns for race conditions
    79→    resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
    80→        edge_url=edge_url,
    81→        payload=payload,
    82→        headers=headers,
    83→        function_name="get-task-output",
    84→        context_id=task_id,
    85→        timeout=30,
    86→        max_retries=max_retries,
    87→        method="POST",
    88→        retry_on_404_patterns=["not found", "Task not found"],  # Handle race conditions
    89→    )
    90→
    91→    if resp and resp.status_code == 200:
    92→        data = resp.json()
    93→        return data.get("dependant_on")
    94→    elif edge_error:
    95→        headless_logger.debug(f"Error fetching dependency for {task_id}: {edge_error}")
    96→
    97→    return None
    98→
    99→def get_orchestrator_child_tasks(orchestrator_task_id: str) -> dict:
   100→    """
   101→    Gets all child tasks for a given orchestrator task ID via edge function.
   102→    Returns dict with task type lists: 'segments', 'stitch', 'join_clips_segment',
   103→    'join_clips_orchestrator', 'join_final_stitch'.
   104→    """
   105→    empty_result = {'segments': [], 'stitch': [], 'join_clips_segment': [], 'join_clips_orchestrator': [], 'join_final_stitch': []}
   106→    headless_logger.debug(f"Fetching child tasks for orchestrator: {orchestrator_task_id}")
   107→
   108→    # Build edge function URL
   109→    edge_url = (
   110→        os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_GET_ORCHESTRATOR_CHILDREN_URL")
   111→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/get-orchestrator-children" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    def _categorize_tasks(tasks_data: list) -> dict:
   115→        """Categorize tasks by type."""
   116→        segments = []
   117→        stitch = []
   118→        join_clips_segment = []
   119→        join_clips_orchestrator = []
   120→        join_final_stitch = []
   121→
   122→        for task in tasks_data:
   123→            task_data = {
   124→                'id': task['id'],
   125→                'task_type': task['task_type'],
   126→                'status': task['status'],
   127→                'params': task.get('params', {}),
   128→                'task_params': task.get('params', {}),
   129→                'output_location': task.get('output_location', '')
   130→            }
   131→            if task['task_type'] == 'travel_segment':
   132→                segments.append(task_data)
   133→            elif task['task_type'] == 'travel_stitch':
   134→                stitch.append(task_data)
   135→            elif task['task_type'] == 'join_clips_segment':
   136→                join_clips_segment.append(task_data)
   137→            elif task['task_type'] == 'join_clips_orchestrator':
   138→                join_clips_orchestrator.append(task_data)
   139→            elif task['task_type'] == 'join_final_stitch':
   140→                join_final_stitch.append(task_data)
   141→
   142→        return {
   143→            'segments': segments,
   144→            'stitch': stitch,
   145→            'join_clips_segment': join_clips_segment,
   146→            'join_clips_orchestrator': join_clips_orchestrator,
   147→            'join_final_stitch': join_final_stitch,
   148→        }
   149→
   150→    # Try edge function first
   151→    if edge_url and _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   152→        headers = {
   153→            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   154→            "Authorization": f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   155→        }
   156→        payload = {"orchestrator_task_id": orchestrator_task_id}
   157→
   158→        try:
   159→            resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   160→                edge_url=edge_url,
   161→                payload=payload,
   162→                headers=headers,
   163→                function_name="get-orchestrator-children",
   164→                context_id=orchestrator_task_id,
   165→                timeout=30,
   166→                max_retries=3,
   167→                method="POST",
   168→                retry_on_404_patterns=["not found"],  # Handle race conditions
   169→            )
   170→
   171→            if resp and resp.status_code == 200:
   172→                data = resp.json()
   173→                tasks = data.get("tasks", [])
   174→                return _categorize_tasks(tasks)
   175→            elif edge_error:
   176→                headless_logger.debug(f"get-orchestrator-children failed: {edge_error}")
   177→        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   178→            headless_logger.debug(f"Error calling get-orchestrator-children: {e}")
   179→
   180→    # Fallback to direct query if edge function not available or failed
   181→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   182→        headless_logger.error(f"No edge function or Supabase client available for get_orchestrator_child_tasks", task_id=orchestrator_task_id)
   183→        return empty_result
   184→
   185→    try:
   186→        response = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME)\
   187→            .select("id, task_type, status, params, output_location")\
   188→            .contains("params", {"orchestrator_task_id_ref": orchestrator_task_id})\
   189→            .order("created_at", desc=False)\
   190→            .execute()
   191→
   192→        if response.data:
   193→            return _categorize_tasks(response.data)
   194→        return empty_result
   195→
   196→    except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   197→        headless_logger.debug(f"Error querying Supabase for orchestrator child tasks {orchestrator_task_id}: {e}")
   198→        traceback.print_exc()
   199→        return empty_result
