   201→def get_task_current_status(task_id: str) -> str | None:
   202→    """
   203→    Lightweight status lookup for a single task via the get-task-status edge function.
   204→    Used by orchestrator handlers to detect cancellation before creating child tasks.
   205→
   206→    Returns the status string (e.g. "Queued", "In Progress", "Cancelled") or None on error.
   207→    """
   208→    # Build edge function URL
   209→    edge_url = (
   210→        os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_GET_TASK_STATUS_URL")
   211→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/get-task-status" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   212→    )
   213→
   214→    # Try edge function first (works for local workers without service key)
   215→    if edge_url and _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   216→        headers = {
   217→            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   218→            "Authorization": f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   219→        }
   220→        payload = {"task_id": task_id}
   221→
   222→        try:
   223→            resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   224→                edge_url=edge_url,
   225→                payload=payload,
   226→                headers=headers,
   227→                function_name="get-task-status",
   228→                context_id=task_id,
   229→                timeout=15,
   230→                max_retries=2,
   231→                method="POST")
   232→
   233→            if resp and resp.status_code == 200:
   234→                data = resp.json()
   235→                return data.get("status")
   236→            elif edge_error:
   237→                headless_logger.debug(f"[GET_TASK_STATUS] Edge function failed for {task_id}: {edge_error}")
   238→        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   239→            headless_logger.debug(f"[GET_TASK_STATUS] Error calling get-task-status for {task_id}: {e}")
   240→
   241→    # Fallback to direct query if edge function not available or failed
   242→    if _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   243→        try:
   244→            resp = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).select("status").eq("id", task_id).single().execute()
   245→            if resp.data:
   246→                return resp.data.get("status")
   247→        except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   248→            headless_logger.debug(f"[GET_TASK_STATUS] Direct query failed for {task_id}: {e}")
   249→
   250→    return None
   251→
   252→def cancel_orchestrator_children(orchestrator_task_id: str, reason: str = "Orchestrator cancelled") -> int:
   253→    """
   254→    Cancel all non-terminal child tasks of an orchestrator.
   255→
   256→    Fetches all children via get_orchestrator_child_tasks(), then sets any that are
   257→    still in a non-terminal state (Queued, In Progress) to "Cancelled" via update-task-status.
   258→
   259→    Args:
   260→        orchestrator_task_id: The orchestrator task ID whose children should be cancelled.
   261→        reason: Human-readable reason stored in output_location.
   262→
   263→    Returns:
   264→        Number of child tasks that were cancelled.
   265→    """
   266→    TERMINAL_STATUSES = {'complete', 'failed', 'cancelled', 'canceled', 'error'}
   267→
   268→    child_tasks = get_orchestrator_child_tasks(orchestrator_task_id)
   269→
   270→    # Flatten all child task categories into a single list
   271→    all_children = []
   272→    for category in child_tasks.values():
   273→        if isinstance(category, list):
   274→            all_children.extend(category)
   275→
   276→    if not all_children:
   277→        headless_logger.debug(f"[CANCEL_CHILDREN] No child tasks found for orchestrator {orchestrator_task_id}")
   278→        return 0
   279→
   280→    cancelled_count = 0
   281→    for child in all_children:
   282→        child_id = child.get('id')
   283→        child_status = (child.get('status') or '').lower()
   284→
   285→        if child_status in TERMINAL_STATUSES:
   286→            headless_logger.debug(f"[CANCEL_CHILDREN] Skipping child {child_id} (already {child_status})")
   287→            continue
   288→
   289→        headless_logger.debug(f"[CANCEL_CHILDREN] Cancelling child task {child_id} (was {child_status})")
   290→        try:
   291→            update_task_status(child_id, "Cancelled", output_location=reason)
   292→            cancelled_count += 1
   293→        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   294→            headless_logger.error(f"[CANCEL_CHILDREN] Failed to cancel child {child_id}: {e}", task_id=child_id)
   295→
   296→    if cancelled_count > 0:
   297→        headless_logger.essential(f"[CANCEL_CHILDREN] Cancelled {cancelled_count}/{len(all_children)} child tasks for orchestrator {orchestrator_task_id}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id)
   298→    else:
   299→        headless_logger.debug(f"[CANCEL_CHILDREN] All {len(all_children)} children already in terminal state for orchestrator {orchestrator_task_id}")
   300→
   301→    return cancelled_count
   302→
   303→def cleanup_duplicate_child_tasks(orchestrator_task_id: str, expected_segments: int) -> dict:
   304→    """
   305→    Detects and removes duplicate child tasks for an orchestrator.
   306→    Returns summary of cleanup actions.
   307→    """
   308→    child_tasks = get_orchestrator_child_tasks(orchestrator_task_id)
   309→    segments = child_tasks['segments']
   310→    stitch_tasks = child_tasks['stitch']
   311→
   312→    cleanup_summary = {
   313→        'duplicate_segments_removed': 0,
   314→        'duplicate_stitch_removed': 0,
   315→        'errors': []
   316→    }
   317→
   318→    try:
   319→        # Remove duplicate segments (keep the oldest for each segment_index)
   320→        segment_by_index = {}
   321→        for segment in segments:
   322→            segment_idx = segment['params'].get('segment_index', -1)
   323→            if segment_idx in segment_by_index:
   324→                # We have a duplicate - keep the older one (first created)
   325→                existing = segment_by_index[segment_idx]
   326→                duplicate_id = segment['id']
   327→
   328→                headless_logger.debug(f"[IDEMPOTENCY] Found duplicate segment {segment_idx}: keeping {existing['id']}, removing {duplicate_id}")
   329→
   330→                # Remove the duplicate
   331→                if _delete_task_by_id(duplicate_id):
   332→                    cleanup_summary['duplicate_segments_removed'] += 1
   333→                else:
   334→                    cleanup_summary['errors'].append(f"Failed to delete duplicate segment {duplicate_id}")
   335→            else:
   336→                segment_by_index[segment_idx] = segment
   337→
   338→        # Remove duplicate stitch tasks (should only be 1)
   339→        if len(stitch_tasks) > 1:
   340→            # Keep the oldest stitch task, remove others
   341→            stitch_sorted = sorted(stitch_tasks, key=lambda x: x.get('created_at', ''))
   342→            for stitch in stitch_sorted[1:]:  # Remove all but first
   343→                duplicate_id = stitch['id']
   344→                headless_logger.debug(f"[IDEMPOTENCY] Found duplicate stitch task: removing {duplicate_id}")
   345→
   346→                if _delete_task_by_id(duplicate_id):
   347→                    cleanup_summary['duplicate_stitch_removed'] += 1
   348→                else:
   349→                    cleanup_summary['errors'].append(f"Failed to delete duplicate stitch {duplicate_id}")
   350→
   351→    except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
   352→        cleanup_summary['errors'].append(f"Cleanup error: {str(e)}")
   353→        headless_logger.debug(f"Error during duplicate cleanup: {e}")
   354→        traceback.print_exc()
   355→
   356→    return cleanup_summary
   357→
   358→def _delete_task_by_id(task_id: str) -> bool:
   359→    """Helper to delete a task by ID from Supabase. Returns True if successful."""
   360→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   361→        headless_logger.error(f"Supabase client not initialized. Cannot delete task {task_id}", task_id=task_id)
   362→        return False
   363→
   364→    try:
   365→        response = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).delete().eq("id", task_id).execute()
   366→        return len(response.data) > 0 if response.data else False
   367→    except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   368→        headless_logger.debug(f"Error deleting Supabase task {task_id}: {e}")
   369→        return False
   370→
   371→def get_predecessor_output_via_edge_function(task_id: str) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
   372→    """
   373→    Gets both the predecessor task ID and its output location using Supabase Edge Function.
   374→
   375→    Args:
   376→        task_id: Task ID to get predecessor for
   377→
   378→    Returns:
   379→        (predecessor_id, output_location) or (None, None) if no dependency or error
   380→    """
   381→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_URL or not _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   382→        headless_logger.error("Supabase configuration incomplete. Falling back to direct queries.", task_id=task_id)
   383→        predecessor_id = get_task_dependency(task_id)
   384→        if predecessor_id:
   385→            output_location = get_task_output_location_from_db(predecessor_id)
   386→            return predecessor_id, output_location
   387→        return None, None
   388→
   389→    edge_url = f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/get-predecessor-output"
   390→
   391→    try:
   392→        headless_logger.debug(f"Calling Edge Function: {edge_url} for task {task_id}")
   393→        headers = {
   394→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   395→            'Authorization': f'Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}'
   396→        }
   397→
   398→        resp = httpx.post(edge_url, json={"task_id": task_id}, headers=headers, timeout=15)
   399→        headless_logger.debug(f"Edge Function response status: {resp.status_code}")
   400→
   401→        if resp.status_code == 200:
   402→            result = resp.json()
   403→            headless_logger.debug(f"Edge Function result: {result}")
   404→
   405→            if result is None:
   406→                # No dependency
   407→                return None, None
   408→
   409→            predecessor_id = result.get("predecessor_id")
   410→            output_location = result.get("output_location")
   411→            return predecessor_id, output_location
   412→
   413→        elif resp.status_code == 404:
   414→            headless_logger.debug(f"Edge Function: Task {task_id} not found")
   415→            return None, None
   416→        else:
   417→            headless_logger.debug(f"Edge Function returned {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}. Falling back to direct queries.")
   418→            # Fall back to separate calls
   419→            predecessor_id = get_task_dependency(task_id)
   420→            if predecessor_id:
   421→                output_location = get_task_output_location_from_db(predecessor_id)
   422→                return predecessor_id, output_location
   423→            return None, None
   424→
   425→    except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e_edge:
   426→        headless_logger.debug(f"Edge Function call failed: {e_edge}. Falling back to direct queries.")
   427→        # Fall back to separate calls
   428→        predecessor_id = get_task_dependency(task_id)
   429→        if predecessor_id:
   430→            output_location = get_task_output_location_from_db(predecessor_id)
   431→            return predecessor_id, output_location
   432→        return None, None
   433→
   434→def get_completed_segment_outputs_for_stitch(run_id: str, project_id: str | None = None) -> list:
   435→    """Gets completed travel_segment outputs for a given run_id for stitching from Supabase."""
   436→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_URL or not _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   437→        headless_logger.error("Supabase configuration incomplete. Cannot get completed segments.")
   438→        return []
   439→
   440→    edge_url = f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/get-completed-segments"
   441→    try:
   442→        headless_logger.debug(f"Calling Edge Function: {edge_url} for run_id {run_id}, project_id {project_id}")
   443→        headers = {
   444→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   445→            'Authorization': f'Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}'
   446→        }
   447→        payload = {"run_id": run_id}
   448→        if project_id:
   449→            payload["project_id"] = project_id
   450→
   451→        resp = httpx.post(edge_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=15)
   452→        if resp.status_code == 200:
   453→            results = resp.json()
   454→            sorted_results = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['segment_index'])
   455→            return [(r['segment_index'], r['output_location']) for r in sorted_results]
   456→        else:
   457→            headless_logger.debug(f"Edge Function returned {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}. Falling back to direct query.")
   458→    except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   459→        headless_logger.debug(f"Edge Function failed: {e}. Falling back to direct query.")
   460→
   461→    # Fallback to direct query
   462→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   463→        headless_logger.error("Supabase client not initialized. Cannot fall back to direct query.")
   464→        return []
   465→
   466→    try:
   467→        # First, let's debug by getting ALL completed tasks to see what's there
   468→        debug_resp = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).select("id, task_type, status, params, output_location")\
   469→            .eq("status", STATUS_COMPLETE).execute()
   470→
   471→        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] Looking for run_id: '{run_id}' (type: {type(run_id)})")
   472→        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] Total completed tasks in DB: {len(debug_resp.data) if debug_resp.data else 0}")
   473→
   474→        travel_segment_count = 0
   475→        matching_run_id_count = 0
   476→
   477→        if debug_resp.data:
   478→            for task in debug_resp.data:
   479→                task_type = task.get("task_type", "")
   480→                if task_type == "travel_segment":
   481→                    travel_segment_count += 1
   482→                    params_raw = task.get("params", {})
   483→                    try:
   484→                        params_obj = params_raw if isinstance(params_raw, dict) else json.loads(params_raw)
   485→                        task_run_id = params_obj.get("orchestrator_run_id")
   486→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] Found travel_segment task {task.get('id')}: orchestrator_run_id='{task_run_id}' (type: {type(task_run_id)}), segment_index={params_obj.get('segment_index')}, output_location={task.get('output_location', 'None')}")
   487→
   488→                        if str(task_run_id) == str(run_id):
   489→                            matching_run_id_count += 1
   490→                            headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] MATCH FOUND! Task {task.get('id')} matches run_id {run_id}")
   491→                    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as e_debug:
   492→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] Error parsing params for task {task.get('id')}: {e_debug}")
   493→
   494→        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] Travel_segment tasks found: {travel_segment_count}")
   495→        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] Tasks matching run_id '{run_id}': {matching_run_id_count}")
   496→
   497→        # Now do the actual query
   498→        sel_resp = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).select("params, output_location")\
   499→            .eq("task_type", "travel_segment").eq("status", STATUS_COMPLETE).execute()
   500→
   501→        results = []
   502→        if sel_resp.data:
   503→            for i, row in enumerate(sel_resp.data):
   504→                params_raw = row.get("params")
   505→                if params_raw is None:
   506→                    continue
   507→                try:
   508→                    params_obj = params_raw if isinstance(params_raw, dict) else json.loads(params_raw)
   509→                except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
   510→                    continue
   511→
   512→                row_run_id = params_obj.get("orchestrator_run_id")
   513→
   514→                # Use string comparison to handle type mismatches
   515→                if str(row_run_id) == str(run_id):
   516→                    seg_idx = params_obj.get("segment_index")
   517→                    output_loc = row.get("output_location")
   518→                    results.append((seg_idx, output_loc))
   519→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] Added to results: segment_index={seg_idx}, output_location={output_loc}")
   520→
   521→        sorted_results = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x[0] if x[0] is not None else 0)
   522→        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG_STITCH] Final sorted results: {sorted_results}")
   523→        return sorted_results
   524→    except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e_sel:
   525→        headless_logger.debug(f"Stitch Supabase: Direct select failed: {e_sel}")
   526→        traceback.print_exc()
   527→        return []
   528→

1→"""
     2→Task claiming and assignment recovery functions.
     3→"""
     4→import os
     5→import sys
     6→import traceback
     7→
     8→import httpx
     9→from postgrest.exceptions import APIError
    10→
    11→from source.core.log import headless_logger
    12→
    13→__all__ = [
    14→    "init_db",
    15→    "init_db_supabase",
    16→    "check_task_counts_supabase",
    17→    "check_my_assigned_tasks",
    18→    "get_oldest_queued_task",
    19→    "get_oldest_queued_task_supabase",
    20→]
    21→
    22→from . import config as _cfg
    23→from .config import (
    24→    STATUS_IN_PROGRESS)
    25→
    26→def init_db():
    27→    """Initializes the Supabase database connection."""
    28→    return init_db_supabase()
    29→
    30→def init_db_supabase():
    31→    """Check if the Supabase tasks table exists and is accessible."""
    32→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
    33→        headless_logger.error("Supabase client not initialized. Cannot check database table.")
    34→        sys.exit(1)
    35→    try:
    36→        # Simply check if the tasks table exists by querying it
    37→        result = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).select("count", count="exact").limit(1).execute()
    38→        headless_logger.essential(f"Supabase: Table '{_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME}' exists and accessible (count: {result.count})")
    39→        return True
    40→    except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
    41→        headless_logger.error(f"Supabase table check failed: {e}")
    42→        # Don't exit - the table might exist but have different permissions
    43→        # Let the actual operations try and fail gracefully
    44→        return False
    45→
    46→def check_task_counts_supabase(run_type: str = "gpu") -> dict | None:
    47→    """Check task counts via Supabase Edge Function before attempting to claim tasks."""
    48→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT or not _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
    49→        headless_logger.error("[TASK_COUNTS] Supabase client or access token not initialized")
    50→        return None
    51→
    52→    # Build task-counts edge function URL using same pattern as other functions
    53→    edge_url = (
    54→        os.getenv('SUPABASE_EDGE_TASK_COUNTS_URL')
    55→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/task-counts" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
    56→    )
    57→
    58→    if not edge_url:
    59→        headless_logger.error("[TASK_COUNTS] No edge function URL available")
    60→        return None
    61→
    62→    try:
    63→        # Use same authentication pattern as other edge functions - SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN
    64→        # This can be a service key, PAT, or JWT - the edge function determines the type
    65→        headers = {
    66→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    67→            'Authorization': f'Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}'
    68→        }
    69→
    70→        payload = {
    71→            "run_type": run_type,
    72→            "include_active": True
    73→        }
    74→
    75→        headless_logger.debug(f"DEBUG check_task_counts_supabase: Calling task-counts at {edge_url}")
    76→        resp = httpx.post(edge_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=10)
    77→        headless_logger.debug(f"Task-counts response status: {resp.status_code}")
    78→
    79→        if resp.status_code == 200:
    80→            counts_data = resp.json()
    81→            # Always log a concise summary so we can observe behavior without enabling debug
    82→            try:
    83→                totals = counts_data.get('totals', {})
    84→                headless_logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNTS] totals={totals} run_type={payload.get('run_type')}")
    85→            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    86→                # Fall back to raw text if JSON structure unexpected
    87→                headless_logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNTS] raw_response={resp.text[:500]}")
    88→            headless_logger.debug(f"Task-counts result: {counts_data.get('totals', {})}")
    89→            return counts_data
    90→        else:
    91→            headless_logger.error(f"[TASK_COUNTS] Edge function returned {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:500]}")
    92→            return None
    93→
    94→    except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e_counts:
    95→        headless_logger.error(f"[TASK_COUNTS] Call failed: {e_counts}")
    96→        return None
    97→
    98→def check_my_assigned_tasks(worker_id: str) -> dict | None:
    99→    """
   100→    Check if this worker has any tasks already assigned to it (In Progress).
   101→    This handles the case where a claim succeeded but the response was lost.
   102→
   103→    Returns task data dict if found, None otherwise.
   104→    """
   105→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT or not worker_id:
   106→        return None
   107→
   108→    try:
   109→        # Query for tasks assigned to this worker that are In Progress
   110→        result = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table('tasks') \
   111→            .select('id, params, task_type, project_id') \
   112→            .eq('worker_id', worker_id) \
   113→            .eq('status', STATUS_IN_PROGRESS) \
   114→            .limit(1) \
   115→            .execute()
   116→
   117→        if result.data and len(result.data) > 0:
   118→            task = result.data[0]
   119→            task_id = task.get('id')
   120→            task_type = task.get('task_type', 'unknown')
   121→            params = task.get('params', {})
   122→
   123→            headless_logger.essential(f"[RECOVERY] Found assigned task {task_id} (type={task_type}) - recovering", task_id=task_id)
   124→            headless_logger.debug(f"[RECOVERY_DEBUG] Task was assigned to us but we didn't process it - likely lost HTTP response")
   125→
   126→            # Return in the same format as claim-next-task Edge Function
   127→            return {
   128→                'task_id': task_id,
   129→                'params': params,
   130→                'task_type': task_type,
   131→                'project_id': task.get('project_id')
   132→            }
   133→
   134→        return None
   135→
   136→    except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   137→        # Don't let recovery check failures block normal operation
   138→        headless_logger.debug(f"[RECOVERY] Failed to check assigned tasks: {e}")
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→def _orchestrator_has_incomplete_children(orchestrator_task_id: str) -> bool:
   142→    """
   143→    Check if an orchestrator has child tasks that are not yet complete.
   144→    Used to prevent re-running orchestrators that are waiting for children.
   145→    """
   146→    def _check_children(children_data: list) -> bool:
   147→        """Check if any child is not complete."""
   148→        for child in children_data:
   149→            status = (child.get("status") or "").lower()
   150→            if status not in ("complete", "failed", "cancelled", "canceled", "error"):
   151→                headless_logger.debug(f"[RECOVERY_CHECK] Orchestrator {orchestrator_task_id} has incomplete child {child['id']} (status={status})")
   152→                return True
   153→        return False
   154→
   155→    # Try edge function first (works for local workers without service key)
   156→    edge_url = (
   157→        os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_GET_ORCHESTRATOR_CHILDREN_URL")
   158→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/get-orchestrator-children" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   159→    )
   160→
   161→    if edge_url and _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   162→        headers = {
   163→            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   164→            "Authorization": f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   165→        }
   166→        payload = {"orchestrator_task_id": orchestrator_task_id}
   167→
   168→        try:
   169→            resp = httpx.post(edge_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=30)
   170→            if resp.status_code == 200:
   171→                data = resp.json()
   172→                tasks = data.get("tasks", [])
   173→                if not tasks:
   174→                    return False  # No children found
   175→                return _check_children(tasks)
   176→        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   177→            headless_logger.debug(f"[RECOVERY_CHECK] Edge function failed: {e}")
   178→
   179→    # Fallback to direct query if edge function not available or failed
   180→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   181→        return False  # Can't check, assume no children
   182→
   183→    try:
   184→        response = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME)\
   185→            .select("id, status")\
   186→            .contains("params", {"orchestrator_task_id_ref": orchestrator_task_id})\
   187→            .execute()
   188→
   189→        if not response.data:
   190→            return False  # No children found
   191→
   192→        return _check_children(response.data)
   193→
   194→    except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   195→        headless_logger.debug(f"[RECOVERY_CHECK] Failed to check orchestrator children: {e}")
   196→        return False  # Can't check, don't block
   197→
   198→def get_oldest_queued_task():
   199→    """Gets the oldest queued task from Supabase."""
   200→    return get_oldest_queued_task_supabase()
   201→
   202→def get_oldest_queued_task_supabase(worker_id: str = None):
   203→    """Fetches the oldest task via Supabase Edge Function. First checks task counts to avoid unnecessary claim attempts."""
   204→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   205→        headless_logger.error("Supabase client not initialized. Cannot get task.")
   206→        return None
   207→
   208→    # Worker ID is required
   209→    if not worker_id:
   210→        headless_logger.error("No worker_id provided to get_oldest_queued_task_supabase")
   211→        return None
   212→
   213→    headless_logger.debug(f"DEBUG: Using worker_id: {worker_id}")
   214→
   215→    # =========================================================================
   216→    # RECOVERY (but don't starve queued tasks)
   217→    #
   218→    # We *used* to immediately return an In Progress task assigned to this worker,
   219→    # assuming a claim succeeded but the HTTP response was lost.
   220→    #
   221→    # That behavior can create a tight loop for orchestrator tasks:
   222→    # - Orchestrator stays "In Progress" while waiting for child tasks
   223→    # - Recovery keeps returning the orchestrator every cycle
   224→    # - Worker never claims queued child tasks => orchestrator can never finish
   225→    #
   226→    # Fix: only prioritize recovery for non-orchestrator tasks.
   227→    # For orchestrators, we defer recovery until AFTER attempting to claim queued work.
   228→    # =========================================================================
   229→    assigned_task = check_my_assigned_tasks(worker_id)
   230→    deferred_orchestrator_recovery = None
   231→    if assigned_task:
   232→        try:
   233→            ttype = (assigned_task.get("task_type") or "").lower()
   234→        except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   235→            ttype = ""
   236→        if ttype.endswith("_orchestrator"):
   237→            deferred_orchestrator_recovery = assigned_task
   238→            headless_logger.debug(f"[RECOVERY] Deferring orchestrator recovery for {assigned_task.get('task_id')} to avoid starving queued tasks")
   239→        else:
   240→            return assigned_task
   241→
   242→    # =========================================================================
   243→    # STEP 2: No assigned tasks - check for new tasks to claim
   244→    # =========================================================================
   245→
   246→    # OPTIMIZATION: Check task counts first to avoid unnecessary claim attempts
   247→    headless_logger.debug("Checking task counts before attempting to claim...")
   248→    task_counts = check_task_counts_supabase("gpu")
   249→
   250→    if task_counts is None:
   251→        headless_logger.debug("WARNING: Could not check task counts, proceeding with direct claim attempt")
   252→    else:
   253→        totals = task_counts.get('totals', {})
   254→        # Gate claim by queued_only to avoid claiming when only active tasks exist
   255→        available_tasks = totals.get('queued_only', 0)
   256→        eligible_queued = totals.get('eligible_queued', 0)
   257→        active_only = totals.get('active_only', 0)
   258→
   259→        headless_logger.debug(f"[CLAIM_DEBUG] Task counts: queued_only={available_tasks}, eligible_queued={eligible_queued}, active_only={active_only}")
   260→
   261→        # Log warning if counts are inconsistent
   262→        if eligible_queued > 0 and available_tasks == 0:
   263→            headless_logger.warning(f"Task count inconsistency detected: eligible_queued={eligible_queued} but queued_only={available_tasks}")
   264→            headless_logger.warning(f"This suggests tasks exist but aren't visible as 'Queued' status - possible replication lag or status corruption")
   265→            # Proceed with claim attempt despite queued_only=0 since eligible_queued>0
   266→            headless_logger.debug(f"[CLAIM_DEBUG] Proceeding with claim attempt despite queued_only=0 because eligible_queued={eligible_queued}")
   267→        elif available_tasks <= 0:
   268→            headless_logger.debug("No queued tasks according to task-counts, skipping claim attempt")
   269→            # If we deferred an orchestrator recovery, check if it actually needs re-running.
   270→            # Orchestrators waiting for children should NOT be recovered - they'll complete
   271→            # when their children complete (via complete-task orchestrator check).
   272→            if deferred_orchestrator_recovery:
   273→                orch_task_id = deferred_orchestrator_recovery.get("task_id")
   274→                if orch_task_id and _orchestrator_has_incomplete_children(orch_task_id):
   275→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[RECOVERY] Skipping orchestrator {orch_task_id} - has incomplete children, will complete via child completion")
   276→                    return None
   277→                return deferred_orchestrator_recovery
   278→            return None
   279→        else:
   280→            headless_logger.debug(f"Found {available_tasks} queued tasks, proceeding with claim")
   281→
   282→    # Use Edge Function exclusively
   283→    edge_url = (
   284→        _cfg.SUPABASE_EDGE_CLAIM_TASK_URL
   285→        or os.getenv('SUPABASE_EDGE_CLAIM_TASK_URL')
   286→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/claim-next-task" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   287→    )
   288→
   289→    if edge_url and _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   290→        try:
   291→            headless_logger.debug(f"DEBUG get_oldest_queued_task_supabase: Calling Edge Function at {edge_url}")
   292→            headless_logger.debug(f"DEBUG: Using worker_id: {worker_id}")
   293→
   294→            headers = {
   295→                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   296→                'Authorization': f'Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}'
   297→            }
   298→
   299→            # Pass worker_id and run_type in the request body for edge function to use
   300→            payload = {"worker_id": worker_id, "run_type": "gpu"}
   301→
   302→            resp = httpx.post(edge_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=15)
   303→            headless_logger.debug(f"Edge Function response status: {resp.status_code}")
   304→
   305→            if resp.status_code == 200:
   306→                task_data = resp.json()
   307→                task_id = task_data.get('task_id', 'unknown')
   308→                task_type = task_data.get('task_type', 'unknown')
   309→                params = task_data.get('params', {})
   310→                segment_index = params.get('segment_index') if isinstance(params, dict) else None
   311→
   312→                headless_logger.essential(f"[CLAIM] Claimed task {task_id} (type={task_type}, segment_index={segment_index})", task_id=task_id)
   313→                headless_logger.debug(f"[CLAIM_DEBUG] Full task data: {task_data}")
   314→                return task_data  # Already in the expected format
   315→            elif resp.status_code == 204:
   316→                headless_logger.debug("Edge Function: No queued tasks available")
   317→                # If no queued tasks are claimable right now, fall back to any
   318→                # deferred orchestrator recovery (lost-response protection).
   319→                if deferred_orchestrator_recovery:
   320→                    return deferred_orchestrator_recovery
   321→                return None
   322→            else:
   323→                headless_logger.error(f"[CLAIM] Edge Function returned {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:500]}")
   324→                if deferred_orchestrator_recovery:
   325→                    return deferred_orchestrator_recovery
   326→                return None
   327→        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e_edge:
   328→            # Log visibly - this is a critical failure that can cause orphaned tasks
   329→            headless_logger.error(f"[CLAIM] Edge Function call failed: {e_edge}")
   330→            headless_logger.debug(f"[CLAIM_DEBUG] Exception type: {type(e_edge).__name__}")
   331→            headless_logger.debug(f"[CLAIM_DEBUG] Full traceback: {traceback.format_exc()}")
   332→            if deferred_orchestrator_recovery:
   333→                return deferred_orchestrator_recovery
   334→            return None
   335→    else:
   336→        headless_logger.error("[CLAIM] No edge function URL or access token available for task claiming")
   337→        if deferred_orchestrator_recovery:
   338→            return deferred_orchestrator_recovery
   339→        return None
   340→
```

    32→def _extract_video_thumbnail(video_path: Path, task_id_str: str) -> Path | None:
    33→    """Extract a thumbnail from a video file.
    34→
    35→    Checks for an existing poster (.jpg alongside the video) first.
    36→    Falls back to extracting the first frame via OpenCV.
    37→
    38→    Returns:
    39→        Path to the thumbnail file (caller must clean up temp files), or None.
    40→        If the returned path equals ``video_path.with_suffix('.jpg')``, it is
    41→        an existing file and should NOT be deleted by the caller.
    42→    """
    43→    import tempfile
    44→    try:
    45→        from ...utils import save_frame_from_video
    46→        import cv2
    47→    except ImportError:
    48→        _log_thumbnail("Cannot import cv2/save_frame_from_video for thumbnail extraction",
    49→                        level="warning", task_id=task_id_str)
    50→        return None
    51→
    52→    # Check for existing poster alongside the video
    53→    existing_poster_path = video_path.with_suffix('.jpg')
    54→    if existing_poster_path.exists():
    55→        _log_thumbnail(f"Found existing poster: {existing_poster_path.name}", level="info", task_id=task_id_str)
    56→        return existing_poster_path
    57→
    58→    # Extract first frame from video
    59→    _log_thumbnail(f"Extracting first frame from video {video_path.name}", task_id=task_id_str)
    60→    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.jpg', delete=False) as temp_frame:
    61→        temp_frame_path = Path(temp_frame.name)
    62→
    63→    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_path))
    64→    if not cap.isOpened():
    65→        _log_thumbnail(f"Could not open video with cv2: {video_path}", level="warning", task_id=task_id_str)
    66→        temp_frame_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    67→        return None
    68→
    69→    width = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
    70→    height = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
    71→    cap.release()
    72→
    73→    if save_frame_from_video(video_path, 0, temp_frame_path, (width, height)):
    74→        _log_thumbnail("First frame extracted successfully", level="info", task_id=task_id_str)
    75→        return temp_frame_path
    76→
    77→    _log_thumbnail(f"save_frame_from_video failed for {video_path.name}", level="warning", task_id=task_id_str)
    78→    temp_frame_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    79→    return None

   118→def requeue_task_for_retry(task_id_str: str, error_message: str, current_attempts: int, error_category: str = None) -> bool:
   119→    """
   120→    Reset a task to Queued status for retry, incrementing the attempts counter.
   121→
   122→    This is used for transient errors (OOM, edge function failures, etc.) that may
   123→    succeed on a subsequent attempt.
   124→
   125→    Args:
   126→        task_id_str: Task ID to requeue
   127→        error_message: Error message from the failed attempt
   128→        current_attempts: Current attempt count (will be incremented)
   129→        error_category: Optional category of the retryable error
   130→
   131→    Returns:
   132→        True if task was successfully requeued, False otherwise
   133→    """
   134→    new_attempts = current_attempts + 1
   135→
   136→    # Build error details message
   137→    error_details = f"Retry {new_attempts}"
   138→    if error_category:
   139→        error_details += f" ({error_category})"
   140→    error_details += f": {error_message[:500]}" if error_message else ""
   141→
   142→    headless_logger.essential(f"Requeuing task {task_id_str} for retry (attempt {new_attempts})", task_id=task_id_str)
   143→    headless_logger.debug(f"[RETRY_DEBUG] Error category: {error_category}, Error: {error_message[:200] if error_message else 'N/A'}...")
   144→
   145→    # Use edge function to update status back to Queued
   146→    edge_url = (
   147→        os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_UPDATE_TASK_URL")
   148→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/update-task-status" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   149→    )
   150→
   151→    if not edge_url:
   152→        headless_logger.error(f"No update-task-status edge function URL available for requeuing task {task_id_str}", task_id=task_id_str)
   153→        # Fallback to direct DB update
   154→        return _requeue_task_direct_db(task_id_str, new_attempts, error_details)
   155→
   156→    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   157→    if _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   158→        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   159→
   160→    payload = {
   161→        "task_id": task_id_str,
   162→        "status": STATUS_QUEUED,
   163→        "attempts": new_attempts,
   164→        "error_details": error_details,
   165→        "clear_worker": True,  # Signal to clear worker_id assignment
   166→    }
   167→
   168→    resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   169→        edge_url=edge_url,
   170→        payload=payload,
   171→        headers=headers,
   172→        function_name="update-task-status",
   173→        context_id=task_id_str,
   174→        timeout=30,
   175→        max_retries=3)
   176→
   177→    if resp and resp.status_code == 200:
   178→        headless_logger.essential(f"Task {task_id_str} requeued for retry (attempt {new_attempts})", task_id=task_id_str)
   179→        return True
   180→    elif edge_error:
   181→        headless_logger.error(f"Failed to requeue task {task_id_str}: {edge_error}", task_id=task_id_str)
   182→        # Fallback to direct DB update
   183→        return _requeue_task_direct_db(task_id_str, new_attempts, error_details)
   184→    elif resp:
   185→        headless_logger.error(f"Failed to requeue task {task_id_str}: {resp.status_code} - {resp.text}", task_id=task_id_str)
   186→        # Fallback to direct DB update
   187→        return _requeue_task_direct_db(task_id_str, new_attempts, error_details)
   188→
   189→    return False
   190→
   191→def _requeue_task_direct_db(task_id_str: str, new_attempts: int, error_details: str) -> bool:
   192→    """
   193→    Fallback: Requeue task directly via Supabase client if edge function fails.
   194→    """
   195→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   196→        headless_logger.error(f"No Supabase client available for direct DB requeue of task {task_id_str}", task_id=task_id_str)
   197→        return False
   198→
   199→    try:
   200→        result = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).update({
   201→            "status": STATUS_QUEUED,
   202→            "worker_id": None,
   203→            "attempts": new_attempts,
   204→            "error_details": error_details,
   205→            "generation_started_at": None,
   206→        }).eq("id", task_id_str).execute()
   207→
   208→        if result.data:
   209→            headless_logger.essential(f"Task {task_id_str} requeued via direct DB (attempt {new_attempts})", task_id=task_id_str)
   210→            return True
   211→        else:
   212→            headless_logger.error(f"Direct DB requeue returned no data for task {task_id_str}", task_id=task_id_str)
   213→            return False
   214→    except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   215→        headless_logger.error(f"Direct DB requeue failed for task {task_id_str}: {e}", task_id=task_id_str)
   216→        return False
   217→
   218→def update_task_status(task_id: str, status: str, output_location: str | None = None):
   219→    """Updates a task's status in Supabase."""
   220→    headless_logger.debug(f"[UPDATE_TASK_STATUS_DEBUG] Called with:")
   221→    headless_logger.debug(f"[UPDATE_TASK_STATUS_DEBUG]   task_id: '{task_id}'")
   222→    headless_logger.debug(f"[UPDATE_TASK_STATUS_DEBUG]   status: '{status}'")
   223→    headless_logger.debug(f"[UPDATE_TASK_STATUS_DEBUG]   output_location: '{output_location}'")
   224→
   225→    try:

   227→        result = update_task_status_supabase(task_id, status, output_location)
   228→        headless_logger.debug(f"[UPDATE_TASK_STATUS_DEBUG] update_task_status_supabase completed successfully")
   229→        return result
   230→    except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   231→        headless_logger.debug(f"[UPDATE_TASK_STATUS_DEBUG] \u274c Exception in update_task_status: {e}")
   232→        headless_logger.debug(f"[UPDATE_TASK_STATUS_DEBUG] Exception type: {type(e).__name__}")
   233→        traceback.print_exc()
   234→        raise
   235→
   236→def update_task_status_supabase(task_id_str, status_str, output_location_val=None, thumbnail_url_val=None):
   237→    """Updates a task's status via Supabase Edge Functions.
   238→
   239→    Args:
   240→        task_id_str: Task ID
   241→        status_str: Status to set
   242→        output_location_val: Output file location or URL
   243→        thumbnail_url_val: Optional thumbnail URL to pass to edge function
   244→    """
   245→    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] update_task_status_supabase called: task_id={task_id_str}, status={status_str}, output_location={output_location_val}, thumbnail={thumbnail_url_val}")
   246→
   247→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   248→        headless_logger.error("Supabase client not initialized. Cannot update task status.", task_id=task_id_str)
   249→        return
   250→
   251→    # --- Use edge functions for ALL status updates ---
   252→    if status_str == STATUS_COMPLETE and output_location_val is not None:
   253→        # Use completion edge function for completion with file
   254→        # NOTE: Canonical deployed edge function is `complete_task` (underscore).
   255→        edge_url = (
   256→            _cfg.SUPABASE_EDGE_COMPLETE_TASK_URL
   257→            or (os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_COMPLETE_TASK_URL") or None)
   258→            or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/complete_task" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   259→        )
   260→
   261→        if not edge_url:
   262→            headless_logger.error(f"No complete_task edge function URL available", task_id=task_id_str)
   263→            return
   264→
   265→        try:
   266→            # Check if output_location_val is a local file path
   267→            output_path = Path(output_location_val)
   268→
   269→            if output_path.exists() and output_path.is_file():
   270→                import base64
   271→                import mimetypes
   272→
   273→                # Get file size for logging
   274→                file_size = output_path.stat().st_size
   275→                file_size_mb = file_size / BYTES_PER_MB
   276→                headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] File size: {file_size_mb:.2f} MB")
   277→
   278→                headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   279→                if _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   280→                    headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   281→
   282→                # Check if this is a video file that needs thumbnail extraction
   283→                from source.core.constants import VIDEO_EXTENSIONS
   284→                is_video = output_path.suffix.lower() in VIDEO_EXTENSIONS
   285→                _log_thumbnail(f"File: {output_path.name}, suffix: {output_path.suffix.lower()}, is_video: {is_video}", task_id=task_id_str)
   286→
   287→                # Use base64 encoding for files under 2MB (MODE 1), presigned URLs for larger files (MODE 3)
   288→                FILE_SIZE_THRESHOLD_MB = 2.0
   289→                use_base64 = file_size_mb < FILE_SIZE_THRESHOLD_MB
   290→
   291→                if use_base64:
   292→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Using base64 upload for {output_path.name} ({file_size_mb:.2f} MB < {FILE_SIZE_THRESHOLD_MB} MB)")
   293→
   294→                    # MODE 1: Base64 upload (for files under 2MB)
   295→                    # Read and encode file
   296→                    with open(output_path, 'rb') as f:
   297→                        file_bytes = f.read()
   298→                        file_data_base64 = base64.b64encode(file_bytes).decode('utf-8')
   299→
   300→                    payload = {
   301→                        "task_id": task_id_str,
   302→                        "file_data": file_data_base64,
   303→                        "filename": output_path.name
   304→                    }
   305→
   306→                    # Extract and encode first frame for videos
   307→                    if is_video:
   308→                        try:
   309→                            thumb_path = _extract_video_thumbnail(output_path, task_id_str)
   310→                            if thumb_path:
   311→                                with open(thumb_path, 'rb') as thumb_file:
   312→                                    first_frame_base64 = base64.b64encode(thumb_file.read()).decode('utf-8')
   313→                                payload["first_frame_data"] = first_frame_base64
   314→                                payload["first_frame_filename"] = f"thumb_{task_id_str[:8]}.jpg"
   315→                                # Clean up temp file (but not existing poster)
   316→                                if thumb_path != output_path.with_suffix('.jpg'):
   317→                                    thumb_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   318→                        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   319→                            _log_thumbnail(f"Exception during thumbnail extraction: {e}", level="warning", task_id=task_id_str)
   320→                            # Continue without thumbnail
   321→
   322→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Calling complete_task Edge Function with base64 data for task {task_id_str}")
   323→                    resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   324→                        edge_url=edge_url,
   325→                        payload=payload,
   326→                        headers=headers,
   327→                        function_name="complete_task",
   328→                        context_id=task_id_str,
   329→                        timeout=60,
   330→                        max_retries=3,
   331→                        fallback_url=None,
   332→                        retry_on_404_patterns=["Task not found", "not found"])
   333→
   334→                    if resp is not None and resp.status_code == 200:
   335→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Edge function SUCCESS for task {task_id_str} \u2192 status COMPLETE with base64 upload")
   336→                        # Parse response to get storage URL and thumbnail URL
   337→                        try:
   338→                            resp_data = resp.json()
   339→                            storage_url = resp_data.get('public_url')
   340→                            thumbnail_url = resp_data.get('thumbnail_url')  # Also get thumbnail
   341→                            if storage_url:
   342→                                headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] File uploaded to: {storage_url}")
   343→                                if thumbnail_url:
   344→                                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Thumbnail available at: {thumbnail_url}")
   345→                                # Return both URLs as a dict
   346→                                return {'public_url': storage_url, 'thumbnail_url': thumbnail_url}
   347→                        except (ValueError, KeyError) as e:
   348→                            headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Failed to parse complete_task response JSON for task {task_id_str}: {e}")
   349→                        return None
   350→                    else:
   351→                        error_msg = edge_error or f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:complete_task:HTTP_{resp.status_code if resp else 'N/A'}] {resp.text[:200] if resp else 'No response'}"
   352→                        headless_logger.error(error_msg, task_id=task_id_str)
   353→                        _mark_task_failed_via_edge_function(task_id_str, f"Upload failed: {error_msg}")
   354→                        return None
   355→
   356→                else:
   357→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Using presigned upload for {output_path.name} ({file_size_mb:.2f} MB >= {FILE_SIZE_THRESHOLD_MB} MB)")
   358→
   359→                    # MODE 3: Presigned URL upload (for files 2MB or larger)
   360→                    # Step 1: Get signed upload URLs (request thumbnail URL for videos) - WITH RETRY
   361→                    generate_url_edge = f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/generate-upload-url"
   362→                    content_type = mimetypes.guess_type(str(output_path))[0] or 'application/octet-stream'
   363→
   364→                    upload_url_resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   365→                        edge_url=generate_url_edge,
   366→                        payload={

   368→                            "filename": output_path.name,

   370→                            "generate_thumbnail_url": is_video  # Request thumbnail URL for videos
   371→                        },
   372→                        headers=headers,
   373→                        function_name="generate-upload-url",
   374→                        context_id=task_id_str,
   375→                        timeout=30,
   376→                        max_retries=3)
   377→
   378→                    if edge_error or not upload_url_resp or upload_url_resp.status_code != 200:
   379→                        # Prefer standardized error from helper (avoids double-wrapping)
   380→                        error_msg = edge_error or f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:generate-upload-url:HTTP_{upload_url_resp.status_code if upload_url_resp else 'N/A'}] {upload_url_resp.text[:200] if upload_url_resp else 'No response'}"
   381→                        headless_logger.error(error_msg, task_id=task_id_str)
   382→                        _mark_task_failed_via_edge_function(task_id_str, f"Upload failed: {error_msg}")
   383→                        return
   384→
   385→                    upload_data = upload_url_resp.json()
   386→
   387→                    # Step 2: Extract and upload thumbnail for videos (if thumbnail URL was generated)
   388→                    thumbnail_storage_path = None
   389→                    if is_video and "thumbnail_upload_url" not in upload_data:
   390→                        _log_thumbnail(f"MODE3: \u26a0\ufe0f Edge function did not return thumbnail_upload_url for video", level="warning", task_id=task_id_str)
   391→                    if is_video and "thumbnail_upload_url" in upload_data:

   410→                                # Clean up temp file (but not existing poster)

   431→                        headless_logger.error(error_msg, task_id=task_id_str)
   432→                        _mark_task_failed_via_edge_function(task_id_str, f"Upload failed: {error_msg}")

   436→

   448→                    resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(

   456→                        fallback_url=None,

   458→
   459→                    if resp is not None and resp.status_code == 200:
   460→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Edge function SUCCESS for task {task_id_str} \u2192 status COMPLETE with file upload")
   461→                        # Parse response to get storage URL and thumbnail URL

   465→                            thumbnail_url = resp_data.get('thumbnail_url')  # Also get thumbnail
   466→                            if storage_url:

   468→                                if thumbnail_url:

   470→                                # Return both URLs as a dict

   472→                        except (ValueError, KeyError) as e:

   474→                        return None
   475→                    else:
   476→                        error_msg = edge_error or f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:complete_task:HTTP_{resp.status_code if resp else 'N/A'}] {resp.text[:200] if resp else 'No response'}"
   477→                        headless_logger.error(error_msg, task_id=task_id_str)
   478→                        # Use update-task-status edge function to mark as failed
   479→                        _mark_task_failed_via_edge_function(task_id_str, f"Upload failed: {error_msg}")
   480→                        return None
   481→            else:
   482→                # Not a local file, could be a Supabase storage URL or external reference
   483→                # Check if it's a Supabase storage URL - if so, extract storage_path for MODE 3/4
   484→                storage_path = None
   485→
   486→                # SPECIAL CASE: JSON output containing a storage URL (e.g., transition_only mode)
   487→                # JSON outputs contain metadata along with a file URL - we need to:
   488→                # 1. Extract the actual file URL from the JSON
   489→                # 2. Use that URL's storage_path for the edge function to mark complete
   490→                # 3. Update output_location with the full JSON afterward
   491→                if output_location_val.strip().startswith('{') and output_location_val.strip().endswith('}'):
   492→                    try:

   500→                        )
   501→                        if url_in_json and "/storage/v1/object/public/image_uploads/" in url_in_json:
   502→                            # Extract storage_path from the URL inside the JSON

   504→                            if len(path_parts) == 2:
   505→                                storage_path = path_parts[1]
   506→                                headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] JSON output detected - extracted storage_path: {storage_path}")
   507→
   508→                                # Step 1: Complete task with storage_path (marks as complete)
   509→                                payload = {
   510→                                    "task_id": task_id_str,
   511→                                    "storage_path": storage_path,
   512→                                }
   513→                                headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Completing task {task_id_str} with storage_path (JSON output mode)")
   514→
   515→                                headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   516→                                if _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:

   518→
   519→                                resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(

   524→                                    context_id=task_id_str,
   525→                                    timeout=30,
   526→                                    max_retries=3,
   527→                                    fallback_url=None,
   528→                                    retry_on_404_patterns=["Task not found", "not found"])
   529→
   530→                                if resp is not None and resp.status_code == 200:
   531→                                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Task {task_id_str} marked complete, now updating output_location with JSON")
   532→
   533→                                    # Step 2: Update output_location with full JSON metadata
   534→                                    # Use update-task-status to overwrite output_location with JSON
   535→                                    update_url = (
   536→                                        os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_UPDATE_TASK_STATUS_URL") or
   537→                                        (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/update-task-status" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   538→                                    )
   539→                                    if update_url:
   540→                                        update_payload = {
   541→                                            "task_id": task_id_str,
   542→                                            "status": STATUS_COMPLETE,
   543→                                            "output_location": output_location_val  # Full JSON
   544→                                        }
   545→                                        update_resp, update_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   546→                                            edge_url=update_url,
   547→                                            payload=update_payload,
   548→                                            headers=headers,
   549→                                            function_name="update-task-status",
   550→                                            context_id=task_id_str,
   551→                                            timeout=30,
   552→                                            max_retries=2,
   553→                                            fallback_url=None)
   554→                                        if update_resp and update_resp.status_code == 200:
   555→                                            headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Output location updated with JSON for task {task_id_str}")
   556→                                        else:
   557→                                            headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Failed to update output_location with JSON: {update_error}")
   558→                                            # Task is still complete, just without JSON metadata
   559→                                    return None
   560→                                else:
   561→                                    error_msg = edge_error or f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:complete_task:HTTP_{resp.status_code if resp else 'N/A'}] {resp.text[:200] if resp else 'No response'}"
   562→                                    headless_logger.error(error_msg, task_id=task_id_str)
   563→                                    _mark_task_failed_via_edge_function(task_id_str, f"Completion failed: {error_msg}")
   564→                                    return None
   565→                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   566→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Output looks like JSON but failed to parse, treating as regular output")
   567→
   568→                if "/storage/v1/object/public/image_uploads/" in output_location_val:
   569→                    # Extract storage path from URL
   570→                    # Format: https://xxx.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/image_uploads/{userId}/{filename}
   571→                    # MODE 3: userId/tasks/{task_id}/filename (pre-signed URL uploads)
   572→                    # MODE 4: userId/filename (orchestrator referencing child task upload)
   573→                    try:
   574→                        path_parts = output_location_val.split("/storage/v1/object/public/image_uploads/", 1)
   575→                        if len(path_parts) == 2:
   576→                            storage_path = path_parts[1]  # e.g., "userId/filename.mp4" or "userId/tasks/{task_id}/filename.mp4"
   577→                            headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Extracted storage_path from URL: {storage_path}")
   578→
   579→                            # Determine if this is MODE 3 or MODE 4 based on path structure
   580→                            path_components = storage_path.split('/')
   581→                            if len(path_components) >= 4 and path_components[1] == 'tasks':
   582→                                headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] MODE 3 path detected (pre-signed URL): {storage_path}")
   583→                            else:
   584→                                headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] MODE 4 path detected (orchestrator reference): {storage_path}")
   585→                    except (ValueError, IndexError) as e_extract:
   586→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Failed to extract storage_path: {e_extract}")
   587→
   588→                # Use MODE 3/4 if we have a storage path, otherwise use output_location (legacy)
   589→                if storage_path:
   590→                    payload = {"task_id": task_id_str, "storage_path": storage_path}
   591→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Using storage_path for task {task_id_str}")
   592→
   593→                    # Extract thumbnail storage path if thumbnail URL provided
   594→                    if thumbnail_url_val and "/storage/v1/object/public/image_uploads/" in thumbnail_url_val:

   597→                            if len(thumb_parts) == 2:
   598→                                thumbnail_storage_path = thumb_parts[1]
   599→                                payload["thumbnail_storage_path"] = thumbnail_storage_path
   600→                                headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Including thumbnail_storage_path: {thumbnail_storage_path}")
   601→                        except (ValueError, IndexError) as e:
   602→                            headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Failed to extract thumbnail path: {e}")
   603→                else:
   604→                    payload = {"task_id": task_id_str, "output_location": output_location_val}
   605→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Using output_location (legacy) for task {task_id_str}")
   606→
   607→                headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   608→                if _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   609→                    headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   610→
   611→                resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   612→                    edge_url=edge_url,
   613→                    payload=payload,
   614→                    headers=headers,
   615→                    function_name="complete_task",
   616→                    context_id=task_id_str,
   617→                    timeout=30,
   618→                    max_retries=3,
   619→                    fallback_url=None,
   620→                    retry_on_404_patterns=["Task not found", "not found"])
   621→
   622→                if resp is not None and resp.status_code == 200:
   623→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Edge function SUCCESS for task {task_id_str} \u2192 status COMPLETE")
   624→                    # Parse response to get storage URL and thumbnail URL
   625→                    try:
   626→                        resp_data = resp.json()
   627→                        storage_url = resp_data.get('public_url')
   628→                        thumbnail_url = resp_data.get('thumbnail_url')  # Also get thumbnail
   629→                        if storage_url:
   630→                            headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Storage URL: {storage_url}")
   631→                            if thumbnail_url:
   632→                                headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Thumbnail available at: {thumbnail_url}")
   633→                            # Return both URLs as a dict
   634→                            return {'public_url': storage_url, 'thumbnail_url': thumbnail_url}
   635→                        # If no URL in response, return the original output_location_val as string (legacy)
   636→                        return output_location_val
   637→                    except (ValueError, KeyError):
   638→                        return output_location_val
   639→                else:
   640→                    error_msg = edge_error or f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:complete_task:HTTP_{resp.status_code if resp else 'N/A'}] {resp.text[:200] if resp else 'No response'}"
   641→                    headless_logger.error(error_msg, task_id=task_id_str)
   642→                    # Use update-task-status edge function to mark as failed
   643→                    _mark_task_failed_via_edge_function(task_id_str, f"Completion failed: {error_msg}")
   644→                    return None
   645→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_edge:
   646→            headless_logger.error(f"complete_task edge function exception: {e_edge}", task_id=task_id_str)
   647→            return None
   648→    else:
   649→        # Use update-task-status edge function for all other status updates (with retry)
   650→        edge_url = (
   651→            os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_UPDATE_TASK_URL")
   652→            or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/update-task-status" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   653→        )
   654→
   655→        if not edge_url:
   656→            headless_logger.error(f"No update-task-status edge function URL available", task_id=task_id_str)
   657→            return
   658→
   659→        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   660→        if _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   661→            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   662→
   663→        payload = {
   664→            "task_id": task_id_str,
   665→            "status": status_str
   666→        }
   667→
   668→        if output_location_val:
   669→            payload["output_location"] = output_location_val
   670→
   671→        if thumbnail_url_val:
   672→            payload["thumbnail_url"] = thumbnail_url_val
   673→
   674→        headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Calling update-task-status Edge Function for task {task_id_str} \u2192 {status_str}")
   675→        resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   676→            edge_url=edge_url,
   677→            payload=payload,
   678→            headers=headers,
   679→            function_name="update-task-status",
   680→            context_id=task_id_str,
   681→            timeout=30,
   682→            max_retries=3)
   683→
   684→        if resp and resp.status_code == 200:
   685→            headless_logger.debug(f"[DEBUG] Edge function SUCCESS for task {task_id_str} \u2192 status {status_str}")
   686→            return
   687→        elif edge_error:
   688→            headless_logger.error(f"update-task-status edge function failed: {edge_error}", task_id=task_id_str)
   689→            return
   690→        elif resp:
   691→            headless_logger.error(f"update-task-status edge function failed: {resp.status_code} - {resp.text}", task_id=task_id_str)
   692→            return
   693→
   694→def mark_task_failed_supabase(task_id_str, error_message):
   695→    """Marks a task as Failed with an error message using direct database update."""
   696→    headless_logger.debug(f"Marking task {task_id_str} as Failed with message: {error_message}")
   697→    if not _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   698→        headless_logger.error("Supabase client not initialized. Cannot mark task failed.", task_id=task_id_str)
   699→        return
   700→
   701→    # Use the standard update function which now uses direct database updates for non-COMPLETE statuses
   702→    update_task_status_supabase(task_id_str, STATUS_FAILED, error_message)
   703→
   704→def reset_generation_started_at(task_id_str: str) -> bool:
   705→    """
   706→    Resets the generation_started_at timestamp to NOW for a task.
   707→
   708→    This should be called after model loading completes, so users are not
   709→    charged for model loading time. The billing period starts from this
   710→    reset timestamp instead of the original claim time.
   711→
   712→    Args:
   713→        task_id_str: Task ID to reset timestamp for
   714→
   715→    Returns:
   716→        True if successful, False otherwise
   717→    """
   718→    edge_url = (
   719→        os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_UPDATE_TASK_URL")
   720→        or (f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/update-task-status" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None)
   721→    )
   722→
   723→    if not edge_url:
   724→        headless_logger.error(f"No update-task-status edge function URL available for resetting generation_started_at", task_id=task_id_str)
   725→        return False
   726→
   727→    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   728→    if _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
   729→        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
   730→
   731→    # Send current status with reset flag - the task is already "In Progress"
   732→    payload = {
   733→        "task_id": task_id_str,
   734→        "status": STATUS_IN_PROGRESS,
   735→        "reset_generation_started_at": True
   736→    }
   737→
   738→    headless_logger.essential(f"[BILLING] Resetting generation_started_at for task {task_id_str} (model loading complete)", task_id=task_id_str)
   739→
   740→    resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   741→        edge_url=edge_url,
   742→        payload=payload,
   743→        headers=headers,
   744→        function_name="update-task-status",
   745→        context_id=task_id_str,
   746→        timeout=30,
   747→        max_retries=3)
   748→
   749→    if resp and resp.status_code == 200:
   750→        headless_logger.essential(f"[BILLING] Successfully reset generation_started_at for task {task_id_str}", task_id=task_id_str)
   751→        return True
   752→    elif edge_error:
   753→        headless_logger.error(f"Failed to reset generation_started_at for task {task_id_str}: {edge_error}", task_id=task_id_str)
   754→        return False
   755→    elif resp:
   756→        headless_logger.error(f"Failed to reset generation_started_at for task {task_id_str}: {resp.status_code} - {resp.text}", task_id=task_id_str)
   757→        return False
   758→
   759→    return False
   760→
```
