```
1→"""
     2→Task insertion into the database via Edge Functions.
     3→"""
     4→import os
     5→import time
     6→
     7→from postgrest.exceptions import APIError
     8→
     9→from source.core.log import headless_logger
    10→
    11→__all__ = [
    12→    "add_task_to_db",
    13→    "requeue_task_for_retry",
    14→    "update_task_status",
    15→    "update_task_status_supabase",
    16→    "mark_task_failed_supabase",
    17→    "reset_generation_started_at",
    18→]
    19→
    20→from . import config as _cfg
    21→from .config import (
    22→    STATUS_QUEUED,
    23→    STATUS_IN_PROGRESS)
    24→from .edge_helpers import _call_edge_function_with_retry
    25→
    26→# Re-export from task_status so existing
    27→# ``from source.core.db.task_completion import *`` continues to work.
    28→from .task_status import (  # noqa: F401
    29→    requeue_task_for_retry,
    30→    update_task_status,
    31→    update_task_status_supabase,
    32→    mark_task_failed_supabase,
    33→    reset_generation_started_at)
    34→
    35→def add_task_to_db(task_payload: dict, task_type_str: str, dependant_on: str | list[str] | None = None, db_path: str | None = None) -> str:
    36→    """
    37→    Adds a new task to the Supabase database via Edge Function.
    38→
    39→    Args:
    40→        task_payload: Task parameters dictionary
    41→        task_type_str: Type of task being created
    42→        dependant_on: Optional dependency - single task ID string or list of task IDs.
    43→                      When list is provided, task will only run when ALL dependencies complete.
    44→        db_path: Ignored (kept for API compatibility)
    45→
    46→    Returns:
    47→        str: The database row ID (UUID) assigned to the task
    48→    """
    49→    # Generate a new UUID for the database row ID
    50→    import uuid
    51→    actual_db_row_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    52→
    53→    # Sanitize payload and get project_id
    54→    params_for_db = task_payload.copy()
    55→    params_for_db.pop("task_type", None)  # Ensure task_type is not duplicated in params
    56→    project_id = task_payload.get("project_id", "default_project_id")
    57→
    58→    # Build Edge URL
    59→    edge_url = (
    60→        _cfg.SUPABASE_EDGE_CREATE_TASK_URL
    61→    ) or os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_CREATE_TASK_URL") or (
    62→        f"{_cfg.SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/create-task" if _cfg.SUPABASE_URL else None
    63→    )
    64→
    65→    if not edge_url:
    66→        raise ValueError("Edge Function URL for create-task is not configured")
    67→
    68→    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    69→    if _cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN:
    70→        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {_cfg.SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN}"
    71→
    72→    # Normalize dependant_on to list format for consistency
    73→    # Edge Function expects: null, or JSON array of UUIDs
    74→    dependant_on_list: list[str] | None = None
    75→    if dependant_on:
    76→        if isinstance(dependant_on, str):
    77→            dependant_on_list = [dependant_on]
    78→        else:
    79→            dependant_on_list = list(dependant_on)
    80→
    81→    # Defensive check: if dependencies are specified, ensure they all exist before enqueueing
    82→    if dependant_on_list:
    83→        max_retries = 3
    84→        retry_delay = 0.5
    85→
    86→        for dep_id in dependant_on_list:
    87→            for attempt in range(max_retries):
    88→                try:
    89→                    if _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
    90→                        resp_exist = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).select("id").eq("id", dep_id).single().execute()
    91→                        if not getattr(resp_exist, "data", None):
    92→                            headless_logger.debug(f"[ERROR][DEBUG_DEPENDENCY_CHAIN] dependant_on not found: {dep_id}. Refusing to create task of type {task_type_str} with broken dependency.")
    93→                            raise RuntimeError(f"dependant_on {dep_id} not found")
    94→                        # Successfully verified this dependency
    95→                        break
    96→                except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e_depchk:
    97→                    error_str = str(e_depchk)
    98→                    # Check if it's a "0 rows" error (race condition) and we have retries left
    99→                    if "0 rows" in error_str and attempt < max_retries - 1:
   100→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[RETRY][DEBUG_DEPENDENCY_CHAIN] Dependency {dep_id} not visible yet (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {retry_delay}s...")
   101→                        time.sleep(retry_delay)
   102→                        continue
   103→                    else:
   104→                        # Final attempt failed or different error - log warning but continue
   105→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[WARN][DEBUG_DEPENDENCY_CHAIN] Could not verify dependant_on {dep_id} existence prior to enqueue: {e_depchk}")
   106→
   107→    payload_edge = {
   108→        "task_id": actual_db_row_id,
   109→        "params": params_for_db,
   110→        "task_type": task_type_str,
   111→        "project_id": project_id,
   112→        "dependant_on": dependant_on_list,  # Always list or None for Edge Function
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    headless_logger.debug(f"Supabase Edge call >>> POST {edge_url} payload={str(payload_edge)[:120]}\u2026")
   116→
   117→    # Use standardized retry helper (handles 5xx, timeout, and network errors)
   118→    resp, edge_error = _call_edge_function_with_retry(
   119→        edge_url=edge_url,
   120→        payload=payload_edge,
   121→        headers=headers,
   122→        function_name="create-task",
   123→        context_id=f"{task_type_str}:{actual_db_row_id[:8]}",
   124→        timeout=45,  # Base timeout
   125→        max_retries=3)
   126→
   127→    # Handle failure cases
   128→    if edge_error:
   129→        raise RuntimeError(f"Edge Function create-task failed: {edge_error}")
   130→    if resp is None:
   131→        raise RuntimeError(f"Edge Function create-task returned no response for {actual_db_row_id}")
   132→
   133→    if resp.status_code == 200:
   134→        headless_logger.essential(f"Task {actual_db_row_id} (Type: {task_type_str}) queued via Edge Function.", task_id=actual_db_row_id)
   135→
   136→        # Verify task visibility and status to catch race conditions
   137→        if _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT:
   138→            max_verify_retries = 10  # Increased from 5 to handle longer replication delays
   139→            verify_start_time = time.time()
   140→
   141→            for attempt in range(max_verify_retries):
   142→                try:
   143→                    # Check status, created_at, and project_id for comprehensive debugging
   144→                    verify_resp = _cfg.SUPABASE_CLIENT.table(_cfg.PG_TABLE_NAME).select("status, created_at, project_id, task_type").eq("id", actual_db_row_id).single().execute()
   145→                    if verify_resp.data:
   146→                        status = verify_resp.data.get("status")
   147→                        created_at = verify_resp.data.get("created_at")
   148→                        db_project_id = verify_resp.data.get("project_id")
   149→                        db_task_type = verify_resp.data.get("task_type")
   150→                        elapsed = time.time() - verify_start_time
   151→
   152→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[VERIFY] Task {actual_db_row_id} found after {elapsed:.2f}s: status={status}, project_id={db_project_id}, task_type={db_task_type}, created_at={created_at}")
   153→
   154→                        if status == STATUS_QUEUED:
   155→                            headless_logger.essential(f"[VERIFY] Task {actual_db_row_id} verified visible and Queued (took {elapsed:.2f}s)", task_id=actual_db_row_id)
   156→                            break
   157→                        elif status == STATUS_IN_PROGRESS:
   158→                            headless_logger.warning(f"[VERIFY] Task {actual_db_row_id} already In Progress (claimed in {elapsed:.2f}s - unusually fast)", task_id=actual_db_row_id)
   159→                            break
   160→                        else:
   161→                            headless_logger.warning(f"[VERIFY] Task {actual_db_row_id} has unexpected status '{status}' after {elapsed:.2f}s", task_id=actual_db_row_id)
   162→                            break
   163→                    else:
   164→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[VERIFY] Attempt {attempt+1}/{max_verify_retries}: Task {actual_db_row_id} not visible yet (no data returned)")
   165→                except (APIError, RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e_ver:
   166→                    error_str = str(e_ver)
   167→                    if "0 rows" in error_str:
   168→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[VERIFY] Attempt {attempt+1}/{max_verify_retries}: Task {actual_db_row_id} not visible yet (0 rows)")
   169→                    else:
   170→                        headless_logger.debug(f"[VERIFY] Attempt {attempt+1}/{max_verify_retries}: Error verifying task {actual_db_row_id}: {e_ver}")
   171→
   172→                if attempt < max_verify_retries - 1:
   173→                    time.sleep(1.0)
   174→            else:
   175→                # Verification failed after all retries
   176→                elapsed = time.time() - verify_start_time
   177→                headless_logger.warning(f"Task {actual_db_row_id} creation confirmed by Edge Function but NOT VISIBLE in DB after {max_verify_retries} attempts ({elapsed:.2f}s)", task_id=actual_db_row_id)
   178→                headless_logger.warning(f"This is likely due to database replication lag. The task IS CREATED and will be processed when visible.", task_id=actual_db_row_id)
   179→                headless_logger.debug(f"[WARN] Task details: type={task_type_str}, project_id={project_id}, dependant_on={dependant_on}")
   180→
   181→        return actual_db_row_id
   182→    else:
   183→        error_msg = f"Edge Function create-task failed: {resp.status_code} - {resp.text}"
   184→        headless_logger.error(error_msg, task_id=actual_db_row_id)
   185→        raise RuntimeError(error_msg)
   186→
```

```
    64→def _log_thumbnail(msg: str, level: str = "debug", task_id: str = None):
    65→    """Log thumbnail-related messages via the centralized logger."""
    66→    full_msg = f"[THUMBNAIL] {msg}"
    67→    if headless_logger:
    68→        if level == "info":
    69→            headless_logger.info(full_msg, task_id=task_id)
    70→        elif level == "warning":
    71→            headless_logger.warning(full_msg, task_id=task_id)
    72→        else:

    78→EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX = "[EDGE_FAIL"  # Used by debug.py to detect edge failures
```

```
    73→class TestAdjustFrameBrightness:
    74→    def test_zero_factor_no_change(self):
    75→        frame = np.full((10, 10, 3), 128, dtype=np.uint8)
    76→        result = adjust_frame_brightness(frame, 0.0)
    77→        # alpha = 1.0 - 0.0 = 1.0 → same values
    78→        np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, frame)
    79→
    80→    def test_positive_factor_darker(self):
    81→        frame = np.full((10, 10, 3), 200, dtype=np.uint8)
    82→        result = adjust_frame_brightness(frame, 0.5)
    83→        # alpha = 1.0 - 0.5 = 0.5 → darker
    84→        assert result.mean() < frame.mean()
    85→
    86→    def test_negative_factor_brighter(self):
    87→        frame = np.full((10, 10, 3), 100, dtype=np.uint8)
    88→        result = adjust_frame_brightness(frame, -0.5)
    89→        # alpha = 1.0 - (-0.5) = 1.5 → brighter
    90→        assert result.mean() > frame.mean()
    91→
    92→    def test_result_is_uint8(self):
    93→        frame = np.full((10, 10, 3), 200, dtype=np.uint8)
    94→        result = adjust_frame_brightness(frame, -1.0)
    95→        assert result.dtype == np.uint8
    96→

    98→class TestGetSequentialTargetPath:
    99→    def test_no_collision(self, tmp_path):
   100→        result = get_sequential_target_path(tmp_path, "video", ".mp4")
   101→        assert result == tmp_path / "video.mp4"
   102→
   103→    def test_single_collision(self, tmp_path):
   104→        (tmp_path / "video.mp4").touch()
   105→        result = get_sequential_target_path(tmp_path, "video", ".mp4")
   106→        assert result == tmp_path / "video_1.mp4"
   107→
   108→    def test_multiple_collisions(self, tmp_path):
   109→        (tmp_path / "video.mp4").touch()
   110→        (tmp_path / "video_1.mp4").touch()
   111→        (tmp_path / "video_2.mp4").touch()
   112→        result = get_sequential_target_path(tmp_path, "video", ".mp4")
   113→        assert result == tmp_path / "video_3.mp4"
   114→
   115→    def test_suffix_without_dot(self, tmp_path):
   116→        result = get_sequential_target_path(tmp_path, "file", "txt")
   117→        assert result == tmp_path / "file.txt"
   118→
```

```
     7→
     8→class TestFlattenParams:
     9→    def test_empty(self):
    10→        assert ParamGroup.flatten_params({}) == {}
    11→
    12→    def test_top_level_only(self):
    13→        params = {"prompt": "hello", "steps": 10}
    14→        result = ParamGroup.flatten_params(params)
    15→        assert result == {"prompt": "hello", "steps": 10}
    16→
    17→    def test_orchestrator_details_merge(self):
    18→        params = {
    19→            "orchestrator_details": {"resolution": "960x544", "steps": 5},
    20→            "prompt": "test",
    21→        }
    22→        result = ParamGroup.flatten_params(params)
    23→        assert result["prompt"] == "test"
    24→        assert result["resolution"] == "960x544"
    25→        assert result["steps"] == 5
    26→
    27→    def test_full_orchestrator_payload_merge(self):
    28→        params = {
    29→            "full_orchestrator_payload": {"seed": 42, "model": "vace"},
    30→            "prompt": "test",
    31→        }
    32→        result = ParamGroup.flatten_params(params)
    33→        assert result["seed"] == 42
    34→        assert result["model"] == "vace"
    35→
    36→    def test_three_level_precedence(self):
    37→        """top_level > orchestrator_details > full_orchestrator_payload."""
    38→        params = {
    39→            "full_orchestrator_payload": {"steps": 1, "seed": 100, "model": "a"},
    40→            "orchestrator_details": {"steps": 2, "seed": 200},
    41→            "steps": 3,
    42→        }
    43→        result = ParamGroup.flatten_params(params)
    44→        assert result["steps"] == 3      # top-level wins
    45→        assert result["seed"] == 200     # details wins over payload
    46→        assert result["model"] == "a"    # only in payload
    47→
    48→    def test_non_dict_nested_dropped(self):
    49→        """If orchestrator_details is not a dict, it's excluded from flattened result."""
    50→        params = {"orchestrator_details": "not_a_dict", "prompt": "test"}
    51→        result = ParamGroup.flatten_params(params)
    52→        # Non-dict orchestrator_details is neither merged nor kept as top-level
    53→        assert "orchestrator_details" not in result
    54→        assert result["prompt"] == "test"
    55→
```

```
    14→def _fake_safe_open(keys=None):
    15→    """Create a mock context manager for safetensors.torch.safe_open."""
    16→    ctx = MagicMock()
    17→    ctx.__enter__ = MagicMock(return_value=ctx)
    18→    ctx.__exit__ = MagicMock(return_value=False)

    40→            ok, msg = validate_lora_file(p, "lora.safetensors")
```

```
1→"""
     2→Edge function call helpers: retry wrapper and JWT utilities.
     3→"""
     4→import json
     5→import time
     6→import base64
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→import httpx
    10→
    11→from source.core.log import headless_logger
    12→
    13→__all__: list[str] = []
    14→
    15→from .config import (
    16→    EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX,
    17→    RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES)
    18→
    19→def _call_edge_function_with_retry(
    20→    edge_url: str,
    21→    payload,
    22→    headers: dict,
    23→    function_name: str,
    24→    *,
    25→    context_id: str = "",  # task_id or worker_id for logging
    26→    timeout: int = 30,
    27→    max_retries: int = 3,
    28→    method: str = "POST",
    29→    fallback_url: str | None = None,  # Optional 404 fallback URL
    30→    retry_on_404_patterns: list[str] | None = None,  # Patterns in 404 response body to retry
    31→) -> tuple[httpx.Response | None, str | None]:
    32→    """
    33→    Call a Supabase edge function with retry logic for transient errors.
    34→
    35→    Retries on:
    36→    - 502 Bad Gateway, 503 Service Unavailable, 504 Gateway Timeout
    37→    - Network errors (TimeoutException, RequestError)
    38→    - 404 errors if response body matches retry_on_404_patterns (e.g. "Task not found")
    39→
    40→    Args:
    41→        edge_url: The edge function URL
    42→        payload: JSON payload (for POST) or None
    43→        headers: HTTP headers
    44→        function_name: Name of function for logging (e.g. "complete_task", "update-task-status")
    45→        context_id: Task/worker ID for logging context
    46→        timeout: Base timeout in seconds (increases on retries)
    47→        max_retries: Maximum retry attempts
    48→        method: HTTP method ("POST" or "PUT")
    49→        fallback_url: Optional fallback URL to try on 404
    50→        retry_on_404_patterns: List of substrings - if 404 response contains any, retry
    51→
    52→    Returns:
    53→        Tuple of (response, error_message)
    54→        - On success: (response, None)
    55→        - On failure: (response_or_None, standardized_error_message)
    56→
    57→        Error message format for detection:
    58→        "[EDGE_FAIL:{function_name}:{error_type}] {details}"
    59→    """
    60→    ctx = f" for {context_id}" if context_id else ""
    61→
    62→    for attempt in range(max_retries):
    63→        try:
    64→            current_timeout = timeout + (attempt * 15)  # Increase timeout on retries
    65→
    66→            if method == "PUT":
    67→                # For large uploads, avoid buffering entire files in memory:
    68→                # - If payload is a Path/str to an existing file, open+stream per attempt.
    69→                # - If payload is bytes, send directly.
    70→                # - Otherwise, pass through to httpx (caller responsibility).
    71→                if isinstance(payload, (str, Path)) and Path(payload).exists():
    72→                    with open(Path(payload), "rb") as f:
    73→                        resp = httpx.put(edge_url, content=f, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    74→                else:
    75→                    resp = httpx.put(edge_url, content=payload, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    76→            else:
    77→                resp = httpx.post(edge_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    78→
    79→            # Handle 404 fallback (e.g. hyphen vs underscore naming)
    80→            if resp.status_code == 404 and fallback_url:
    81→                headless_logger.debug(f"[RETRY] {function_name} returned 404; trying fallback URL: {fallback_url}")
    82→                if method == "PUT":
    83→                    if isinstance(payload, (str, Path)) and Path(payload).exists():
    84→                        with open(Path(payload), "rb") as f:
    85→                            resp = httpx.put(fallback_url, content=f, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    86→                    else:
    87→                        resp = httpx.put(fallback_url, content=payload, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    88→                else:
    89→                    resp = httpx.post(fallback_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=current_timeout)
    90→
    91→            # Success
    92→            if resp.status_code in [200, 201]:
    93→                return resp, None
    94→
    95→            # Retry on specific 404 patterns (e.g. "Task not found" due to replication lag)
    96→            if resp.status_code == 404 and retry_on_404_patterns and attempt < max_retries - 1:
    97→                resp_text = resp.text
    98→                should_retry = any(pattern.lower() in resp_text.lower() for pattern in retry_on_404_patterns)
    99→                if should_retry:
   100→                    wait_time = 2 ** attempt  # Exponential backoff: 1s, 2s, 4s
   101→                    headless_logger.essential(f"[RETRY] {function_name} got 404 with retryable pattern{ctx} (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {wait_time}s...")
   102→                    headless_logger.debug(f"[RETRY] 404 response: {resp_text[:200]}")
   103→                    time.sleep(wait_time)
   104→                    continue
   105→
   106→            # Retryable error (5xx)
   107→            if resp.status_code in RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES and attempt < max_retries - 1:
   108→                wait_time = 2 ** attempt  # Exponential backoff: 1s, 2s, 4s
   109→                headless_logger.essential(f"[RETRY] {function_name} got {resp.status_code}{ctx} (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {wait_time}s...")
   110→                time.sleep(wait_time)
   111→                continue
   112→
   113→            # Non-retryable error or final attempt
   114→            error_type = "5XX_TRANSIENT" if resp.status_code in RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES else f"HTTP_{resp.status_code}"
   115→            error_msg = f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:{function_name}:{error_type}] {resp.status_code} after {attempt + 1} attempts{ctx}: {resp.text[:200]}"
   116→
   117→            if resp.status_code in RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES:
   118→                headless_logger.error(f"{function_name} failed with {resp.status_code} after {max_retries} attempts{ctx}")
   119→
   120→            return resp, error_msg
   121→
   122→        except httpx.TimeoutException as e:
   123→            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
   124→                wait_time = 2 ** attempt
   125→                headless_logger.essential(f"[RETRY] {function_name} timeout{ctx} (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {wait_time}s...")
   126→                time.sleep(wait_time)
   127→                continue
   128→            else:
   129→                error_msg = f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:{function_name}:TIMEOUT] Timed out after {max_retries} attempts{ctx}: {e}"
   130→                headless_logger.error(error_msg)
   131→                return None, error_msg
   132→
   133→        except httpx.RequestError as e:
   134→            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
   135→                wait_time = 2 ** attempt
   136→                headless_logger.essential(f"[RETRY] {function_name} network error{ctx} (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}), retrying in {wait_time}s: {e}")
   137→                time.sleep(wait_time)
   138→                continue
   139→            else:
   140→                error_msg = f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:{function_name}:NETWORK] Request failed after {max_retries} attempts{ctx}: {e}"
   141→                headless_logger.error(error_msg)
   142→                return None, error_msg
   143→
   144→    # Should not reach here, but safety fallback
   145→    error_msg = f"{EDGE_FAIL_PREFIX}:{function_name}:UNKNOWN] All retries exhausted{ctx}"
   146→    return None, error_msg
   147→
   148→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Internal Helpers for Supabase
   150→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→def _get_user_id_from_jwt(jwt_str: str) -> str | None:
   153→    """Decodes a JWT and extracts the 'sub' (user ID) claim without validation."""
   154→    if not jwt_str:
   155→        return None
   156→    try:
   157→        # JWT is composed of header.payload.signature
   158→        _, payload_b64, _ = jwt_str.split('.')
   159→        # The payload is base64 encoded. It needs to be padded to be decoded correctly.
   160→        payload_b64 += '=' * (-len(payload_b64) % 4)
   161→        payload_json = base64.b64decode(payload_b64).decode('utf-8')
   162→        payload = json.loads(payload_json)
   163→        user_id = payload.get('sub')
   164→        headless_logger.debug(f"JWT Decode: Extracted user ID (sub): {user_id}")
   165→        return user_id
   166→    except (ValueError, KeyError, json.JSONDecodeError) as e:
   167→        headless_logger.debug(f"[ERROR] Could not decode JWT to get user ID: {e}")
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→def _is_jwt_token(token_str: str) -> bool:
   171→    """
   172→    Checks if a token string looks like a JWT (has 3 parts separated by dots).
   173→    """
   174→    if not token_str:
   175→        return False
   176→    parts = token_str.split('.')
   177→    return len(parts) == 3
   178→
```

```
    13→class TestFormat1WgpLegacy:
```

```
   396→class TestThreePhaseConfig:
   397→    """Test 3-phase configurations (common for Lightning models)."""
   398→    
   399→    THREE_PHASE_CONFIG = {
   400→        "num_phases": 3,
   401→        "steps_per_phase": [2, 2, 2],
   402→        "phases": [
   403→            {
   404→                "guidance_scale": 1.0,
   405→                "loras": [
   406→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/high_noise.safetensors", "multiplier": 1.2},
   407→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/style.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.5},
   408→                ]
   409→            },
   410→            {
   411→                "guidance_scale": 1.0,
   412→                "loras": [
   413→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/high_noise.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.6},
   414→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/style.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.5},
   415→                ]
   416→            },
   417→            {
   418→                "guidance_scale": 1.0,
   419→                "loras": [
   420→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/high_noise.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.0},
   421→                    {"url": "https://hf.co/style.safetensors", "multiplier": 0.8},
   422→                ]
   423→            }
   424→        ]
   425→    }
   426→    
   427→    def test_three_phase_multipliers(self):
   428→        """3-phase config should produce multipliers with 3 values."""
   429→        result = parse_phase_config(
   430→            self.THREE_PHASE_CONFIG,
   431→            num_inference_steps=6,
   432→            task_id="test"
   433→        )
   434→        
   435→        # Should have 2 LoRAs
   436→        assert len(result["lora_names"]) == 2
   437→        assert len(result["lora_multipliers"]) == 2
   438→        
   439→        # Each multiplier should have 3 semicolon-separated values
   440→        for mult in result["lora_multipliers"]:
   441→            values = mult.split(";")
   442→            assert len(values) == 3, f"Expected 3 phases, got {len(values)}: {mult}"
   443→        
   444→        # Verify specific values
   445→        assert result["lora_multipliers"][0] == "1.2;0.6;0.0"  # high_noise
   446→        assert result["lora_multipliers"][1] == "0.5;0.5;0.8"  # style
   447→    
   448→    def test_three_phase_through_taskconfig(self):
   449→        """3-phase config should work through full TaskConfig flow."""
   450→        parsed = parse_phase_config(
   451→            self.THREE_PHASE_CONFIG,
   452→            num_inference_steps=6,
   453→            task_id="test"
   454→        )
   455→        
   456→        params = {
   457→            "activated_loras": parsed["lora_names"],
   458→            "loras_multipliers": " ".join(parsed["lora_multipliers"]),
   459→        }
   460→        
   461→        config = LoRAConfig.from_params(params, task_id="test")
   462→        
   463→        # Should preserve 3-phase multipliers
   464→        for entry in config.entries:
   465→            if ";" in str(entry.multiplier):
   466→                values = str(entry.multiplier).split(";")
   467→                assert len(values) == 3

   470→class TestQueueIntegration:
   471→    """Test that simulates what _convert_to_wgp_task does."""
   472→    
   473→    def test_queue_conversion_flow(self):
   474→        """
   475→        Simulate the exact flow in HeadlessTaskQueue._convert_to_wgp_task.
   476→        This is the critical integration point.
   477→        """
   478→        # Params as they would come from a GenerationTask
   479→        task_params = {
   480→            "prompt": "test prompt",
   481→            "model_name": "wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2",
   482→            "resolution": "896x496",
   483→            "video_length": 61,
   484→            "activated_loras": [
   485→                "https://huggingface.co/test/lora1.safetensors",
   486→                "https://huggingface.co/test/lora2.safetensors",
   487→            ],
   488→            "loras_multipliers": "1.0;0.0 0.5;0.8",
   489→            "_source_task_type": "travel_segment",
   490→        }
   491→        
   492→        # Step 1: Parse into TaskConfig (like _convert_to_wgp_task does)
   493→        config = TaskConfig.from_db_task(
   494→            task_params,
   495→            task_id="test_queue",
   496→            task_type=task_params.get('_source_task_type', ''),
   497→            model="wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2",
   498→            debug_mode=True
   499→        )
   500→        
   501→        # Step 2: Check for pending downloads
   502→        assert config.lora.has_pending_downloads()
   503→        pending = config.lora.get_pending_downloads()
   504→        assert len(pending) == 2
   505→        
   506→        # Step 3: Simulate downloads
   507→        for url, mult in list(pending.items()):
   508→            local_filename = os.path.basename(url)
   509→            config.lora.mark_downloaded(url, local_filename)
   510→        
   511→        # Step 4: Validate
   512→        config.validate()
   513→        # Should have no critical errors after download
   514→        
   515→        # Step 5: Convert to WGP format
   516→        wgp_params = config.to_wgp_format()
   517→        
   518→        # Step 6: Verify output
   519→        assert "activated_loras" in wgp_params
   520→        loras = wgp_params["activated_loras"]
   521→        
   522→        # Should have 2 LoRAs
   523→        assert len(loras) == 2
   524→        
   525→        # Should be filenames, NOT URLs
   526→        for lora in loras:
   527→            assert not lora.startswith("http"), f"URL leaked to WGP: {lora}"
   528→            assert lora.endswith(".safetensors")
   529→        
   530→        # Multipliers should be preserved
   531→        assert "loras_multipliers" in wgp_params
   532→        assert ";" in wgp_params["loras_multipliers"]  # Phase config format
   533→        
   534→        print("\n✅ Queue integration test passed!")
   535→        print(f"   Input URLs: {task_params['activated_loras']}")
   536→        print(f"   Output filenames: {wgp_params['activated_loras']}")
   537→        print(f"   Output multipliers: {wgp_params['loras_multipliers']}")
   538→

   540→class TestDownloaderUtils:
   541→    """Test downloader helper behaviors used by the queue on feat/typed-params."""
   542→
   543→    def test_download_normalizes_hf_blob_url(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
   544→        """
   545→        HF URLs sometimes come in as /blob/ links; we should normalize to /resolve/
   546→        and pass the correct repo_id + filename to hf_hub_download.
   547→        """
   548→        loras_dir = tmp_path / "loras"
   549→        loras_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   550→
   551→        called = {}
   552→
   553→        def fake_hf_hub_download(repo_id, filename, local_dir, local_dir_use_symlinks=False, subfolder=None, **kwargs):
   554→            called["repo_id"] = repo_id
   555→            called["filename"] = filename
   556→            called["local_dir"] = local_dir
   557→            called["subfolder"] = subfolder
   558→            # create the file the function expects to exist afterwards
   559→            Path(local_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   560→            out = Path(local_dir) / filename
   561→            out.write_bytes(b"stub")
   562→            return str(out)
   563→
   564→        # Patch the import used inside _download_lora_from_url
   565→        import huggingface_hub
   566→        monkeypatch.setattr(huggingface_hub, "hf_hub_download", fake_hf_hub_download)
   567→
   568→        # Mock path resolution so downloads go to tmp_path instead of real Wan2GP/
   569→        import source.models.lora.lora_paths as lora_paths
   570→        monkeypatch.setattr(lora_paths, "get_lora_dir_for_model", lambda model_type, wan_dir: loras_dir)
   571→
   572→        url = "https://huggingface.co/org/repo/blob/main/loras/my_lora.safetensors"
   573→        out = _download_lora_from_url(url, task_id="test")
   574→
   575→        assert out == "my_lora.safetensors"
   576→        assert called["repo_id"] == "org/repo"
   577→        # lora_utils passes filename as basename and subfolder for resolve/main/<subfolder>/<filename>
   578→        assert called["filename"] == "my_lora.safetensors"
   579→        assert called["subfolder"] == "loras"
   580→        assert Path(called["local_dir"]).name == "loras"
   581→
   582→    def test_download_collision_prefixes_parent_and_removes_legacy_generic(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
   583→        """
   584→        When a collision-prone filename like high_noise_model.safetensors is used, we prefix the
   585→        parent folder and remove any legacy generic file in standard lora dirs.
   586→        """
   587→        loras_dir = tmp_path / "loras"
   588→        loras_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   589→
   590→        # Create a legacy collision-prone file that should be cleaned up
   591→        legacy = loras_dir / "high_noise_model.safetensors"
   592→        legacy.write_bytes(b"old")
   593→
   594→        # Patch urlretrieve to avoid network and write the "downloaded" file
   595→        def fake_urlretrieve(url, filename):
   596→            Path(filename).write_bytes(b"new")
   597→            return filename, None
   598→
   599→        import source.models.lora.lora_utils as lora_utils
   600→        monkeypatch.setattr(lora_utils, "urlretrieve", fake_urlretrieve)
   601→
   602→        # Mock path resolution so downloads and cleanup use tmp_path
   603→        import source.models.lora.lora_paths as lora_paths
   604→        monkeypatch.setattr(lora_paths, "get_lora_dir_for_model", lambda model_type, wan_dir: loras_dir)
   605→        monkeypatch.setattr(lora_paths, "get_lora_search_dirs", lambda wan_dir, repo_root=None: [loras_dir])
   606→
   607→        url = "https://example.com/Wan2.2-I2V-A14B-4steps-lora-rank64-Seko-V1/high_noise_model.safetensors"
   608→        out = _download_lora_from_url(url, task_id="test")
   609→
   610→        # Legacy generic file removed
   611→        assert not legacy.exists()
   612→
   613→        # New file name is collision-safe
   614→        expected = [REDACTED]
   615→        assert out == expected
   616→        assert (loras_dir / expected).is_file()
   617→
   618→
   619→if __name__ == "__main__":
   620→    pytest.main([__file__, "-v"])
```

```
    64→
    65→def create_test_video(output_path: Path, num_frames: int = 30, resolution: tuple = (256, 256), fps: int = 16) -> Path:
    66→    """Create a simple test video with colored frames."""
    67→    import cv2
    68→    
    69→    fourcc = cv2.VideoWriter_fourcc(*'mp4v')
    70→    writer = cv2.VideoWriter(str(output_path), fourcc, fps, resolution)
    71→    
    72→    for i in range(num_frames):
    73→        # Create a frame with color gradient based on frame number
    74→        # This makes it easy to visually verify frame extraction
    75→        hue = int(180 * i / num_frames)  # Cycle through hues
    76→        hsv = np.full((resolution[1], resolution[0], 3), [hue, 255, 200], dtype=np.uint8)
    77→        bgr = cv2.cvtColor(hsv, cv2.COLOR_HSV2BGR)
    78→        
    79→        # Add frame number text
    80→        cv2.putText(bgr, f"Frame {i}", (10, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (255, 255, 255), 2)
    81→        
    82→        writer.write(bgr)
    83→    
    84→    writer.release()
    85→    return output_path

   321→class TestCalculateSegmentStitchedPosition:
   322→    """Tests for segment position calculation in stitched timeline."""
   323→    
   324→    def test_first_segment(self):
   325→        """First segment starts at 0."""
   326→        seg_frames = [81, 81, 81, 81, 81]
   327→        overlaps = [10, 10, 10, 10]
   328→        
   329→        start, count = calculate_segment_stitched_position(0, seg_frames, overlaps)
   330→        
   331→        assert start == 0
   332→        assert count == 81
   333→    
   334→    def test_second_segment(self):
   335→        """Second segment starts at first_frames - overlap."""
   336→        seg_frames = [81, 81, 81, 81, 81]
   337→        overlaps = [10, 10, 10, 10]
   338→        
   339→        start, count = calculate_segment_stitched_position(1, seg_frames, overlaps)
   340→        
   341→        assert start == 71  # 81 - 10
   342→        assert count == 81
   343→    
   344→    def test_third_segment(self):
   345→        """Third segment position."""
   346→        seg_frames = [81, 81, 81, 81, 81]
   347→        overlaps = [10, 10, 10, 10]
   348→        
   349→        start, count = calculate_segment_stitched_position(2, seg_frames, overlaps)
   350→        
   351→        assert start == 142  # 71 + 81 - 10
   352→        assert count == 81
   353→    
   354→    def test_fifth_segment(self):
   355→        """Fifth segment (last in 5-segment config)."""
   356→        seg_frames = [81, 81, 81, 81, 81]
   357→        overlaps = [10, 10, 10, 10]
   358→        
   359→        start, count = calculate_segment_stitched_position(4, seg_frames, overlaps)
   360→        
   361→        assert start == 284  # 0, 71, 142, 213, 284
   362→        assert count == 81
   363→    
   364→    def test_varying_segment_frames(self):
   365→        """Test with varying frame counts per segment."""
   366→        seg_frames = [81, 65, 49, 81]
   367→        overlaps = [10, 10, 10]
   368→        
   369→        # Segment 0: start=0, count=81
   370→        start0, count0 = calculate_segment_stitched_position(0, seg_frames, overlaps)
   371→        assert start0 == 0
   372→        assert count0 == 81
   373→        
   374→        # Segment 1: start=71 (81-10), count=65
   375→        start1, count1 = calculate_segment_stitched_position(1, seg_frames, overlaps)
   376→        assert start1 == 71
   377→        assert count1 == 65
   378→        
   379→        # Segment 2: start=126 (71+65-10), count=49
   380→        start2, count2 = calculate_segment_stitched_position(2, seg_frames, overlaps)
   381→        assert start2 == 126
   382→        assert count2 == 49
   383→    
   384→    def test_no_overlap(self):
   385→        """Test with no overlap."""
   386→        seg_frames = [81, 81, 81]
   387→        overlaps = [0, 0]
   388→        
   389→        start0, _ = calculate_segment_stitched_position(0, seg_frames, overlaps)
   390→        start1, _ = calculate_segment_stitched_position(1, seg_frames, overlaps)
   391→        start2, _ = calculate_segment_stitched_position(2, seg_frames, overlaps)
   392→        
   393→        assert start0 == 0
   394→        assert start1 == 81
   395→        assert start2 == 162
```

```
    62→class QwenHandler:
    63→    """Handles all Qwen image editing task types."""
    64→
    65→    def __init__(self, wan_root: str, task_id: str):
    66→        """
    67→        Initialize Qwen handler.
    68→
    69→        Args:
    70→            wan_root: Path to Wan2GP root directory
    71→            task_id: Task ID for logging
    72→        """
    73→        self.wan_root = Path(wan_root).resolve()
    74→        self.task_id = task_id
    75→        self.qwen_lora_dir = self.wan_root / "loras_qwen"
    76→        self.qwen_lora_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    77→
    78→    # ── Logging helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    79→
    80→    def _log_debug(self, message: str):
    81→        """Log debug message."""
    82→        model_logger.debug(f"[QWEN_HANDLER] Task {self.task_id}: {message}", task_id=self.task_id)
    83→
    84→    def _log_info(self, message: str):
    85→        """Log info message."""
    86→        model_logger.info(f"[QWEN_HANDLER] Task {self.task_id}: {message}", task_id=self.task_id)
    87→
    88→    def _log_warning(self, message: str):
    89→        """Log warning message."""
    90→        model_logger.warning(f"[QWEN_HANDLER] Task {self.task_id}: {message}", task_id=self.task_id)
    91→
    92→    def _log_error(self, message: str):
    93→        """Log error message."""
    94→        model_logger.error(f"[QWEN_HANDLER] Task {self.task_id}: {message}", task_id=self.task_id)
    95→
    96→    # ── Model config helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────
    97→
    98→    def _get_edit_model_config(self, db_task_params: Dict[str, Any]) -> dict:
    99→        """Get the config for the selected edit model variant."""
   100→        variant = db_task_params.get("qwen_edit_model", "qwen-edit")
   101→        config = QWEN_EDIT_MODEL_CONFIG.get(variant)
   102→        if config is None:
   103→            self._log_warning(f"Unknown qwen_edit_model '{variant}', falling back to 'qwen-edit'")
   104→            config = QWEN_EDIT_MODEL_CONFIG["qwen-edit"]
   105→        return config
   106→
   107→    def get_edit_model_name(self, db_task_params: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   108→        """Return the WGP model name for the selected edit model variant."""
   109→        return self._get_edit_model_config(db_task_params)["model_name"]
   110→
   111→    # ── Compositor / resolution delegates ───────────────────────────────
   112→
   113→    def cap_qwen_resolution(self, resolution_str: str) -> Optional[str]:
   114→        """Cap resolution to 1200px max dimension while maintaining aspect ratio."""
   115→        return cap_qwen_resolution(resolution_str, task_id=self.task_id)

   117→    def create_qwen_masked_composite(
   118→        self,
   119→        image_url: str,
   120→        mask_url: str,
   121→        output_dir: Path,
   122→    ) -> str:

   131→    # ── LoRA management ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   132→
   133→    def _download_lora_if_missing(self, repo_id: str, filename: str, hf_subfolder: str = None) -> Optional[Path]:
   134→        """Helper to download a LoRA if it doesn't exist locally."""
   135→        target_path = self.qwen_lora_dir / filename
   136→        if target_path.exists():
   137→            self._log_debug(f"LoRA already present: {target_path}")
   138→            return target_path.resolve()
   139→
   140→        self._log_info(f"Downloading LoRA '{filename}' from '{repo_id}'" +
   141→                       (f" (subfolder: {hf_subfolder})" if hf_subfolder else ""))
   142→        try:
   143→            dl_path = hf_hub_download(
   144→                repo_id=repo_id,
   145→                filename=filename,
   146→                subfolder=hf_subfolder,
   147→                revision="main",
   148→                local_dir=str(self.qwen_lora_dir)
   149→            )
   150→            # If downloaded to a subdirectory, move to flat location
   151→            actual_path = Path(dl_path)
   152→            if actual_path.exists() and actual_path.resolve() != target_path.resolve():
   153→                actual_path.replace(target_path)
   154→            self._log_info(f"Successfully downloaded {filename}")
   155→            return target_path.resolve()
   156→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   157→            self._log_warning(f"LoRA download failed for {filename}: {e}")
   158→            return None
   159→
   160→    def _ensure_lora_lists(self, generation_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   161→        """Ensure lora_names and lora_multipliers exist as lists."""
   162→        generation_params.setdefault("lora_names", [])
   163→        generation_params.setdefault("lora_multipliers", [])
   164→
   165→    def _add_lightning_lora(
   166→        self,
   167→        db_task_params: Dict[str, Any],
   168→        generation_params: Dict[str, Any],
   169→        lightning_fname: str = [REDACTED],
   170→        lightning_repo: str = "lightx2v/Qwen-Image-Lightning",
   171→        hf_subfolder: str = None,
   172→        default_phase1: float = 0.85,
   173→        default_phase2: float = 0.0,
   174→    ):
   175→        """Download Lightning LoRA if needed and add to generation params with phase support."""
   176→        self._ensure_lora_lists(generation_params)
   177→
   178→        if not (self.qwen_lora_dir / lightning_fname).exists():
   179→            self._download_lora_if_missing(lightning_repo, lightning_fname, hf_subfolder=hf_subfolder)
   180→
   181→        if lightning_fname in generation_params["lora_names"]:
   182→            return
   183→
   184→        phase1 = float(db_task_params.get("lightning_lora_strength_phase_1", default_phase1))
   185→        phase2 = float(db_task_params.get("lightning_lora_strength_phase_2", default_phase2))
   186→
   187→        generation_params["lora_names"].append(lightning_fname)
   188→        if db_task_params.get("hires_scale") is not None:
   189→            generation_params["lora_multipliers"].append(f"{phase1};{phase2}")
   190→            self._log_info(f"Added Lightning LoRA with strength Phase1={phase1}, Phase2={phase2}")
   191→        else:
   192→            generation_params["lora_multipliers"].append(phase1)
   193→            self._log_info(f"Added Lightning LoRA with strength {phase1}")
   194→
   195→    def _add_lightning_lora_simple(
   196→        self,
   197→        db_task_params: Dict[str, Any],
   198→        generation_params: Dict[str, Any],
   199→        lightning_fname: str = [REDACTED],
   200→        lightning_repo: str = "lightx2v/Qwen-Image-Lightning",
   201→        strength_param: str = "lightning_lora_strength",
   202→        default_strength: float = 0.45,
   203→    ):
   204→        """Download Lightning LoRA if needed and add with a single strength value (no phases)."""
   205→        self._ensure_lora_lists(generation_params)
   206→
   207→        if not (self.qwen_lora_dir / lightning_fname).exists():
   208→            self._download_lora_if_missing(lightning_repo, lightning_fname)
   209→
   210→        if lightning_fname in generation_params["lora_names"]:
   211→            return
   212→
   213→        strength = float(db_task_params.get(strength_param, default_strength))
   214→        generation_params["lora_names"].append(lightning_fname)
   215→        generation_params["lora_multipliers"].append(strength)
   216→        self._log_info(f"Added Lightning LoRA '{lightning_fname}' @ {strength}")
   217→
   218→    def _add_task_lora(self, generation_params: Dict[str, Any], repo_id: str, filename: str, multiplier: float = 1.0):
   219→        """Download and add a task-specific LoRA."""
   220→        self._download_lora_if_missing(repo_id, filename)
   221→        self._ensure_lora_lists(generation_params)
   222→        if filename not in generation_params["lora_names"]:
   223→            generation_params["lora_names"].append(filename)
   224→            generation_params["lora_multipliers"].append(multiplier)
   225→
   226→    def _apply_additional_loras(self, db_task_params: Dict[str, Any], generation_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   227→        """Apply additional LoRAs from task params (loras array or additional_loras dict)."""
   228→        # Handle array format: [{"path": "url", "scale": 0.8}]
   229→        loras = db_task_params.get("loras", [])
   230→        for lora in loras:
   231→            if isinstance(lora, dict):
   232→                path = lora.get("path", "")
   233→                scale = float(lora.get("scale", 1.0))
   234→                if path:
   235→                    if "additional_loras" not in generation_params:
   236→                        generation_params["additional_loras"] = {}
   237→                    generation_params["additional_loras"][path] = scale
   238→                    self._log_debug(f"Added LoRA from array: {path} @ {scale}")
   239→
   240→        # Handle dict format: {"url": scale}
   241→        additional_loras = db_task_params.get("additional_loras", {})
   242→        if additional_loras:
   243→            if "additional_loras" not in generation_params:
   244→                generation_params["additional_loras"] = {}
   245→            generation_params["additional_loras"].update(additional_loras)
   246→            self._log_debug(f"Added {len(additional_loras)} additional LoRAs from dict")
   247→
   248→    # ── Hires fix ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   249→
   250→    def _maybe_add_hires_config(self, db_task_params: Dict[str, Any], generation_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   251→        """Add hires_config if hires params are present in task params."""
   252→        hires_scale = db_task_params.get("hires_scale")
   253→        if hires_scale is None:
   254→            return  # No hires fix requested
   255→
   256→        hires_config = {
   257→            "enabled": True,
   258→            "scale": float(hires_scale),
   259→            "hires_steps": int(db_task_params.get("hires_steps", 6)),
   260→            "denoising_strength": float(db_task_params.get("hires_denoise", 0.5)),
   261→            "upscale_method": db_task_params.get("hires_upscale_method", "bicubic"),
   262→        }
   263→        generation_params["hires_config"] = hires_config
   264→
   265→        # Convert LoRA multipliers to phase-based format (pass1;pass2)
   266→        # This allows fine control over which LoRAs are active in each pass
   267→        self._convert_to_phase_multipliers(generation_params)
   268→
   269→        self._log_info(
   270→            f"Hires fix enabled: {hires_config['scale']}x scale, "
   271→            f"{hires_config['hires_steps']} steps @ {hires_config['denoising_strength']} denoise"
   272→        )
   273→
   274→    def _convert_to_phase_multipliers(self, generation_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   275→        """
   276→        Convert simple LoRA multipliers to phase-based format for hires fix.
   277→
   278→        IMPORTANT: This stores the full phase format in hires_config but sends
   279→        only pass 1 values to WGP to avoid conflicts with WGP's phase system.
   280→
   281→        Converts:
   282→          ["1.1", "0.5"] -> ["1.1;1.1", "0.5;0.5"]
   283→
   284→        Where the format is "pass1_strength;pass2_strength".
   285→
   286→        Lightning LoRA phases are manually controlled via:
   287→        - lightning_lora_strength_phase_1 (default: 0.85)
   288→        - lightning_lora_strength_phase_2 (default: 0.0)
   289→
   290→        All other LoRAs -> "X;X" (same strength in both passes unless phase format specified).
   291→        """
   292→        if "lora_multipliers" not in generation_params:
   293→            return
   294→
   295→        lora_names = generation_params.get("lora_names", [])
   296→        multipliers = generation_params["lora_multipliers"]
   297→
   298→        # Ensure multipliers is a list
   299→        if not isinstance(multipliers, list):
   300→            multipliers = [multipliers]
   301→
   302→        # Convert to phase-based format
   303→        # Note: auto_detect_lightning=False because Lightning LoRA phases are now manually specified
   304→        phase_multipliers = format_phase_multipliers(
   305→            lora_names=lora_names,
   306→            multipliers=multipliers,
   307→            num_phases=2,  # Hires fix = 2 passes
   308→            auto_detect_lightning=False  # Manual control via lightning_lora_strength_phase_1/2
   309→        )
   310→
   311→        # Store full phase format AND LoRA names in hires_config for qwen_main.py to use
   312→        if "hires_config" in generation_params:
   313→            generation_params["hires_config"]["lora_names"] = lora_names
   314→            generation_params["hires_config"]["phase_lora_multipliers"] = phase_multipliers
   315→            self._log_info(f"Stored {len(lora_names)} LoRA names and phase multipliers in hires_config")
   316→            for i, (name, mult) in enumerate(zip(lora_names, phase_multipliers)):
   317→                self._log_info(f"   LoRA {i+1}: {name} @ {mult}")
   318→
   319→        # Extract ONLY pass 1 values to send to WGP (avoids conflict with WGP's phase system)
   320→        pass1_multipliers = extract_phase_values(
   321→            phase_multipliers,
   322→            phase_index=0,  # Pass 1
   323→            num_phases=2
   324→        )
   325→
   326→        generation_params["lora_multipliers"] = pass1_multipliers
   327→        self._log_info(f"Sending pass 1 multipliers to WGP: {pass1_multipliers}")
   328→
   329→    # ── Task handlers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
   330→
   331→    def handle_qwen_image_edit(self, db_task_params: Dict[str, Any], generation_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   332→        """Handle qwen_image_edit task type."""
   333→        self._log_info("Processing qwen_image_edit task")
   334→
   335→        image_url = db_task_params.get("image") or db_task_params.get("image_url")
   336→        if not image_url:
   337→            raise ValueError(f"Task {self.task_id}: 'image' or 'image_url' required for qwen_image_edit")
   338→
   339→        downloads_dir = Path("outputs/qwen_edit_images")
   340→        downloads_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   341→        local_image_path = download_image_if_url(
   342→            image_url, downloads_dir,
   343→            task_id_for_logging=self.task_id,
   344→            debug_mode=False,
   345→            descriptive_name="edit_image"
   346→        )
   347→        generation_params["image_guide"] = str(local_image_path)
   348→        self._log_info(f"Using image_guide: {local_image_path}")
   349→
   350→        if "resolution" in db_task_params:
   351→            capped_res = self.cap_qwen_resolution(db_task_params["resolution"])
   352→            if capped_res:
   353→                generation_params["resolution"] = capped_res
   354→
   355→        generation_params.setdefault("video_prompt_type", "KI")
   356→        generation_params.setdefault("guidance_scale", 1)
   357→        generation_params.setdefault("num_inference_steps", 12)
   358→        generation_params.setdefault("video_length", 1)
   359→
   360→        apply_system_prompt(db_task_params, generation_params, SYSTEM_PROMPT_IMAGE_EDIT)
   361→
   362→        edit_config = self._get_edit_model_config(db_task_params)
   363→        self._add_lightning_lora(
   364→            db_task_params, generation_params,
   365→            lightning_fname=edit_config["lightning_fname"],
   366→            lightning_repo=edit_config["lightning_repo"],
   367→            hf_subfolder=edit_config["hf_subfolder"],
   368→            default_phase1=0.85, default_phase2=0.0,
   369→        )
   370→
   371→        # Optional hires fix - can be enabled on any qwen_image_edit task
   372→        self._maybe_add_hires_config(db_task_params, generation_params)
   373→
   374→    def _handle_qwen_image_task(
   375→        self,
   376→        db_task_params: Dict[str, Any],
   377→        generation_params: Dict[str, Any],
   378→        task_label: str,
   379→        composite_subdir: str,
   380→        default_system_prompt: str,
   381→        task_lora_repo: str,
   382→        task_lora_fname: str,
   383→    ):
   384→        """Shared implementation for mask-based Qwen image tasks (inpaint, annotated edit)."""
   385→        self._log_info(f"Processing {task_label} task")
   386→
   387→        image_url = db_task_params.get("image_url") or db_task_params.get("image")
   388→        mask_url = db_task_params.get("mask_url")
   389→
   390→        if not image_url or not mask_url:
   391→            raise ValueError(f"Task {self.task_id}: 'image_url' and 'mask_url' required for {task_label}")
   392→
   393→        composite_dir = Path(f"outputs/{composite_subdir}")
   394→        composite_path = self.create_qwen_masked_composite(image_url, mask_url, composite_dir)
   395→
   396→        generation_params["image_guide"] = str(composite_path)
   397→
   398→        if "resolution" in db_task_params:
   399→            capped_res = self.cap_qwen_resolution(db_task_params["resolution"])
   400→            if capped_res:
   401→                generation_params["resolution"] = capped_res
   402→
   403→        generation_params.setdefault("video_prompt_type", "KI")
   404→        generation_params.setdefault("guidance_scale", 1)
   405→        generation_params.setdefault("num_inference_steps", 12)
   406→        generation_params.setdefault("video_length", 1)
   407→
   408→        apply_system_prompt(db_task_params, generation_params, default_system_prompt)
   409→
   410→        edit_config = self._get_edit_model_config(db_task_params)
   411→        self._add_lightning_lora(
   412→            db_task_params, generation_params,
   413→            lightning_fname=edit_config["lightning_fname"],
   414→            lightning_repo=edit_config["lightning_repo"],
   415→            hf_subfolder=edit_config["hf_subfolder"],
   416→            default_phase1=0.75, default_phase2=0.0,
   417→        )
   418→        self._add_task_lora(generation_params, task_lora_repo, task_lora_fname)
   419→
   420→        # Optional hires fix
   421→        self._maybe_add_hires_config(db_task_params, generation_params)
   422→

   434→    def handle_annotated_image_edit(self, db_task_params: Dict[str, Any], generation_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   435→        """Handle annotated_image_edit task type."""

   438→            task_label="annotated_image_edit",
   439→            composite_subdir="qwen_annotate_composites",

   445→    def handle_qwen_image_style(self, db_task_params: Dict[str, Any], generation_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   446→        """Handle qwen_image_style task type."""
   447→        self._log_info("Processing qwen_image_style task")
   448→
   449→        original_prompt = generation_params.get("prompt", db_task_params.get("prompt", ""))
   450→
   451→        style_strength = float(db_task_params.get("style_reference_strength", 0.0) or 0.0)
   452→        subject_strength = float(db_task_params.get("subject_strength", 0.0) or 0.0)
   453→        scene_strength = float(db_task_params.get("scene_reference_strength", 0.0) or 0.0)
   454→        subject_description = db_task_params.get("subject_description", "")
   455→        in_this_scene = db_task_params.get("in_this_scene", False)
   456→
   457→        # Build modified prompt using extracted helper
   458→        modified_prompt = build_style_prompt(
   459→            original_prompt, style_strength, subject_strength,
   460→            subject_description, in_this_scene,
   461→        )
   462→        if modified_prompt != original_prompt:
   463→            generation_params["prompt"] = modified_prompt
   464→            self._log_info(f"Modified prompt to: {modified_prompt}")
   465→
   466→        reference_image = db_task_params.get("style_reference_image") or db_task_params.get("subject_reference_image")
   467→        if reference_image:
   468→            try:
   469→                downloads_dir = Path("outputs/style_refs")
   470→                downloads_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   471→                local_ref_path = download_image_if_url(
   472→                    reference_image, downloads_dir,
   473→                    task_id_for_logging=self.task_id,
   474→                    debug_mode=False,
   475→                    descriptive_name="reference_image"
   476→                )
   477→                generation_params["image_guide"] = str(local_ref_path)
   478→            except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   479→                self._log_warning(f"Failed to download reference image: {e}")
   480→
   481→        if "resolution" in db_task_params:
   482→            capped_res = self.cap_qwen_resolution(db_task_params["resolution"])
   483→            if capped_res:
   484→                generation_params["resolution"] = capped_res
   485→
   486→        generation_params.setdefault("video_prompt_type", "KI")
   487→        generation_params.setdefault("guidance_scale", 1)
   488→        generation_params.setdefault("num_inference_steps", 12)
   489→        generation_params.setdefault("video_length", 1)
   490→
   491→        # Set system prompt based on parameters
   492→        custom_prompt = db_task_params.get("system_prompt")
   493→        if custom_prompt:
   494→            generation_params["system_prompt"] = custom_prompt
   495→        else:
   496→            generation_params["system_prompt"] = select_style_system_prompt(
   497→                has_subject=subject_strength > 0,
   498→                has_style=style_strength > 0,
   499→                has_scene=scene_strength > 0,
   500→            )
   501→
   502→        # Lightning LoRA (variant-aware)
   503→        edit_config = self._get_edit_model_config(db_task_params)
   504→        self._add_lightning_lora(
   505→            db_task_params, generation_params,
   506→            lightning_fname=edit_config["lightning_fname"],
   507→            lightning_repo=edit_config["lightning_repo"],
   508→            hf_subfolder=edit_config["hf_subfolder"],
   509→            default_phase1=0.85, default_phase2=0.0,
   510→        )
   511→
   512→        # Conditional style/subject/scene LoRAs
   513→        style_fname = "style_transfer_qwen_edit_2_000011250.safetensors"
   514→        subject_fname = "in_subject_qwen_edit_2_000006750.safetensors"
   515→        scene_fname = "in_scene_different_object_000010500.safetensors"
   516→
   517→        if style_strength > 0.0:
   518→            self._add_task_lora(generation_params, "peteromallet/ad_motion_loras", style_fname, style_strength)
   519→        if subject_strength > 0.0:
   520→            self._add_task_lora(generation_params, "peteromallet/mystery_models", subject_fname, subject_strength)
   521→        if scene_strength > 0.0:
   522→            self._add_task_lora(generation_params, "peteromallet/random_junk", scene_fname, scene_strength)
   523→
   524→        # Optional hires fix
   525→        self._maybe_add_hires_config(db_task_params, generation_params)
   526→
   527→    def handle_qwen_image_hires(self, db_task_params: Dict[str, Any], generation_params: Dict[str, Any]):
   528→        """
   529→        Handle qwen_image_hires task type - two-pass generation with latent upscaling.
   530→
   531→        Workflow:
   532→        - Pass 1: Generate at base resolution (default 1328x1328)
   533→        - Latent upscale: Bicubic interpolation in latent space
   534→        - Pass 2: Refine at higher resolution with partial denoising
   535→
   536→        This replicates the ComfyUI two-pass hires fix workflow.
   537→        """
   538→        self._log_info("Processing qwen_image_hires task (two-pass generation)")
   539→
   540→        # Default base resolutions matching ComfyUI workflow
   541→        BASE_RESOLUTIONS = {
   542→            "1:1": (1328, 1328),
   543→            "16:9": (1664, 928),
   544→            "9:16": (928, 1664),
   545→            "4:3": (1472, 1104),
   546→            "3:4": (1104, 1472),
   547→            "3:2": (1584, 1056),
   548→            "2:3": (1056, 1584),
   549→        }
   550→
   551→        # Determine base resolution
   552→        resolution_input = db_task_params.get("resolution", "1328x1328")
   553→        if resolution_input in BASE_RESOLUTIONS:
   554→            # Aspect ratio key provided
   555→            w, h = BASE_RESOLUTIONS[resolution_input]
   556→            resolution_str = f"{w}x{h}"
   557→        else:
   558→            resolution_str = resolution_input
   559→
   560→        generation_params["resolution"] = resolution_str
   561→
   562→        # Build hires config
   563→        hires_config = {
   564→            "enabled": True,
   565→            "scale": float(db_task_params.get("hires_scale", 2.0)),
   566→            "hires_steps": int(db_task_params.get("hires_steps", 6)),
   567→            "denoising_strength": float(db_task_params.get("hires_denoise", 0.5)),
   568→            "upscale_method": db_task_params.get("hires_upscale_method", "bicubic"),
   569→        }
   570→        generation_params["hires_config"] = hires_config
   571→
   572→        # Base generation params
   573→        generation_params.setdefault("video_prompt_type", "KI")
   574→        generation_params.setdefault("guidance_scale", 1)
   575→        generation_params.setdefault("num_inference_steps", int(db_task_params.get("num_inference_steps", 10)))
   576→        generation_params.setdefault("video_length", 1)
   577→
   578→        # System prompt
   579→        apply_system_prompt(db_task_params, generation_params, SYSTEM_PROMPT_IMAGE_HIRES)
   580→
   581→        self._add_lightning_lora_simple(db_task_params, generation_params, default_strength=0.45)
   582→
   583→        # Log final config
   584→        base_w, base_h = map(int, resolution_str.split("x"))
   585→        final_w = int(base_w * hires_config["scale"])
   586→        final_h = int(base_h * hires_config["scale"])
   587→        self._log_info(

   681→        - Cost-sensitive workflows
   682→

   684→        """
   685→        self._log_info("Processing z_image_turbo task (fast text-to-image)")
   686→
   687→        # Resolution handling
   688→        resolution = db_task_params.get("resolution", "512x512")  # Default smaller for speed
   689→        capped_res = self.cap_qwen_resolution(resolution)
   690→        if capped_res:
   691→            generation_params["resolution"] = capped_res
   692→
   693→        # Base generation params - optimized for SPEED
   694→        generation_params.setdefault("video_prompt_type", "")  # No input image
   695→        generation_params.setdefault("guidance_scale", 2.5)  # Lower for faster convergence
   696→        generation_params.setdefault("num_inference_steps", int(db_task_params.get("num_inference_steps", 8)))  # Fast!
   697→        generation_params.setdefault("video_length", 1)
   698→
   699→        # System prompt
   700→        apply_system_prompt(db_task_params, generation_params, SYSTEM_PROMPT_TURBO)

   703→
   704→        # Handle additional LoRAs

   706→
   707→        # Note: hires fix generally not recommended for turbo mode (defeats the speed purpose)
   708→        # but still allow it if explicitly requested
   709→        if db_task_params.get("hires_scale"):
   710→            self._maybe_add_hires_config(db_task_params, generation_params)
   711→            self._log_warning("Hires fix enabled on turbo mode - this will significantly increase generation time")
```
