1→"""
     2→LoRA configuration handling with typed entries.
     3→
     4→Uses LoRAEntry objects instead of parallel arrays to preserve ordering
     5→and ensure filename/multiplier pairs stay in sync.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     9→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Union
    10→from enum import Enum
    11→import os
    12→import logging
    13→
    14→from .base import ParamGroup
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→
    19→class LoRAStatus(Enum):
    20→    """Status of a LoRA entry."""
    21→    PENDING = "pending"      # URL needs to be downloaded
    22→    DOWNLOADED = "downloaded"  # Downloaded, has local_path
    23→    LOCAL = "local"          # Local file reference
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass
    27→class LoRAEntry:
    28→    """
    29→    Single LoRA entry with all its metadata.
    30→    
    31→    Using objects instead of parallel arrays ensures:
    32→    - Filename and multiplier stay paired
    33→    - Order is preserved during operations
    34→    - Clear status tracking
    35→    """
    36→    url: Optional[str] = None
    37→    local_path: Optional[str] = None
    38→    filename: Optional[str] = None
    39→    multiplier: Union[float, str] = 1.0  # str for phase-config format like "1.0;0.5"
    40→    status: LoRAStatus = LoRAStatus.LOCAL
    41→    source: str = ""  # Where this entry came from (for debugging)
    42→    
    43→    def mark_downloaded(self, local_path: str):
    44→        """Mark this entry as downloaded with the given local path."""
    45→        self.local_path = local_path
    46→        self.filename = os.path.basename(local_path)
    47→        self.status = LoRAStatus.DOWNLOADED
    48→    
    49→    def get_effective_path(self) -> Optional[str]:
    50→        """Get the path to use for this LoRA (local_path or filename)."""
    51→        return self.local_path or self.filename
    52→    
    53→    def is_phase_config_multiplier(self) -> bool:
    54→        """Check if multiplier is phase-config format (contains semicolons)."""
    55→        return isinstance(self.multiplier, str) and ';' in str(self.multiplier)
    56→
    57→
    58→@dataclass  
    59→class LoRAConfig(ParamGroup):
    60→    """
    61→    Typed LoRA configuration.
    62→    
    63→    Handles all LoRA naming conventions:
    64→    - WGP: activated_loras, loras_multipliers (string)
    65→    - Internal: lora_names, lora_multipliers (list)
    66→    - Phase config: additional_loras dict with URL keys
    67→    """
    68→    entries: List[LoRAEntry] = field(default_factory=list)
    69→    
    70→    @classmethod
    71→    def from_params(cls, params: Dict[str, Any], **context) -> 'LoRAConfig':
    72→        """
    73→        Parse LoRA config from various input formats.
    74→        
    75→        Handles:
    76→        - activated_loras / lora_names (list of filenames)
    77→        - loras_multipliers / lora_multipliers (string or list)
    78→        - additional_loras (dict of URL -> multiplier for downloads)
    79→        """
    80→        entries = []
    81→        
    82→        # Get filenames from either naming convention
    83→        filenames = (
    84→            params.get('activated_loras') or 
    85→            params.get('lora_names') or 
    86→            []
    87→        )
    88→        if isinstance(filenames, str):
    89→            filenames = [f.strip() for f in filenames.split(',') if f.strip()]
    90→        
    91→        # Get multipliers from either naming convention
    92→        multipliers_raw = (
    93→            params.get('loras_multipliers') or 
    94→            params.get('lora_multipliers') or
    95→            []
    96→        )
    97→        
    98→        # Parse multipliers
    99→        if isinstance(multipliers_raw, str):
   100→            # Could be space-separated or comma-separated
   101→            if ';' in multipliers_raw:
   102→                # Phase-config format: space-separated strings with semicolons
   103→                multipliers = multipliers_raw.split()
   104→            elif ',' in multipliers_raw:
   105→                multipliers = [m.strip() for m in multipliers_raw.split(',')]
   106→            else:
   107→                multipliers = multipliers_raw.split()
   108→        elif isinstance(multipliers_raw, list):
   109→            multipliers = multipliers_raw
   110→        else:
   111→            multipliers = []
   112→        
   113→        # Create entries from filenames (could be URLs, absolute paths, or filenames)
   114→        for i, filename in enumerate(filenames):
   115→            mult = multipliers[i] if i < len(multipliers) else 1.0
   116→            # Try to convert to float if it's a simple number
   117→            if isinstance(mult, str) and ';' not in mult:
   118→                try:
   119→                    mult = float(mult)
   120→                except ValueError:
   121→                    pass
   122→            
   123→            # Detect if this is a URL that needs downloading
   124→            is_url = filename.startswith(('http://', 'https://'))
   125→            
   126→            if is_url:
   127→                # URL needs downloading
   128→                entries.append(LoRAEntry(
   129→                    url=filename,
   130→                    filename=os.path.basename(filename),
   131→                    multiplier=mult,
   132→                    status=LoRAStatus.PENDING,
   133→                    source='params_url'
   134→                ))
   135→            else:
   136→                # Local file or filename
   137→                entries.append(LoRAEntry(
   138→                    filename=filename,
   139→                    local_path=filename if os.path.isabs(filename) else None,
   140→                    multiplier=mult,
   141→                    status=LoRAStatus.LOCAL,
   142→                    source='params'
   143→                ))
   144→        
   145→        # Handle additional_loras (URLs needing download)
   146→        additional_loras = params.get('additional_loras', {})
   147→        if isinstance(additional_loras, dict):
   148→            for url, mult in additional_loras.items():
   149→                # Check if we already have this URL's file
   150→                filename = os.path.basename(url) if url else None
   151→                existing = next((e for e in entries if e.filename == filename), None)
   152→                
   153→                if existing:
   154→                    # Update existing entry with URL info
   155→                    existing.url = url
   156→                    if mult and ';' in str(mult):
   157→                        # Phase-config multiplier takes precedence
   158→                        existing.multiplier = mult
   159→                    existing.status = LoRAStatus.PENDING
   160→                else:
   161→                    # New entry for download
   162→                    entries.append(LoRAEntry(
   163→                        url=url,
   164→                        filename=filename,
   165→                        multiplier=mult if mult else 1.0,
   166→                        status=LoRAStatus.PENDING,
   167→                        source='additional_loras'
   168→                    ))
   169→        
   170→        return cls(entries=entries)
   171→    
   172→    @classmethod
   173→    def from_phase_config(cls, phase_config: Dict[str, Any], **context) -> 'LoRAConfig':
   174→        """
   175→        Parse LoRA config from phase_config structure.
   176→
   177→        Phase config has LoRAs per phase with per-phase multipliers.
   178→        We combine into single entries with semicolon-separated multipliers.
   179→        """
   180→        entries = []
   181→        phases = phase_config.get('phases', [])
   182→
   183→        # Collect all unique LoRAs with their per-phase multipliers
   184→        lora_phases: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}  # url -> [mult_phase1, mult_phase2, ...]
   185→
   186→        for phase_idx, phase in enumerate(phases):
   187→            phase_loras = phase.get('loras', [])
   188→            for lora in phase_loras:
   189→                url = lora.get('url', '')
   190→                mult = lora.get('multiplier', 1.0)
   191→
   192→                if url not in lora_phases:
   193→                    lora_phases[url] = [0.0] * len(phases)
   194→                lora_phases[url][phase_idx] = mult
   195→
   196→        # Create entries with combined multipliers
   197→        for url, mults in lora_phases.items():
   198→            mult_str = ';'.join(str(m) for m in mults)
   199→            entries.append(LoRAEntry(
   200→                url=url,
   201→                filename=os.path.basename(url) if url else None,
   202→                multiplier=mult_str,
   203→                status=LoRAStatus.PENDING,
   204→                source='phase_config'
   205→            ))
   206→
   207→        return cls(entries=entries)
   208→
   209→    @classmethod
   210→    def from_segment_loras(cls, segment_loras: List[Dict[str, Any]], **context) -> 'LoRAConfig':
   211→        """
   212→        Parse LoRA config from per-segment LoRA override format.
   213→
   214→        Args:
   215→            segment_loras: List of LoRA dicts from frontend:
   216→                [{"id": "...", "path": "...", "strength": 0.8, "name": "..."}, ...]
   217→
   218→        Returns:
   219→            LoRAConfig with entries for each LoRA
   220→        """
   221→        entries = []
   222→
   223→        for lora_dict in segment_loras:
   224→            # Frontend sends: {id, path, strength, name?}
   225→            # path could be a URL or a local path/filename
   226→            lora_path = lora_dict.get('path', '')
   227→            strength = lora_dict.get('strength', 1.0)
   228→            if not lora_path:
   229→                continue
   230→
   231→            # Detect if this is a URL that needs downloading
   232→            is_url = lora_path.startswith(('http://', 'https://'))
   233→
   234→            if is_url:
   235→                # URL needs downloading
   236→                entries.append(LoRAEntry(
   237→                    url=lora_path,
   238→                    filename=os.path.basename(lora_path),
   239→                    multiplier=strength,
   240→                    status=LoRAStatus.PENDING,
   241→                    source='segment_loras'
   242→                ))
   243→            else:
   244→                # Local file or filename
   245→                entries.append(LoRAEntry(
   246→                    filename=lora_path,
   247→                    local_path=lora_path if os.path.isabs(lora_path) else None,
   248→                    multiplier=strength,
   249→                    status=LoRAStatus.LOCAL,
   250→                    source='segment_loras'
   251→                ))
   252→
   253→        return cls(entries=entries)
   254→    
   255→    def to_wgp_format(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   256→        """
   257→        Convert to WGP-compatible format.
   258→        
   259→        Returns:
   260→            activated_loras: list of filenames/paths
   261→            loras_multipliers: space-separated string (for phase-config) or comma-separated
   262→        """
   263→        if not self.entries:
   264→            return {'activated_loras': [], 'loras_multipliers': ''}
   265→        
   266→        # Only include downloaded/local entries
   267→        ready_entries = [e for e in self.entries if e.status != LoRAStatus.PENDING]
   268→        
   269→        activated_loras = []
   270→        multipliers = []
   271→        
   272→        for entry in ready_entries:
   273→            path = entry.get_effective_path()
   274→            if path:
   275→                activated_loras.append(path)
   276→                multipliers.append(str(entry.multiplier))
   277→        
   278→        # Format multipliers as space-separated string
   279→        # WGP's preparse_loras_multipliers() splits on spaces, so both
   280→        # phase-config ("0.9;0.5 1.1;1.1") and regular ("0.9 1.1 0.5")
   281→        # must be space-separated
   282→        loras_multipliers = ' '.join(multipliers)
   283→        
   284→        return {
   285→            'activated_loras': activated_loras,
   286→            'loras_multipliers': loras_multipliers
   287→        }
   288→    
   289→    def has_pending_downloads(self) -> bool:
   290→        """Check if any entries need downloading."""
   291→        return any(e.status == LoRAStatus.PENDING and e.url for e in self.entries)
   292→    
   293→    def get_pending_downloads(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   294→        """Get dict of URL -> multiplier for pending downloads."""
   295→        return {
   296→            e.url: e.multiplier 
   297→            for e in self.entries 
   298→            if e.status == LoRAStatus.PENDING and e.url
   299→        }
   300→    
   301→    def mark_downloaded(self, url: str, local_path: str):
   302→        """Mark a URL as downloaded with the given local path."""
   303→        for entry in self.entries:
   304→            if entry.url == url:
   305→                entry.mark_downloaded(local_path)
   306→                return
   307→    
   308→    def merge(self, other: 'LoRAConfig') -> 'LoRAConfig':
   309→        """
   310→        Merge another LoRAConfig, with other taking precedence for duplicates.
   311→        Phase-config multipliers take precedence over regular ones.
   312→        """
   313→        merged_entries = list(self.entries)
   314→        
   315→        for other_entry in other.entries:
   316→            # Find existing entry by filename
   317→            existing_idx = next(
   318→                (i for i, e in enumerate(merged_entries) 
   319→                 if e.filename == other_entry.filename),
   320→                None
   321→            )
   322→            
   323→            if existing_idx is not None:
   324→                existing = merged_entries[existing_idx]
   325→                # Phase-config multipliers take precedence
   326→                if other_entry.is_phase_config_multiplier():
   327→                    merged_entries[existing_idx] = other_entry
   328→                elif not existing.is_phase_config_multiplier():
   329→                    # Other takes precedence for non-phase-config
   330→                    merged_entries[existing_idx] = other_entry
   331→            else:
   332→                merged_entries.append(other_entry)
   333→        
   334→        return LoRAConfig(entries=merged_entries)
   335→    
   336→    def validate(self) -> List[str]:
   337→        """Validate the LoRA configuration."""
   338→        errors = []
   339→        
   340→        for entry in self.entries:
   341→            if entry.status == LoRAStatus.LOCAL and entry.filename:
   342→                # Could add file existence check here if needed
   343→                pass
   344→            if entry.status == LoRAStatus.PENDING and not entry.url:
   345→                errors.append(f"Pending LoRA entry has no URL: {entry.filename}")
   346→        
   347→        return errors
   348→

1→"""Resolution parsing and snapping utilities."""
     2→
     3→__all__ = [
     4→    "snap_resolution_to_model_grid",
     5→    "parse_resolution",
     6→]
     7→
     8→
     9→def snap_resolution_to_model_grid(parsed_res: tuple[int, int]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    10→    """
    11→    Snaps resolution to model grid requirements (multiples of 16).
    12→
    13→    Args:
    14→        parsed_res: (width, height) tuple
    15→
    16→    Returns:
    17→        (width, height) tuple snapped to nearest valid values
    18→    """
    19→    width, height = parsed_res
    20→    # Ensure resolution is compatible with model requirements (multiples of 16)
    21→    width = (width // 16) * 16
    22→    height = (height // 16) * 16
    23→    return width, height
    24→
    25→
    26→def parse_resolution(res_str: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    27→    """Parses 'WIDTHxHEIGHT' string to (width, height) tuple."""
    28→    try:
    29→        w, h = map(int, res_str.split('x'))
    30→        if w <= 0 or h <= 0:
    31→            raise ValueError("Width and height must be positive.")
    32→        return w, h
    33→    except ValueError as e:
    34→        raise ValueError(f"Resolution string must be in WIDTHxHEIGHT format with positive integers (e.g., '960x544'), got {res_str}. Error: {e}")
    35→

    57→def _get_param(key, *sources, default=_MISSING, prefer_truthy: bool = False):
    58→    """
    59→    Get a parameter from multiple sources with precedence (first source wins).
    60→
    61→    By default, only skips None values.
    62→    If prefer_truthy=True, also skips falsy non-bool values (e.g. "", {}, [], 0),
    63→    matching historical `a or b` fallback semantics while still preserving explicit booleans.
    64→    """
    65→    for source in sources:
    66→        if not source or key not in source:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        value = source[key]
    70→        if value is None:
    71→            continue
    72→
    73→        if prefer_truthy and not isinstance(value, bool) and not value:
    74→            continue
    75→
    76→        return value
    77→
    78→    return None if default is _MISSING else default

   315→                                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: WARNING: Extracted {prefix_frames} frames, expected {frames_needed}")
   316→                        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_trim:
   317→                            # Fallback: use full video if extraction failed
   318→                            svi_predecessor_video_for_source = predecessor_video_path
   319→                            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: WARNING - Failed to extract last frames ({e_trim}), using full predecessor video")
   320→                    else:
   321→                        # Fallback: use full video if we can't get frame count
   322→                        svi_predecessor_video_for_source = predecessor_video_path
   323→                        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Could not get predecessor frame count, using full video")
   324→                    
   325→                    # Still extract last frame for image_refs (anchor reference)
   326→                    start_ref_path = extract_last_frame_as_image(
   327→                        predecessor_video_path, 
   328→                        segment_processing_dir, 
   329→                        task_id
   330→                    )
   331→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Extracted last frame as anchor start_ref: {start_ref_path}")
   332→                else:
   333→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: ERROR - Predecessor video not available at {predecessor_video_path}")
   334→            
   335→            # For SVI, end_ref is the target image from input array
   336→            target_end_idx = segment_idx + 1 if segment_idx > 0 else 1
   337→            if svi_predecessor_video_url and segment_idx == 0:
   338→                target_end_idx = 1 if len(orchestrator_images) > 1 else 0
   339→            
   340→            if len(orchestrator_images) > target_end_idx:
   341→                end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[target_end_idx]
   342→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: end_ref from input_images[{target_end_idx}]: {end_ref_path}")
   343→            elif len(orchestrator_images) > 0:
   344→                end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[-1]
   345→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: end_ref fallback to last input image: {end_ref_path}")
   346→        
   347→        # =============================================================================
   348→        # SVI MODE: First segment uses input images normally (no manual predecessor)
   349→        # =============================================================================
   350→        elif use_svi and segment_idx == 0:
   351→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: First segment in SVI mode - using input images")
   352→            if len(orchestrator_images) > 0:
   353→                start_ref_path = orchestrator_images[0]
   354→            if len(orchestrator_images) > 1:
   355→                end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[1]
   356→        
   357→        # =============================================================================
   358→        # NON-SVI MODES: Original logic
   359→        # =============================================================================
   360→        # Check individual_segment_params first (highest priority for standalone)
   361→        elif individual_params.get("start_image_url") or individual_params.get("end_image_url"):
   362→            start_ref_path = individual_params.get("start_image_url")
   363→            end_ref_path = individual_params.get("end_image_url")
   364→            task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: Using individual_segment_params URLs: start={start_ref_path}")
   365→        elif len(individual_images) >= 2:
   366→            start_ref_path = individual_images[0]
   367→            end_ref_path = individual_images[1]
   368→            task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: Using individual_segment_params array: start={start_ref_path}")
   369→        elif is_standalone and len(top_level_images) >= 2:
   370→            start_ref_path = top_level_images[0]
   371→            end_ref_path = top_level_images[1]
   372→            task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: Using top-level images directly: start={start_ref_path}")
   373→        elif is_continuing:
   374→            if segment_idx == 0:
   375→                continued_video_path = orchestrator_details.get("continue_from_video_resolved_path")
   376→                if continued_video_path and Path(continued_video_path).exists():
   377→                    start_ref_path = extract_last_frame_as_image(continued_video_path, segment_processing_dir, task_id)
   378→                if orchestrator_images:
   379→                    end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[0]
   380→            else:
   381→                if len(orchestrator_images) > segment_idx:
   382→                    start_ref_path = orchestrator_images[segment_idx - 1]
   383→                    end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[segment_idx]
   384→        else:
   385→            task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: Using orchestrator images (from scratch)")
   386→            if len(orchestrator_images) > segment_idx:
   387→                start_ref_path = orchestrator_images[segment_idx]
   388→            
   389→            if len(orchestrator_images) > segment_idx + 1:
   390→                end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[segment_idx + 1]
   391→        task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: start_ref_path after logic: {start_ref_path}")
   392→        
   393→        if start_ref_path:
   394→            start_ref_path = download_image_if_url(start_ref_path, segment_processing_dir, task_id, debug_mode=debug_enabled)
   395→            task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: start_ref_path AFTER DOWNLOAD: {start_ref_path}")
   396→        if end_ref_path:
   397→            end_ref_path = download_image_if_url(end_ref_path, segment_processing_dir, task_id, debug_mode=debug_enabled)
   398→
   399→        guide_video_path = None
   400→        mask_video_path_for_wgp = None
   401→        video_prompt_type_str = None
   402→
   403→        # Always parse unified structure guidance config up-front so later logic can rely on it.
   404→        # This handles both new format (structure_guidance.videos) and legacy params (structure_videos).
   405→        structure_config = StructureGuidanceConfig.from_params({
   406→            **orchestrator_details,
   407→            **segment_params
   408→        })
   409→
   410→        # Check if structure guidance is configured (uses unified config)
   411→        has_structure_guidance = structure_config.has_guidance
   412→
   413→        # Run TravelSegmentProcessor for:
   414→        # 1. VACE mode (always - requires masks and guides)
   415→        # 2. Any mode with structure guidance configured (enables uni3c/flow guidance for i2v, etc.)
   416→        if travel_mode == "vace" or has_structure_guidance:
   417→            if has_structure_guidance and travel_mode != "vace":
   418→                task_logger.debug(f"[STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE] Task {task_id}: Running TravelSegmentProcessor for {travel_mode} mode (structure_guidance configured)")
   419→            try:
   420→                processor_context = TravelSegmentContext(
   421→                    task_id=task_id,
   422→                    segment_idx=segment_idx,
   423→                    model_name=model_name,
   424→                    total_frames_for_segment=total_frames_for_segment,
   425→                    parsed_res_wh=parsed_res_wh,
   426→                    segment_processing_dir=segment_processing_dir,
   427→                    main_output_dir_base=main_output_dir_base,
   428→                    orchestrator_details=orchestrator_details,
   429→                    segment_params=segment_params,
   430→                    mask_active_frames=mask_active_frames,
   431→                    debug_enabled=debug_enabled)
   432→                processor = TravelSegmentProcessor(processor_context)
   433→                segment_outputs = processor.process_segment()
   434→                
   435→                guide_video_path = segment_outputs.get("video_guide")
   436→                mask_video_path_for_wgp = Path(segment_outputs["video_mask"]) if segment_outputs.get("video_mask") else None
   437→                video_prompt_type_str = segment_outputs["video_prompt_type"]
   438→                detected_structure_type = segment_outputs.get("structure_type")
   439→
   440→                # Debug: Log segment_outputs keys and structure_type
   441→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_DEBUG] Task {task_id}: segment_outputs keys: {list(segment_outputs.keys())}")
   442→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_DEBUG] Task {task_id}: detected_structure_type={repr(detected_structure_type)}, guide_video_path={bool(guide_video_path)}")
   443→
   444→                # If config targets uni3c and we have a guide video, update the config's guidance URL
   445→                # This is used later by the UNI3C MODE section
   446→                if structure_config.is_uni3c and guide_video_path:
   447→                    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_AUTO] Task {task_id}: Uni3C mode with guide from processor: {guide_video_path}")
   448→                    # Store the processor's guide video path in the config
   449→                    structure_config._guidance_video_url = guide_video_path
   450→
   451→            except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e_shared_processor:
   452→                traceback.print_exc()
   453→                return False, f"Shared processor failed: {e_shared_processor}"
   454→        
   455→        # Seed: individual_segment_params > top-level > default
   456→        seed_to_use = individual_params.get("seed_to_use") or segment_params.get("seed_to_use", 12345)
   457→        
   458→        generation_params = {
   459→            "model_name": model_name,
   460→            "negative_prompt": negative_prompt_for_wgp,
   461→            "resolution": f"{parsed_res_wh[0]}x{parsed_res_wh[1]}",
   462→            "video_length": total_frames_for_segment,
   463→            "seed": seed_to_use,
   464→        }
   465→        
   466→        # Always pass images if available, regardless of specific travel_mode string (hybrid models need them)
   467→        if start_ref_path: 
   468→            generation_params["image_start"] = str(Path(start_ref_path).resolve())
   469→        if end_ref_path: 
   470→            generation_params["image_end"] = str(Path(end_ref_path).resolve())
   471→            
   472→        task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: generation_params image_start: {generation_params.get('image_start')}")
   473→        task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: generation_params image_end: {generation_params.get('image_end')}")
   474→        
   475→        # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   476→        # Parameter extraction with precedence: individual > segment > orchestrator
   477→        # Using _get_param() helper for consistent fallback behavior
   478→        # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   479→        
   480→        # Travel-only: pass `additional_loras` through on the segment task payload if present,
   481→        # but DO NOT propagate into `generation_params` (so it won't trigger LoRA downloads/application).
   482→        additional_loras = _get_param(
   483→            "additional_loras",
   484→            individual_params,
   485→            segment_params,
   486→            orchestrator_details,
   487→            default={},
   488→            prefer_truthy=True,  # match previous `a or b or c` behavior for empty dicts
   489→        )
   490→        if additional_loras and debug_enabled:
   491→            task_logger.debug(
   492→                f"[TRAVEL_LORA] Task {task_id}: additional_loras present on payload but intentionally ignored for generation ({len(additional_loras)} entries)"
   493→            )
   494→
   495→        # IMPORTANT: Do NOT treat orchestrator/UI `steps` as diffusion `num_inference_steps`.
   496→        # In travel payloads, `steps` often means "timeline steps" (UI concept), whereas
   497→        # diffusion steps must come from `num_inference_steps` (or `phase_config.steps_per_phase`).
   498→        explicit_steps = _get_param("num_inference_steps", segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True)
   499→        if explicit_steps:
   500→            generation_params["num_inference_steps"] = explicit_steps
   501→        
   502→        explicit_guidance = _get_param("guidance_scale", segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True)
   503→        if explicit_guidance: 
   504→            generation_params["guidance_scale"] = explicit_guidance
   505→        
   506→        explicit_flow_shift = _get_param("flow_shift", segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True)
   507→        if explicit_flow_shift:
   508→            generation_params["flow_shift"] = explicit_flow_shift
   509→
   510→        # =============================================================================
   511→        # Per-Segment Parameter Overrides
   512→        # =============================================================================
   513→        # Log what per-segment overrides were received
   514→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: ========== Per-Segment Override Check ==========")
   515→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: individual_params keys: {list(individual_params.keys()) if individual_params else 'None'}")
   516→
   517→        # Check for segment-specific LoRAs first (highest priority)
   518→        # This allows per-segment LoRA overrides to take precedence over phase_config LoRAs
   519→        segment_loras = _get_param("segment_loras", individual_params, default=None)
   520→        segment_lora_config = None
   521→
   522→        if segment_loras:
   523→            task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_LORAS] Task {task_id}: Using segment-specific LoRAs ({len(segment_loras)} LoRAs)")
   524→            # Pass through URLs/filenames without downloading - convert_to_wgp_task_impl() handles resolution
   525→            segment_lora_config = True  # Flag to skip phase_config LoRAs below
   526→            activated = []
   527→            mults = []
   528→            for lora_dict in segment_loras:
   529→                path = lora_dict.get("path", "")
   530→                strength = lora_dict.get("strength", 1.0)
   531→                if path:
   532→                    activated.append(path)
   533→                    mults.append(str(strength))
   534→            if activated:
   535→                generation_params["activated_loras"] = activated
   536→                generation_params["loras_multipliers"] = " ".join(mults)
   537→                headless_logger.info(f"Set {len(activated)} segment-specific LoRAs (download deferred)", task_id=task_id)
   538→        else:
   539→            task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_LORAS] Task {task_id}: No segment-specific LoRAs - will use phase_config/shot-level LoRAs if present")
   540→
   541→        # Determine phase_config source for logging
   542→        phase_config_from_individual = individual_params.get("phase_config") if individual_params else None
   543→        phase_config_from_segment = segment_params.get("phase_config") if segment_params else None
   544→        _phase_config_from_orchestrator = orchestrator_details.get("phase_config") if orchestrator_details else None
   545→
   546→        phase_config_source = _get_param(
   547→            "phase_config", individual_params, segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True
   548→        )
   549→
   550→        # Log which source phase_config came from
   551→        if phase_config_source:
   552→            if phase_config_from_individual:
   553→                source_name = "PER-SEGMENT (individual_params)"
   554→            elif phase_config_from_segment:
   555→                source_name = "segment_params"
   556→            else:
   557→                source_name = "SHOT-LEVEL (orchestrator_details)"
   558→
   559→            preset_name = phase_config_source.get("preset_name", phase_config_source.get("name", "unknown"))
   560→            task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: Using phase_config from {source_name}")
   561→            task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: preset_name={preset_name}")
   562→
   563→        if phase_config_source:
   564→            try:
   565→                steps_per_phase = phase_config_source.get("steps_per_phase", [2, 2, 2])
   566→                phase_config_steps = sum(steps_per_phase)
   567→                task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: steps_per_phase={steps_per_phase} (total={phase_config_steps})")
   568→                
   569→                parsed_phase_config = parse_phase_config(
   570→                    phase_config=phase_config_source,
   571→                    num_inference_steps=phase_config_steps,
   572→                    task_id=task_id,
   573→                    model_name=generation_params.get("model_name"),
   574→                    debug_mode=debug_enabled
   575→                )
   576→                
   577→                generation_params["num_inference_steps"] = phase_config_steps
   578→                
   579→                # Copy non-LoRA params from phase_config
   580→                for key in ["guidance_phases", "switch_threshold", "switch_threshold2",
   581→                           "guidance_scale", "guidance2_scale", "guidance3_scale",
   582→                           "flow_shift", "sample_solver", "model_switch_phase"]:
   583→                    if key in parsed_phase_config and parsed_phase_config[key] is not None:
   584→                        generation_params[key] = parsed_phase_config[key]
   585→
   586→                # Copy LoRA params from phase_config ONLY if no segment-specific LoRAs
   587→                # (segment_loras takes precedence over phase_config LoRAs)
   588→                # Pass through URLs without downloading - convert_to_wgp_task_impl() handles resolution
   589→                if not segment_lora_config:
   590→                    if "lora_names" in parsed_phase_config:
   591→                        generation_params["activated_loras"] = parsed_phase_config["lora_names"]
   592→                    if "lora_multipliers" in parsed_phase_config:
   593→                        generation_params["loras_multipliers"] = " ".join(str(m) for m in parsed_phase_config["lora_multipliers"])
   594→                    lora_count = len(parsed_phase_config.get("lora_names", []))
   595→                    if lora_count:
   596→                        headless_logger.info(f"Set {lora_count} phase_config LoRAs (download deferred)", task_id=task_id)
   597→                else:
   598→                    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: Skipping phase_config LoRAs (segment-specific LoRAs take precedence)")
   599→
   600→                # Pass phase_config patch data through to task_processor.py for proper apply+restore lifecycle
   601→                # (instead of applying directly here where it would never be cleaned up)
   602→                if "_patch_config" in parsed_phase_config:
   603→                    generation_params["_parsed_phase_config"] = parsed_phase_config
   604→                    generation_params["_phase_config_model_name"] = model_name
   605→
   606→            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
   607→                raise ValueError(f"Task {task_id}: Invalid phase_config: {e}")
   608→        else:
   609→            task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: No phase_config found - using explicit params only")
   610→
   611→        # Summary log for per-segment overrides
   612→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: ========== Per-Segment Override Summary ==========")
   613→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: LoRAs applied: {generation_params.get('activated_loras', [])}")
   614→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: LoRA multipliers: {generation_params.get('loras_multipliers', '')}")
   615→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: num_inference_steps: {generation_params.get('num_inference_steps', 'not set')}")
   616→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: guidance_scale: {generation_params.get('guidance_scale', 'not set')}")
   617→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: ================================================")
   618→
   619→        if guide_video_path: generation_params["video_guide"] = str(guide_video_path)
   620→        if mask_video_path_for_wgp: generation_params["video_mask"] = str(mask_video_path_for_wgp.resolve())
   621→        generation_params["video_prompt_type"] = video_prompt_type_str
   622→
   623→        # Diagnostic: Log guidance configuration when uni3c is enabled
   624→        if structure_config.is_uni3c and guide_video_path:
   625→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK] Task {task_id}: Checking guidance configuration...")
   626→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   video_guide path: {guide_video_path}")
   627→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   structure_config.guidance_video_url: {structure_config.guidance_video_url}")
   628→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   image_end (i2v): {generation_params.get('image_end', 'None')}")
   629→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   video_prompt_type: '{video_prompt_type_str}'")
   630→            if "V" in video_prompt_type_str:
   631→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   🔴 WARNING: video_prompt_type has 'V' - VACE will ALSO use video_guide!")
   632→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   This causes double-feeding: both VACE and Uni3C use same guide")
   633→            else:
   634→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   ✅ OK: video_prompt_type='{video_prompt_type_str}' (no 'V')")
   635→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   Uni3C handles motion guidance, VACE only uses mask for frame preservation")
   636→        
   637→        # =============================================================================
   638→        # SVI MODE: Add SVI-specific generation parameters
   639→        # =============================================================================
   640→        if use_svi:
   641→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Configuring WGP payload for SVI mode")
   642→            
   643→            # Enable SVI encoding mode
   644→            generation_params["svi2pro"] = True
   645→            
   646→            # SVI requires video_prompt_type="I" to enable image_refs passthrough
   647→            generation_params["video_prompt_type"] = "I"
   648→            
   649→            # Set image_refs to start image (anchor for SVI encoding)
   650→            if start_ref_path:
   651→                generation_params["image_refs_paths"] = [str(Path(start_ref_path).resolve())]
   652→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set image_refs_paths to start image: {start_ref_path}")
   653→            
   654→            # CRITICAL: Pass predecessor video as video_source for SVI continuation
   655→            # WGP uses prefix_video[:, -(5 + overlap_size):] to create overlapped_latents
   656→            # This provides temporal continuity between segments (uses last ~9 frames)
   657→            # IMPORTANT: Must NOT set image_start when video_source is set, otherwise
   658→            # wgp.py prioritizes image_start and creates only a 1-frame prefix_video!
   659→            if svi_predecessor_video_for_source:
   660→                video_source_path = str(Path(svi_predecessor_video_for_source).resolve())
   661→                generation_params["video_source"] = video_source_path
   662→                
   663→                # GROUND TRUTH LOG: What WGP will receive
   664→                try:
   665→                    wgp_input_frames, wgp_input_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(video_source_path)
   666→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: ========== WGP INPUT ANALYSIS ==========")
   667→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: video_source: {video_source_path}")
   668→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: video_source frames: {wgp_input_frames}")
   669→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: WGP will extract last 9 frames from this for overlapped_latents")
   670→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: WGP will prepend ALL {wgp_input_frames} frames to output")
   671→                except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_wgp_log:
   672→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: Could not analyze video_source: {e_wgp_log}")
   673→                
   674→                # Remove image_start so WGP uses video_source for multi-frame prefix_video
   675→                # The anchor image is provided via image_refs instead
   676→                if "image_start" in generation_params:
   677→                    del generation_params["image_start"]
   678→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Removed image_start (anchor provided via image_refs)")
   679→                # CRITICAL: Set image_prompt_type to include "V" to enable video_source usage
   680→                # SVI2Pro allows "SVL" - we use "SV" for start+video continuation
   681→                generation_params["image_prompt_type"] = "SV"
   682→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set image_prompt_type='SV' for video continuation")
   683→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set video_source for SVI continuation: {svi_predecessor_video_for_source}")
   684→            
   685→            # SVI Pro sliding window overlap = 4 frames (standard for SVI)
   686→            # Note: WGP applies latent alignment formula (x-1)//4*4+1, but we bypass it
   687→            # by patching sliding_window_defaults.overlap_default=4 in headless_model_management.py
   688→            generation_params["sliding_window_overlap"] = 4
   689→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set sliding_window_overlap=4 for SVI")
   690→
   691→            # IMPORTANT (SVI continuation frame accounting):
   692→            # When `video_source` is provided, WGP will:
   693→            #   - generate `video_length` frames total
   694→            #   - discard the first `sliding_window_overlap` frames of the generated sample
   695→            #   - prepend the prefix video frames (pixels) to the output
   696→            #
   697→            # Therefore the number of **new** frames produced by diffusion is:
   698→            #   new_frames = video_length - sliding_window_overlap
   699→            #
   700→            # In our travel pipeline, `segment_frames_target` / `segment_frames_expanded[idx]` represent
   701→            # the desired number of **new** frames for the segment (4N+1). To actually get that many
   702→            # new frames while still maintaining overlap for stitching, we must request:
   703→            #   video_length = desired_new_frames + sliding_window_overlap
   704→            if svi_predecessor_video_for_source:
   705→                try:
   706→                    desired_new_frames = int(total_frames_for_segment) if total_frames_for_segment else 0
   707→                    overlap_size = int(generation_params.get("sliding_window_overlap") or 4)
   708→                    current_video_length = int(generation_params.get("video_length") or 0)
   709→                    if desired_new_frames > 0 and overlap_size > 0:
   710→                        # Only bump if the payload is still "new-frames based" (avoid double-bumping).
   711→                        if current_video_length == desired_new_frames:
   712→                            generation_params["video_length"] = desired_new_frames + overlap_size
   713→                            task_logger.debug(
   714→                                f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: SVI continuation => bump video_length "
   715→                                f"{current_video_length} -> {generation_params['video_length']} "
   716→                                f"(desired_new_frames={desired_new_frames}, overlap_size={overlap_size})"
   717→                            )
   718→                        else:
   719→                            task_logger.debug(
   720→                                f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: SVI continuation => NOT bumping video_length "
   721→                                f"(current={current_video_length}, desired_new_frames={desired_new_frames}, overlap_size={overlap_size})"
   722→                            )
   723→                except (ValueError, TypeError) as e_len_bump:
   724→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: WARNING: Could not adjust video_length for SVI continuation: {e_len_bump}")
   725→            
   726→            # Add SVI generation parameters from segment_params (set by orchestrator)
   727→            for key in ["guidance_phases", "num_inference_steps", "guidance_scale", "guidance2_scale",
   728→                       "flow_shift", "switch_threshold", "model_switch_phase", "sample_solver"]:
   729→                if key in segment_params and segment_params[key] is not None:
   730→                    generation_params[key] = segment_params[key]
   731→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set {key}={segment_params[key]}")
   732→            
   733→            # Merge SVI LoRAs with existing LoRAs
   734→            from source.task_handlers.travel.svi_config import merge_svi_into_generation_params
   735→
   736→            svi_strength = _get_param("svi_strength", segment_params, orchestrator_details)
   737→            svi_strength_1 = _get_param("svi_strength_1", segment_params, orchestrator_details)
   738→            svi_strength_2 = _get_param("svi_strength_2", segment_params, orchestrator_details)
   739→
   740→            merge_svi_into_generation_params(
   741→                generation_params,
   742→                svi_strength=svi_strength,
   743→                svi_strength_1=svi_strength_1,
   744→                svi_strength_2=svi_strength_2)
   745→
   746→            task_logger.debug(
   747→                f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Merged SVI LoRAs "
   748→                f"(svi_strength={svi_strength}, svi_strength_1={svi_strength_1}, svi_strength_2={svi_strength_2})"
   749→            )
   750→        
   751→        # =============================================================================
   752→        # UNI3C MODE: Motion guidance via Uni3C ControlNet
   753→        # =============================================================================
   754→        # Use unified structure_config exclusively
   755→        use_uni3c = structure_config.is_uni3c
   756→
   757→        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_DEBUG] Task {task_id}: structure_config.is_uni3c={structure_config.is_uni3c}, use_uni3c={use_uni3c}")
   758→
   759→        if use_uni3c:
   760→            from source.utils import download_file as download_file
   761→
   762→            # Get guide video from config (may have been set by processor)
   763→            uni3c_guide = structure_config.guidance_video_url
   764→
   765→            # If Uni3C is enabled but there's no guide video, skip to avoid downstream crashes.
   766→            if not uni3c_guide:
   767→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Uni3C requested but no guide video provided; skipping Uni3C injection")
   768→            else:
   769→                # Use config values (already handles legacy param fallback)
   770→                uni3c_strength = structure_config.strength
   771→                uni3c_start = structure_config.step_window[0]
   772→                uni3c_end = structure_config.step_window[1]
   773→                uni3c_keep_gpu = structure_config.keep_on_gpu
   774→                uni3c_frame_policy = structure_config.frame_policy
   775→                uni3c_zero_empty = structure_config.zero_empty_frames
   776→
   777→                # Download guide video if URL
   778→                if uni3c_guide.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
   779→                    local_filename = Path(uni3c_guide).name or "uni3c_guide_video.mp4"
   780→                    local_download_path = segment_processing_dir / f"uni3c_{local_filename}"
   781→                    if not local_download_path.exists():
   782→                        download_file(uni3c_guide, segment_processing_dir, local_download_path.name)
   783→                        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Downloaded guide video to {local_download_path}")
   784→                    else:
   785→                        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Guide video already exists at {local_download_path}")
   786→                    uni3c_guide = str(local_download_path)
   787→
   788→                # Layer 2 logging
   789→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Uni3C ENABLED (via structure_guidance config)")
   790→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   guide_video={uni3c_guide}")
   791→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   strength={uni3c_strength}")
   792→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   start_percent={uni3c_start}")
   793→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   end_percent={uni3c_end}")
   794→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   frame_policy={uni3c_frame_policy}")
   795→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   keep_on_gpu={uni3c_keep_gpu}")
   796→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   zero_empty_frames={uni3c_zero_empty}")
   797→
   798→                # Inject into generation_params
   799→                generation_params["use_uni3c"] = True
   800→                generation_params["uni3c_guide_video"] = uni3c_guide
   801→                generation_params["uni3c_strength"] = uni3c_strength
   802→                generation_params["uni3c_start_percent"] = uni3c_start
   803→                generation_params["uni3c_end_percent"] = uni3c_end
   804→                generation_params["uni3c_keep_on_gpu"] = uni3c_keep_gpu
   805→                generation_params["uni3c_frame_policy"] = uni3c_frame_policy
   806→                generation_params["uni3c_zero_empty_frames"] = uni3c_zero_empty
   807→
   808→                # For i2v with end anchor: blackout last uni3c frame so i2v handles it alone
   809→                # This prevents uni3c from "overcooking" the final frame
   810→                is_last_segment = segment_params.get("is_last_segment", True)
   811→                has_end_anchor = end_ref_path is not None
   812→                if is_last_segment and has_end_anchor:
   813→                    generation_params["uni3c_blackout_last_frame"] = True
   814→                    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Will blackout last frame (is_last_segment + has end anchor)")
   815→        
   816→        # === WGP SUBMISSION DIAGNOSTIC SUMMARY ===
   817→        # Log key frame-related parameters before WGP submission
   818→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: ========== WGP GENERATION REQUEST ==========")
   819→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: video_length (target frames): {generation_params.get('video_length')}")
   820→        is_valid_4n1 = (generation_params.get('video_length', 0) - 1) % 4 == 0
   821→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: Valid 4N+1: {is_valid_4n1} {'✓' if is_valid_4n1 else '✗ WARNING'}")
   822→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: video_guide: {generation_params.get('video_guide', 'None')}")
   823→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: video_mask: {generation_params.get('video_mask', 'None')}")
   824→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: model: {model_name}")
   825→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: resolution: {generation_params.get('resolution')}")
   826→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: =============================================")
   827→        
   828→        # IMPORTANT: Use the DB task_id as the queue task id.
   829→        # This keeps logs, fatal error handling, and debug tooling consistent (no "travel_seg_" indirection).
   830→        # We still include a hint in parameters so the queue can apply any task-type specific behavior.
   831→        generation_params["_source_task_type"] = "travel_segment"
   832→        generation_task = GenerationTask(
   833→            id=task_id,
   834→            model=model_name,
   835→            prompt=prompt_for_wgp,
   836→            parameters=generation_params
   837→        )
   838→        
   839→        _submitted_task_id = task_queue.submit_task(generation_task)
   840→        
   841→        max_wait_time = 1800
   842→        wait_interval = 2
   843→        elapsed_time = 0
   844→        
   845→        while elapsed_time < max_wait_time:
   846→            status = task_queue.get_task_status(task_id)
   847→            if status is None: return False, f"Travel segment {task_id}: Task status became None"
   848→            
   849→            if status.status == "completed":
   850→                # In standalone mode, skip chaining (no orchestrator to coordinate with)
   851→                if is_standalone:
   852→                    headless_logger.debug(f"Standalone segment completed, skipping chaining", task_id=task_id)
   853→                    return True, status.result_path
   854→                
   855→                # Orchestrator mode: run chaining
   856→                chain_success, chain_message, final_chained_path = _handle_travel_chaining_after_wgp(
   857→                    wgp_task_params={
   858→                        # Provide minimal ground-truth frame params for downstream debug logging / trimming analysis.
   859→                        "use_svi": use_svi,
   860→                        "video_length": generation_params.get("video_length"),
   861→                        "sliding_window_overlap": generation_params.get("sliding_window_overlap"),
   862→                        "video_source": generation_params.get("video_source"),
   863→                        "travel_chain_details": {
   864→                            "orchestrator_task_id_ref": orchestrator_task_id_ref,
   865→                            "orchestrator_run_id": orchestrator_run_id,
   866→                            "segment_index_completed": segment_idx,
   867→                            "orchestrator_details": orchestrator_details,
   868→                            "segment_processing_dir_for_saturation": str(segment_processing_dir),
   869→                            "is_first_new_segment_after_continue": segment_params.get("is_first_segment", False) and orchestrator_details.get("continue_from_video_resolved_path"),
   870→                            "is_subsequent_segment": not segment_params.get("is_first_segment", True),
   871→                            "colour_match_videos": colour_match_videos,
   872→                            "cm_start_ref_path": None, "cm_end_ref_path": None, "show_input_images": False, "start_image_path": None, "end_image_path": None,
   873→                        }
   874→                    },
   875→                    actual_wgp_output_video_path=status.result_path,
   876→                    image_download_dir=segment_processing_dir,
   877→                    main_output_dir_base=main_output_dir_base)
   878→                if chain_success and final_chained_path:
   879→                    return True, final_chained_path
   880→                else:
   881→                    return True, status.result_path
   882→            elif status.status == "failed":
   883→                return False, f"Travel segment {task_id}: Generation failed: {status.error_message}"
   884→            
   885→            time.sleep(wait_interval)
   886→            elapsed_time += wait_interval
   887→        
   888→        return False, f"Travel segment {task_id}: Generation timeout"
   889→
   890→    except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError, KeyError) as e:
   891→        traceback.print_exc()
   892→        return False, f"Travel segment {task_id}: Exception: {str(e)}"
   893→
   894→class TaskRegistry:
   895→    """Registry for task handlers."""
   896→    
   897→    @staticmethod
   898→    def dispatch(task_type: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:
   899→        """
   900→        Dispatch a task to the appropriate handler.
   901→        
   902→        Args:
   903→            task_type: The type of task to execute.
   904→            context: Dictionary containing all necessary context variables:
   905→                - task_params_dict
   906→                - main_output_dir_base
   907→                - task_id
   908→                - project_id
   909→                - task_queue
   910→                - colour_match_videos
   911→                - mask_active_frames
   912→                - debug_mode
   913→                - wan2gp_path
   914→        
   915→        Returns:
   916→            Tuple (success, output_location)
   917→        """
   918→        task_id = context["task_id"]
   919→        params = context["task_params_dict"]
   920→
   921→        # 1. Direct Queue Tasks
   922→        if task_type in DIRECT_QUEUE_TASK_TYPES and context["task_queue"]:
   923→            return TaskRegistry._handle_direct_queue_task(task_type, context)
   924→
   925→        # 2. Orchestrator & Specialized Handlers
   926→        handlers = {
   927→            "travel_orchestrator": lambda: travel_orchestrator.handle_travel_orchestrator_task(
   928→                task_params_from_db=params,
   929→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   930→                orchestrator_task_id_str=task_id,
   931→                orchestrator_project_id=context["project_id"]),
   932→            "travel_segment": lambda: handle_travel_segment_via_queue(
   933→                task_params_dict=params,
   934→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   935→                task_id=task_id,
   936→                colour_match_videos=context["colour_match_videos"],
   937→                mask_active_frames=context["mask_active_frames"],
   938→                task_queue=context["task_queue"],
   939→                is_standalone=False
   940→            ),
   941→            "individual_travel_segment": lambda: handle_travel_segment_via_queue(
   942→                task_params_dict=params,
   943→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   944→                task_id=task_id,
   945→                colour_match_videos=context["colour_match_videos"],
   946→                mask_active_frames=context["mask_active_frames"],
   947→                task_queue=context["task_queue"],
   948→                is_standalone=True
   949→            ),
   950→            "travel_stitch": lambda: _handle_travel_stitch_task(
   951→                task_params_from_db=params,
   952→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   953→                stitch_task_id_str=task_id),
   954→            "magic_edit": lambda: me.handle_magic_edit_task(
   955→                task_params_from_db=params,
   956→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   957→                task_id=task_id),
   958→            "join_clips_orchestrator": lambda: _handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task(
   959→                task_params_from_db=params,
   960→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   961→                orchestrator_task_id_str=task_id,
   962→                orchestrator_project_id=context["project_id"]),
   963→            "edit_video_orchestrator": lambda: _handle_edit_video_orchestrator_task(
   964→                task_params_from_db=params,
   965→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   966→                orchestrator_task_id_str=task_id,
   967→                orchestrator_project_id=context["project_id"]),
   968→            "join_clips_segment": lambda: handle_join_clips_task(
   969→                task_params_from_db=params,
   970→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   971→                task_id=task_id,
   972→                task_queue=context["task_queue"]),
   973→            "join_final_stitch": lambda: handle_join_final_stitch(
   974→                task_params_from_db=params,
   975→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   976→                task_id=task_id),
   977→            "inpaint_frames": lambda: _handle_inpaint_frames_task(
   978→                task_params_from_db=params,
   979→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   980→                task_id=task_id,
   981→                task_queue=context["task_queue"]),
   982→            "create_visualization": lambda: _handle_create_visualization_task(
   983→                task_params_from_db=params,
   984→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   985→                viz_task_id_str=task_id),
   986→            "extract_frame": lambda: handle_extract_frame_task(
   987→                params, context["main_output_dir_base"], task_id
   988→            ),
   989→            "rife_interpolate_images": lambda: handle_rife_interpolate_task(
   990→                params, context["main_output_dir_base"], task_id, task_queue=context["task_queue"]
   991→            ),
   992→            "comfy": lambda: handle_comfy_task(
   993→                task_params_from_db=params,
   994→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],
   995→                task_id=task_id)
   996→        }
   997→
   998→        if task_type in handlers:
   999→            # Orchestrator setup
  1000→            if task_type in ["travel_orchestrator", "join_clips_orchestrator", "edit_video_orchestrator"]:
  1001→                params["task_id"] = task_id
  1002→                if "orchestrator_details" in params:
  1003→                    params["orchestrator_details"]["orchestrator_task_id"] = task_id
  1004→            
  1005→            return handlers[task_type]()
  1006→
  1007→        # Default fallthrough to queue
  1008→        if context["task_queue"]:
  1009→             return TaskRegistry._handle_direct_queue_task(task_type, context)
  1010→        
  1011→        raise ValueError(f"Unknown task type {task_type} and no queue available")
  1012→
  1013→    @staticmethod
  1014→    def _handle_direct_queue_task(task_type: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:
  1015→        task_id = context["task_id"]
  1016→        task_queue = context["task_queue"]
  1017→        params = context["task_params_dict"]
  1018→        
  1019→        try:
  1020→            generation_task = db_task_to_generation_task(
  1021→                params, task_id, task_type, context["wan2gp_path"], context["debug_mode"]
  1022→            )
  1023→            
  1024→            if task_type == "wan_2_2_t2i":
  1025→                generation_task.parameters["video_length"] = 1
  1026→            
  1027→            if context["colour_match_videos"]:
  1028→                generation_task.parameters["colour_match_videos"] = True
  1029→            if context["mask_active_frames"]:
  1030→                generation_task.parameters["mask_active_frames"] = True
  1031→            
  1032→            task_queue.submit_task(generation_task)
  1033→            
  1034→            # Wait for completion
  1035→            max_wait_time = 3600
  1036→            elapsed = 0
  1037→            while elapsed < max_wait_time:
  1038→                status = task_queue.get_task_status(task_id)
  1039→                if not status: return False, "Task status became None"
  1040→                
  1041→                if status.status == "completed":
  1042→                    return True, status.result_path
  1043→                elif status.status == "failed":
  1044→                    return False, status.error_message or "Failed without message"
  1045→                
  1046→                time.sleep(2)
  1047→                elapsed += 2
  1048→            
  1049→            return False, "Timeout"
  1050→            
  1051→        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
  1052→            traceback.print_exc()
  1053→            return False, f"Queue error: {e}"
  1054→
  1055→

1→"""Prompt validation and task ID generation."""
     2→
     3→import uuid
     4→
     5→__all__ = [
     6→    "ensure_valid_prompt",
     7→    "ensure_valid_negative_prompt",
     8→    "generate_unique_task_id",
     9→]
    10→
    11→
    12→def _ensure_valid_text(text: str | None) -> str:
    13→    """Return *text* stripped, or a single space when *text* is None/blank."""
    14→    if not text or not text.strip():
    15→        return " "
    16→    return text.strip()
    17→
    18→
    19→def ensure_valid_prompt(prompt: str | None) -> str:
    20→    """Ensures prompt is valid (not None or empty), returns space as default."""
    21→    return _ensure_valid_text(prompt)
    22→
    23→
    24→def ensure_valid_negative_prompt(negative_prompt: str | None) -> str:
    25→    """Ensures negative prompt is valid (not None or empty), returns space as default."""
    26→    return _ensure_valid_text(negative_prompt)
    27→
    28→
    29→def generate_unique_task_id(prefix: str = "") -> str:
    30→    """Generates a UUID4 string.
    31→
    32→    The optional *prefix* parameter is now ignored so that the returned value
    33→    is a bare RFC-4122 UUID which can be stored in a Postgres `uuid` column
    34→    without casting errors.  The argument is kept in the signature to avoid
    35→    breaking existing call-sites that still pass a prefix.
    36→    """
    37→    return str(uuid.uuid4())
    38→
